+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07c section 6, Gate Y0's first clause (the census agent; new in 07c, after 07b's
+ast_check_07b.py, a record, not edited, whose form it keeps): is the parent's code 07b's (S0'), and with the parent's
+AdaptiveLevin/levin_quadrature.py installed in tree h, is the code that executes 07b's? Read only.
+
+1. S0': every file that differs between 07b's code commit 5e19c4b6 and 07c's code commit's parent cff4237f outside docs/
+   and prompts/ (git diff --name-only), with what it is. Expected: CLAUDE.md alone (not code).
+2. Each .py file of (1), if any, at 5e19c4b6 and at cff4237f (git show) parsed with ast, every docstring removed, and
+   ast.dump of the two compared (07b's method). Expected: none to check.
+3. Every file that differs between 07b's census tree 0a103b5a and h (cc3368ce) outside docs/ and prompts/, with what it
+   is: levin_quadrature.py (replaced by the parent's file in the K0 run at the parent), a test module (never imported by
+   the harness), CLAUDE.md, or one of (2)'s AST-checked modules.
+4. Whether any code in h reads ``__doc__`` (a grep over the tree's .py files outside tests, docs and prompts).
+
+Which of these files the K0 workers actually loaded is u0a_imports_07c.py's (after the K0 run).
+Writes OUT_TXT (provenance header, then the findings). Exits 1 unless S0' lists only the expected files and every .py file
+of (2) is AST-equal.
+
+Usage: python -B ast_check_07c.py OUT_TXT
+"""
+
+import ast
+import hashlib
+import subprocess
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import CODE_SHA_07B, H07B_SHA, H_SHA, H_TREE, PARENT_SHA_07C, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+EXPECTED_S0 = {"CLAUDE.md"}
+
+
+def git(*args):
+    return subprocess.run(["git", "--no-optional-locks", "-C", str(H_TREE), *args], capture_output=True, text=True,
+                          check=True).stdout
+
+
+def strip_docstrings(tree):
+    removed = []
+    for node in ast.walk(tree):
+        if isinstance(node, (ast.Module, ast.ClassDef, ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
+            body = node.body
+            if body and isinstance(body[0], ast.Expr) and isinstance(body[0].value, ast.Constant) and isinstance(
+                    body[0].value.value, str):
+                name = getattr(node, "name", "<module>")
+                removed.append((type(node).__name__, name, body[0].value.value))
+                node.body = body[1:] or [ast.Pass()]
+    return tree, removed
+
+
+def main():
+    out_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
+    lines = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07c: Gate Y0 (1), the parent's code is 07b's (S0', AST check)")
+    ok = True
+    s0 = git("diff", "--name-only", CODE_SHA_07B, PARENT_SHA_07C, "--", ".", ":!docs", ":!prompts").split()
+    lines.append(f"# S0': git diff --name-only {CODE_SHA_07B[:8]} {PARENT_SHA_07C[:8]} -- . ':!docs' ':!prompts': {len(s0)} files")
+    for n in s0:
+        lines.append(f"  {n}: {'expected (CLAUDE.md, not code)' if n in EXPECTED_S0 else 'UNEXPECTED'}")
+    ok &= set(s0) <= EXPECTED_S0
+    lines.append(f"# S0' lists only CLAUDE.md: {set(s0) <= EXPECTED_S0}")
+    lines.append("# the build imports none of S0''s files: " + str(all(not n.endswith('.py') for n in s0)))
+    for m in sorted(n for n in s0 if n.endswith(".py")):
+        src_a = git("show", f"{CODE_SHA_07B}:{m}")
+        src_p = git("show", f"{PARENT_SHA_07C}:{m}")
+        ta, _ra = strip_docstrings(ast.parse(src_a))
+        tp, _rp = strip_docstrings(ast.parse(src_p))
+        eq = ast.dump(ta, include_attributes=False) == ast.dump(tp, include_attributes=False)
+        ok &= eq
+        lines.append(f"# {m}: AST without docstrings equal: {eq}")
+    names = git("diff", "--name-only", H07B_SHA, H_SHA, "--", ".", ":!docs", ":!prompts").split()
+    lines.append(f"# files differing between 07b's census tree {H07B_SHA[:8]} and h {H_SHA[:8]} outside docs/ and prompts/: "
+                 f"{len(names)}")
+    for n in names:
+        role = ("replaced in the K0 run at the parent by the parent's file (variant_loader); h's own at the code"
+                if n == "AdaptiveLevin/levin_quadrature.py"
+                else "a test module (not imported by the harness)" if "/tests/" in n
+                else "not code" if n == "CLAUDE.md"
+                else "docstrings only: AST checked above" if n in EXPECTED_S0 else "UNEXPECTED")
+        ok &= role != "UNEXPECTED"
+        lines.append(f"  {n}: {role}")
+    lq_p = git("show", f"{PARENT_SHA_07C}:AdaptiveLevin/levin_quadrature.py")
+    lq_b = git("show", f"{CODE_SHA_07B}:AdaptiveLevin/levin_quadrature.py")
+    lines.append(f"# AdaptiveLevin/levin_quadrature.py at {PARENT_SHA_07C[:8]} sha256 {hashlib.sha256(lq_p.encode()).hexdigest()}, "
+                 f"at {CODE_SHA_07B[:8]} {hashlib.sha256(lq_b.encode()).hexdigest()}: byte-identical {lq_p == lq_b}")
+    ok &= lq_p == lq_b
+    grep = subprocess.run(["grep", "-rn", "--include=*.py", "__doc__", "."], cwd=str(H_TREE), capture_output=True,
+                          text=True).stdout.splitlines()
+    grep = [g for g in grep if not g.startswith(("./docs/", "./prompts/")) and "/tests/" not in g]
+    lines.append(f"# '__doc__' read in h's .py files outside docs/, prompts/ and tests: {len(grep)} lines")
+    lines += [f"  {g}" for g in grep[:40]]
+    lines.append(f"# verdict: {'HOLDS' if ok else 'FAILS'}")
+    out_p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    out_p.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
+    print("\n".join(lines))
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")
+    return 0 if ok else 1
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())

--- /Users/ds283/Documents/Code/SecondaryGWKit-lpf07c-cc3368c/docs/levin-phase-floor/07b/census/scripts/census_projection_07c.py	1970-01-01 01:00:00

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07c section 6 (and section 9.4): the census's wall-clock projection, from 07b's census and from
+Gate Y0's K0 units at h. Copied from 07b's census_projection_07b.py (a record, not edited), changed only where marked
+"prompt 07c": the "before" is 07b's census (prov.RUN07B, checkpoint 396784bc..., wall 45,016 s, 07b's census log), every
+"07a" below reads "07b", and one figure is added: 07b's own projection ran short by the ratio of its measured wall to its
+central projection (45,016 / 37,615.6 = 1.197; 07c section 9.4 and the dispatch: "state the projection with that
+margin"), so ``projection_central_x_07b_shortfall_s`` is the central projection times that ratio, and
+``projection_highest_x_07b_shortfall_s`` the highest margin's. The limit is 48 hours (07c section 6 and section 9.4).
+07b's text follows.
+
+levin-phase-floor prompt 07b section 6 (and section 9.4): the census's wall-clock projection, from 07a's census and from
+Gate U0's K0 units at h. New in 07b, after 07a's census_projection_07a.py (a record, not edited), whose unit time and
+class ratio it keeps, with a schedule simulation and stated margins added. Read only.
+
+- **A unit's time** t (07a's definition): ``wall_s`` plus every post-timed record's seconds (``scan_s``, ``scan_07_s``,
+  ``w3.seconds``, ``deep_lines.seconds``, ``cc_floor_s``, ``declared_06h_s``, ``checked_06g_s``,
+  ``analytic_06h.seconds``), each missing one counted 0. ``rebuild_s`` (the producers, unchanged by 07b) is kept apart.
+- **The ratio** rho(class) = sum over the class's K0 units of t at h / the same sum in 07a's census. Three classings:
+  (i) 07a's: (rtol, in the 720 or not); (ii) (rtol, k_key), the census's workers; (iii) one ratio for all.
+- **Model 1 (07a's form):** P1 = 07a's census wall x (sum over 07a's 18,560 units of rho x t, plus rebuild) / (sum of t,
+  plus rebuild), with classing (i).
+- **Model 2 (the schedule):** the census runs one worker per (rtol, k), 8 per rtol, 1,160 units each, at most six at
+  once, every 1e-7 worker before any 1e-8 worker, started in run_census.plan's order (all have 1,160 units, so by k_key)
+  as a slot frees. Each worker's duration is simulated as the sum of its units' rho x t plus their rebuild_s plus its
+  overhead in 07a (the worker's span in 07a, from its first unit's start to its last record, less its units' t and
+  rebuild_s). The simulation is checked first on 07a's own times (rho = 1) against 07a's measured wall (23,284 s); the
+  ratio of measured to simulated is applied as a calibration. Run under classings (i), (ii) and (iii).
+- **Margins:** (a) the overhead scaled by rho as well (pessimistic: pickling and JSON grow with regions); (b) rho at the
+  upper end of a bootstrap over the K0 units (2,000 resamples within each class, its 97.5th percentile, seed 7); (c) the
+  over-budget pass: every census unit whose rho x t exceeds 300 s re-runs at up to 3,600 s, added serially at the end
+  (pessimistic), counted from the largest per-unit K0 ratio applied to 07a's unit walls.
+Writes OUT_JSON and prints a summary. Usage: python -B census_projection_07b.py K0_H_DIR K0_PARENT_DIR OUT_JSON
+"""
+
+import datetime
+import json
+import random
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import CHECKPOINT07A_SHA256, H_TREE, RUN07A, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+from prov import CHECKPOINT07B_SHA256, RUN07B  # noqa: E402  prompt 07c
+RUN07A, CHECKPOINT07A_SHA256 = RUN07B, CHECKPOINT07B_SHA256  # prompt 07c: the names kept, 07b's census read
+
+WALL_07A = 23284.0  # 07a's census wall (docs/levin-phase-floor/07a/census/log.md)
+WALL_07A = 45016.0  # prompt 07c: 07b's census wall (docs/levin-phase-floor/07b/census/log.md), the name kept
+SHORTFALL_07B = 45016.0 / 37615.55663062413  # prompt 07c: 07b's measured wall over its central projection
+K0_UNITS = H_TREE / "docs" / "levin-phase-floor" / "06l" / "out" / "k0" / "units.json"
+LIMIT_S = 48 * 3600.0  # 07b section 9.4; prompt 07c: 07c section 9.4, the same
+
+
+def t_unit(d):
+    t = d.get("wall_s") or 0.0
+    for k in ("scan_s", "scan_07_s", "cc_floor_s", "declared_06h_s", "checked_06g_s"):
+        t += d.get(k) or 0.0
+    for k in ("w3", "deep_lines", "analytic_06h"):
+        t += (d.get(k) or {}).get("seconds") or 0.0
+    return t
+
+
+def iso(s):
+    return datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(s).timestamp()
+
+
+def simulate(workers_by_rtol, dur, slots=6):
+    """The census's schedule: per rtol, workers in order, each started as a slot frees; the next rtol after all end."""
+    t0 = 0.0
+    for rt in ("1e-7", "1e-8"):
+        free = [t0] * slots
+        ends = []
+        for w in workers_by_rtol[rt]:
+            i = min(range(slots), key=lambda j: free[j])
+            free[i] = free[i] + dur[w]
+            ends.append(free[i])
+        t0 = max(ends)
+    return t0
+
+
+def main():
+    h_dir, p_dir, out_p = Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2]), Path(sys.argv[3])
+    ck = RUN07A / "checkpoint.jsonl"
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07c: the census's wall-clock projection",  # prompt 07c
+                 [ck, K0_UNITS, h_dir / "units.jsonl", p_dir / "units.jsonl"])
+    if sha256(ck) != CHECKPOINT07A_SHA256:
+        raise SystemExit("07b's checkpoint has another SHA-256")  # prompt 07c
+    k0 = {"h-" + u["uid"].split("-", 1)[1]: u for u in json.loads(K0_UNITS.read_text())["units"]}
+    rows720 = {u["row"] for u in k0.values() if u["in_720"]}
+    U = {}  # 07a's units: uid -> dict
+    with open(ck) as f:
+        for line in f:
+            r = json.loads(line)
+            d = r["data"]
+            U[r["unit"]] = {"t": t_unit(d), "rb": d.get("rebuild_s") or 0.0, "rtol": d["rtol"], "row": d["row"],
+                            "k": d["k_key"], "worker": d["worker"], "wall": d["wall_s"], "rec": iso(r["recorded"])}
+    # 07a's workers: order, span and overhead
+    workers = {}
+    for uid, u in U.items():
+        workers.setdefault(u["worker"], []).append(uid)
+    order = {rt: sorted([w for w in workers if w.startswith(rt + "-")]) for rt in ("1e-7", "1e-8")}
+    over = {}
+    span = {}
+    for w, uids in workers.items():
+        uids.sort(key=lambda x: U[x]["rec"])
+        first = U[uids[0]]
+        start = first["rec"] - first["t"] - first["rb"]
+        span[w] = U[uids[-1]]["rec"] - start
+        over[w] = span[w] - sum(U[x]["t"] + U[x]["rb"] for x in uids)
+    sim07a = simulate(order, {w: span[w] for w in workers})
+    calib = WALL_07A / sim07a
+
+    def load(dirp):
+        return {json.loads(l)["unit"]: json.loads(l)["data"] for l in open(dirp / "units.jsonl")}
+
+    H, P = load(h_dir), load(p_dir)
+    if set(H) != set(k0) or set(P) != set(k0):
+        raise SystemExit("the K0 runs do not hold K0's 100 units")
+    classings = {
+        "i_rtol_720": lambda uid: (U[uid]["rtol"], U[uid]["row"] in rows720),
+        "ii_rtol_k": lambda uid: (U[uid]["rtol"], U[uid]["k"]),
+        "iii_all": lambda uid: "all",
+    }
+    out = {"header": hdr, "wall_07a_s": WALL_07A, "limit_s": LIMIT_S, "n_units_07a": len(U),
+           "sum_t_07a_s": sum(u["t"] for u in U.values()), "sum_rebuild_07a_s": sum(u["rb"] for u in U.values()),
+           "workers_07a": {w: {"span_s": span[w], "overhead_s": over[w], "n": len(workers[w])} for w in sorted(workers)},
+           "schedule_check": {"simulated_07a_s": sim07a, "measured_07a_s": WALL_07A, "calibration": calib}}
+    per_unit = {uid: {"t_07a": U[uid]["t"], "t_h": t_unit(H[uid]), "t_parent": t_unit(P[uid]),
+                      "ratio_h": t_unit(H[uid]) / U[uid]["t"] if U[uid]["t"] > 0 else None,
+                      "wall_h": H[uid]["wall_s"], "wall_07a": U[uid]["wall"], "status_h": H[uid]["status"]}
+                for uid in sorted(k0)}
+    out["k0_units"] = per_unit
+    out["k0_sum_t"] = {"07a": sum(v["t_07a"] for v in per_unit.values()), "h": sum(v["t_h"] for v in per_unit.values()),
+                       "parent": sum(v["t_parent"] for v in per_unit.values())}
+    out["k0_max_wall_h_s"] = max(v["wall_h"] for v in per_unit.values())
+    rs = sorted(v["ratio_h"] for v in per_unit.values() if v["ratio_h"] is not None)
+    out["k0_unit_ratio_h"] = {"min": rs[0], "median": rs[len(rs) // 2], "max": rs[-1]}
+    rng = random.Random(7)
+    res = {}
+    for cname, cf in classings.items():
+        groups = {}
+        for uid in k0:
+            groups.setdefault(cf(uid), []).append(uid)
+        rho = {c: sum(per_unit[u]["t_h"] for u in us) / sum(per_unit[u]["t_07a"] for u in us) for c, us in groups.items()}
+        rho_p = {c: sum(per_unit[u]["t_parent"] for u in us) / sum(per_unit[u]["t_07a"] for u in us) for c, us in groups.items()}
+        boot = {}
+        for c, us in groups.items():
+            vals = []
+            for _ in range(2000):
+                smp = [rng.choice(us) for _ in us]
+                vals.append(sum(per_unit[u]["t_h"] for u in smp) / sum(per_unit[u]["t_07a"] for u in smp))
+            vals.sort()
+            boot[c] = vals[int(0.975 * len(vals))]
+
+        def rho_of(uid, table):
+            c = cf(uid)
+            if c in table:
+                return table[c]
+            return sum(per_unit[u]["t_h"] for u in k0) / sum(per_unit[u]["t_07a"] for u in k0)  # a class K0 lacks
+
+        def project(table, scale_overhead):
+            dur = {}
+            for w, uids in workers.items():
+                body = sum(U[x]["t"] * rho_of(x, table) + U[x]["rb"] for x in uids)
+                rw = (sum(U[x]["t"] * rho_of(x, table) for x in uids) / sum(U[x]["t"] for x in uids)) if scale_overhead else 1.0
+                dur[w] = body + over[w] * rw
+            return simulate(order, dur) * calib, dur
+
+        tot_t = sum(u["t"] for u in U.values())
+        rb = sum(u["rb"] for u in U.values())
+        p1 = WALL_07A * (sum(u["t"] * rho_of(x, rho) for x, u in U.items()) + rb) / (tot_t + rb)
+        p2, dur2 = project(rho, False)
+        p2a, _ = project(rho, True)
+        p2b, _ = project(boot, True)
+        res[cname] = {"rho_h": {str(c): v for c, v in rho.items()}, "rho_parent": {str(c): v for c, v in rho_p.items()},
+                      "rho_h_boot975": {str(c): v for c, v in boot.items()},
+                      "n_k0_by_class": {str(c): len(us) for c, us in groups.items()},
+                      "model1_07a_form_s": p1, "model2_schedule_s": p2, "model2_overhead_scaled_s": p2a,
+                      "model2_overhead_scaled_boot975_s": p2b,
+                      "model2_worker_durations_s": dur2}
+    out["projections"] = res
+    # (c) the over-budget pass, pessimistic: the largest K0 unit ratio applied to every 07a unit's wall
+    rmax = out["k0_unit_ratio_h"]["max"]
+    ob = [x for x, u in U.items() if u["wall"] * rmax > 300.0]
+    out["overbudget_pessimistic"] = {"ratio_applied": rmax, "n_units_over_300s": len(ob), "units": ob[:50],
+                                     "added_serial_s_at_3600_each": 3600.0 * len(ob)}
+    allp = [v for r in res.values() for k, v in r.items() if k.startswith("model") and isinstance(v, float)]
+    out["projection_central_s"] = res["i_rtol_720"]["model2_schedule_s"]
+    out["projection_highest_s"] = max(allp)
+    out["projection_highest_with_overbudget_s"] = max(allp) + out["overbudget_pessimistic"]["added_serial_s_at_3600_each"]
+    out["within_48h"] = out["projection_highest_with_overbudget_s"] < LIMIT_S
+    # prompt 07c: 07b's shortfall applied
+    out["shortfall_07b"] = SHORTFALL_07B
+    out["projection_central_x_07b_shortfall_s"] = out["projection_central_s"] * SHORTFALL_07B
+    out["projection_highest_x_07b_shortfall_s"] = out["projection_highest_with_overbudget_s"] * SHORTFALL_07B
+    out["within_48h_x_07b_shortfall"] = out["projection_highest_x_07b_shortfall_s"] < LIMIT_S
+    out_p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    out_p.write_text(json.dumps(out, indent=1, default=str))
+    summ = {k: v for k, v in out.items() if k not in ("header", "k0_units", "workers_07a", "projections")}
+    print(json.dumps(summ, indent=1, default=str))
+    for c, r in res.items():
+        print(c, json.dumps({k: v for k, v in r.items() if k != "model2_worker_durations_s"}, default=str))
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07c section 6: units run by this harness against 07b's census
+(levin-phase-floor-07b-census-20261117T010200, checkpoint 396784bc...), bit for bit, with only 07c's new keys set aside.
+Copied from 07b's compare_07b.py (a record, not edited), changed where marked "prompt 07c":
+- **the "before"** is 07b's census (prov.RUN07B, its index state/index_07b.json); its units carry the same names as this
+  harness's (``h-<rtol>-<shard>-<serial>``, tree ``h``), so nothing is normalised;
+- **the keys set aside** (NEW_KEYS_07A, the name kept; each one a key 07c's harness adds, worker.py "prompt 07c", none in
+  07b's records, checked on every unit: ``new_keys_in_07_records`` must be 0): the call summary's ``num_chase_stops``,
+  ``num_chase_stopped_leaves``, ``num_chase_noise_bisections`` and ``chase_07c``; the unit's ``instrument_07c``; the
+  pickle's ``chase_07c``. 07b's own new keys (the two refusal counts, ``rate_rule_07b``) are in both records and compared;
+- **the evidence**: the parent's module is state/variants/levin_quadrature_parent.py (8956c7b6..., 07b's), h's d141a80b...;
+- **mode ``calls``** classes each call by 07c section 9.5's rule for Y1: ``untouched`` if num_chase_stops is 0, ``acted``
+  otherwise, ``no_counts`` if a count is missing (a fault, reported). Per call it records Y1's five outputs at h and at 07b
+  (value, abserr, converged, num_regions, max_depth; floats by hex) and whether they are equal (``u1_equal``, the name
+  kept), beside the whole call's diff by category (07b's), the three counts and the stop instrument's summary; and per
+  unit whether all its calls are untouched, and then whether ``total`` and ``total_abserr`` equal 07b's (hex);
+- **mode ``instr``** also requires the stop's instrument on every call of the run ON (its counts equal to the result's)
+  and on none of the run OFF; the instruments' keys (07a's, 07b's and 07c's) are set aside, the three counts are not.
+(07b's text follows, unchanged; read 07a as 07b and 07b as 07c, and the classes of mode ``calls`` as above.)
+
+levin-phase-floor prompt 07b section 6: units run by this harness against 07a's census
+(levin-phase-floor-07a-census-20261112T132744, checkpoint 461df2ec...), bit for bit, with only 07b's new keys set aside.
+Copied from 07a's compare_07a.py (a record, not edited), changed where marked "prompt 07b":
+- **the "before"** is 07a's census (prov.RUN07A, its index state/index_07a.json); its units carry the same names as this
+  harness's (``h-<rtol>-<shard>-<serial>``, tree ``h``), so nothing is normalised (``normalised`` is recorded, empty);
+- **the keys set aside** (NEW_KEYS_07B; each one a key 07b's harness adds, worker.py "prompt 07b", none in 07a's records,
+  checked on every unit: ``new_keys_in_07a_records`` must be 0): the call summary's ``num_edge_rule_refusals``,
+  ``num_range_rule_refusals`` and ``rate_rule_07b``; the unit's ``instrument_07b``; the pickle's ``rate_rule_07b``.
+  07a's own new keys (``route_07a`` and the rest) are in both records and are compared;
+- **the evidence**: the parent's module is state/variants/levin_quadrature_parent.py (4ea1948b..., 07a's), h's 8956c7b6...;
+- **mode ``calls``** classes each call by 07b section 9.5's rule for U1: ``untouched`` if num_edge_rule_refusals +
+  num_range_rule_refusals is 0, ``acted`` otherwise (with ``edge`` and ``range`` apart), ``no_counts`` if a count is
+  missing (a fault, reported). Per call it records U1's five outputs at h and at 07a (value, abserr, converged,
+  num_regions, max_depth; floats by hex) and whether they are equal (``u1_equal``), beside the whole call's diff by
+  category (07a's: ``location``, ``harness``, ``call``; one more, ``oracle``, for the unit's LiouvilleGreen oracle,
+  ``analytic_rad`` and ``analytic_06h``, whose Levin calls are not among the recorded calls); and per unit whether all its
+  calls are untouched, and then whether ``total`` and ``total_abserr`` equal 07a's (hex);
+- **mode ``instr``** also requires the rate rules' instrument on every call of the run ON and on none of the run OFF.
+(07a's text follows, unchanged; read 07 as 07a, and the classes of mode ``calls`` as above.)
+
+levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6: units run by this harness against 07's census
+(levin-phase-floor-07-census-20261108T171137, checkpoint 03dfe786...), bit for bit, by 07's comparison with only 07a's
+new keys set aside. New in 07a, after 07's compare_07.py (a record, not edited), whose matching, evidence and diff it
+follows; the diff is compare_k0.diff (06l's, unchanged), and compare_k0's ``EXCLUDED`` (timing and provenance, 07's set;
+every key ending ``_s`` likewise) is extended at import by NEW_KEYS_07A below. 07's own new keys (``guard_07`` and the
+rest) are in both records and are compared. Read only.
+
+**The keys set aside, and why each** (NEW_KEYS_07A): every one is a key 07a's harness adds (worker.py, "prompt 07a");
+none occurs in 07's records (checked on every unit compared: ``new_keys_in_07_records`` must be 0).
+- ``route_07a`` (each call's summary; the pickle's per-call list): section 6's diagnostics;
+- ``route_07a_post`` (each call's summary; the pickle): the tally of this harness's own re-solves after the timed
+  section; ``route_07a_post_unit`` (unit): the same for 06h's analytic counterfactual;
+- ``instrument_07a`` (unit): whether the diagnostics ran.
+``phase_span`` (prompt 07a section 6: "the comparison with 07's census, which sets phase_span aside") is not a key of any
+record of 07's harness, at 07 or at h; every unit compared counts its occurrences as a dict key in both records
+(``phase_span_keys``: must be 0), so that setting it aside is shown to be vacuous rather than assumed.
+**Normalised, not excluded** (modes ``whole``, ``calls``): a unit run at h (``h-X``, tree ``h``) is compared under 07's
+names (``g-X``, ``g``), in the checkpoint record and the pickle.
+
+Modes:
+- ``whole RUN_DIR OUT_JSON``: Gate W0's second clause. Every unit of a run_k0.py run made in h with the parent's module
+  installed (variant ``parent``) compared whole with 07's unit. Module evidence: each unit's
+  ``AdaptiveLevin.levin_quadrature`` is the parent's file (state/variants/levin_quadrature_parent.py, 4e5da371...), counted
+  under the root ``variant``, every other module from h, none foreign; each worker's hello shows the variant installed and
+  bound (variant_loader's evidence all true, both production importers bound to it), the module's SHA-256 4e5da371..., h's
+  own file 4ea1948b... (not executed), h at its SHA and clean; each bye, no Ray.
+- ``calls RUN_DIR OUT_JSON``: per call, every Levin call (group ``calls`` and analytic ``other_calls``) of every unit of a
+  run at h (run_k0.py's ``units.jsonl``, or the census's ``checkpoint.jsonl``) against 07's call. Each call is classed:
+  - ``window``: a stationary-point window at 07 or at h (guard_07's ``n_windows`` > 0 in either record), with
+    ``window_both``, ``window_07_only`` and ``window_h_only`` apart;
+  - otherwise by section 6's diagnostics at h (route_07a): ``own`` if a solve of the class ``own`` (its span a region the
+    driver made) changed its C2 route or LU choice; ``aux_only`` if only solves of the auxiliary classes did (a trust-check
+    quarter, a comparison half, a fallback half, other); ``untouched`` if none did;
+  - ``no_diagnostics`` if route_07a is missing or in error at h (a fault of the harness, reported).
+  Every call is diffed against 07's on everything that belongs to it (compare_07.py's _call_parts: its summary, its
+  entries of the unit's ``scan``, ``deep_lines``, ``deep_lines_06e``, every pickle list entry carrying its index, and a
+  1e-7 group call's entry of the pickle's ``scan``), and each differing path is put in one category:
+  - ``location``: guard_07's ``location_skipped_phases``, ``unlocated_minima`` and ``n_unlocated_minima`` (summary and
+    pickle): the location's own report, which 07a section 1.1 item 1 changes (Rule A's entries carried a variation; 07a's
+    carry a reason, and a phase Rule A skipped is now scanned for minima);
+  - ``harness``: this harness's own re-solves after the timed section, which re-solve spans the driver may never have
+    visited, under h's routing: the summary's ``cc_floor`` and ``declared_06h``, the unit's ``deep_lines`` and
+    ``deep_lines_06e`` lines, the pickle's ``w3``, ``deep_lines``, ``deep_lines_06e``, ``cc_floor_terms`` and
+    ``declared_terms_06h``;
+  - ``call``: everything else (the call's value, abserr, converged, regions, leaves, its trust checks' and premise checks'
+    records, its guard_07 windows and counts, its scans, ...).
+  A unit all of whose calls are of the W1 classes under a reading is checked for ``result.total`` and
+  ``result.total_abserr`` (hex) under that reading, and is diffed whole with its differing paths categorised (the unit's
+  ``analytic_06h.parent_counterfactual`` and ``w3`` are ``harness``).
+  Writes OUT_JSON (provenance, counts by class and category, per-unit summaries) and OUT_JSON's ``.calls.jsonl`` beside it
+  (one line per call: unit, kind, index, class, windows at 07 and h, route_07a counts, the differing categories and the
+  first differing paths, and value, abserr and converged at 07 and at h, hex).
+- ``det RUN_DIR_A RUN_DIR_B OUT_JSON``: Gate W0's fourth clause. The units two run_k0.py runs share, compared whole with
+  compare_k0's EXCLUDED alone (timing and provenance): every key compared, 07a's included.
+- ``instr RUN_DIR_ON RUN_DIR_OFF OUT_JSON``: Gate W0's third clause. The units of a run with the diagnostics on and one
+  with them off, compared whole with compare_k0's EXCLUDED and NEW_KEYS_07A set aside; the run ON must carry route_07a on
+  every call and the run OFF on none.
+
+Floats are compared by bit pattern (``float.hex``; NaN by repr), arrays by dtype, shape and bytes. Prints a summary.
+Exits 1 unless every comparison the mode's verdict rests on is equal and every evidence check holds (``calls`` exits 0
+whatever the classes show: it describes, and the scoring reads it).
+
+Usage: PYTHONPATH=<tree h>:<scripts> python -B compare_07a.py whole|calls RUN_DIR OUT_JSON [--processes N]
+       PYTHONPATH=<tree h>:<scripts> python -B compare_07a.py det|instr RUN_DIR_A RUN_DIR_B OUT_JSON
+"""
+
+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import json
+import multiprocessing as mp
+import re
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+import compare_k0 as CK  # noqa: E402  (06l's, unchanged)
+from prov import (CHECKPOINT07_SHA256, H_TREE, LQ_SHA256_07AH, LQ_SHA256_07APARENT, PARENT_VARIANT_07A, RUN07,  # noqa: E402
+                  TREES, header, sha256)
+from prov import CHECKPOINT07A_SHA256, LQ_SHA256_07BH, LQ_SHA256_07BPARENT, PARENT_VARIANT_07B, RUN07A  # noqa: E402  prompt 07b
+from prov import CHECKPOINT07B_SHA256, LQ_SHA256_07CH, LQ_SHA256_07CPARENT, PARENT_VARIANT_07C, RUN07B  # noqa: E402  prompt 07c
+
+# prompt 07b: the "before" is 07a's census; the module hashes and the parent variant are 07b's (the names below are
+# rebound so that 07a's code reads 07b's values)
+RUN07, CHECKPOINT07_SHA256 = RUN07A, CHECKPOINT07A_SHA256  # prompt 07b
+LQ_SHA256_07AH, LQ_SHA256_07APARENT, PARENT_VARIANT_07A = LQ_SHA256_07BH, LQ_SHA256_07BPARENT, PARENT_VARIANT_07B  # prompt 07b
+# prompt 07c: the "before" is 07b's census; the module hashes and the parent variant are 07c's
+RUN07, CHECKPOINT07_SHA256 = RUN07B, CHECKPOINT07B_SHA256  # prompt 07c
+LQ_SHA256_07AH, LQ_SHA256_07APARENT, PARENT_VARIANT_07A = LQ_SHA256_07CH, LQ_SHA256_07CPARENT, PARENT_VARIANT_07C  # prompt 07c
+
+EXCLUDED_07 = frozenset(CK.EXCLUDED)  # 07's set (06m's), as compare_k0.py has it
+NEW_KEYS_07A = frozenset({"route_07a", "route_07a_post", "route_07a_post_unit", "instrument_07a"})
+NEW_KEYS_07A_ORIG = NEW_KEYS_07A  # prompt 07b: 07a's diagnostics' keys, in both records
+NEW_KEYS_07A = frozenset({"num_edge_rule_refusals", "num_range_rule_refusals", "rate_rule_07b", "instrument_07b"})  # prompt 07b: 07b's new keys (the name kept)
+# prompt 07b: mode instr sets aside the instruments' keys, 07a's and 07b's, but not the two counts (the code's outputs)
+INSTR_KEYS_07B = NEW_KEYS_07A_ORIG | frozenset({"rate_rule_07b", "instrument_07b"})
+# prompt 07c: 07c's new keys (the name NEW_KEYS_07A kept); mode instr sets aside the three instruments' keys, not the counts
+NEW_KEYS_07B_ORIG = NEW_KEYS_07A  # prompt 07c: 07b's keys, in both records
+NEW_KEYS_07A = frozenset({"num_chase_stops", "num_chase_stopped_leaves", "num_chase_noise_bisections", "chase_07c",
+                          "instrument_07c"})  # prompt 07c
+INSTR_KEYS_07B = INSTR_KEYS_07B | frozenset({"chase_07c", "instrument_07c"})  # prompt 07c (the name kept)
+INDEX = HERE.parent / "state" / "index_07.json"
+INDEX = HERE.parent / "state" / "index_07a.json"  # prompt 07b: 07a's checkpoint
+INDEX = HERE.parent / "state" / "index_07b.json"  # prompt 07c: 07b's checkpoint
+ORACLE_RE = re.compile(r"analytic_rad|analytic_06h")  # prompt 07b: the unit's LiouvilleGreen oracle
+LOC_RE = re.compile(r"\.guard_07(\[\d+\])?\.(location_skipped_phases|unlocated_minima|n_unlocated_minima)\b")
+HARNESS_RE = re.compile(r"\.summary\[\d+\]\.(cc_floor|declared_06h)\b|\.data\.(deep_lines|deep_lines_06e)\b|"
+                        r"\.pickle\.(w3|deep_lines|deep_lines_06e|cc_floor_terms|declared_terms_06h)\b")
+UNIT_HARNESS_RE = re.compile(r"^data\.(analytic_06h\.parent_counterfactual|w3|deep_lines|deep_lines_06e)\b|"
+                             r"^data\.(calls|other_calls)\[\d+\]\.(cc_floor|declared_06h)\b|"
+                             r"^pickle\.(w3|deep_lines|deep_lines_06e|cc_floor_terms|declared_terms_06h)\b")
+UNIT_LOC_RE = re.compile(r"\.guard_07\.(location_skipped_phases|unlocated_minima|n_unlocated_minima)\b|"
+                         r"^pickle\.guard_07\[\d+\]\.(location_skipped_phases|unlocated_minima)\b")
+
+
+def set_excluded(with_new):
+    """compare_k0.excluded() reads compare_k0.EXCLUDED at call time."""
+    CK.EXCLUDED = set(EXCLUDED_07 | (NEW_KEYS_07A if with_new else frozenset()))
+
+
+def norm(p):
+    return re.sub(r"\[\d+\]", "[]", p)
+
+
+def span_key(x_span):
+    return tuple(e["hex"] if isinstance(e, dict) else float(e).hex() for e in x_span)
+
+
+def call_keys(d):
+    return [(kind, c["index"], c["notify_label"], span_key(c["x_span"]))
+            for kind, key in (("group", "calls"), ("analytic", "other_calls")) for c in d.get(key) or []]
+
+
+def region_keys(p):
+    return [(kind, r["index"], span_key(r["x_span"]))
+            for kind, key in (("group", "regions"), ("analytic", "other_regions")) for r in p.get(key) or []]
+
+
+def count_keys(x, names):
+    """How many times a name in names occurs as a dict key in x."""
+    n = 0
+    if isinstance(x, dict):
+        for k, v in x.items():
+            n += (k in names) + count_keys(v, names)
+    elif isinstance(x, (list, tuple)):
+        for v in x:
+            n += count_keys(v, names)
+    return n
+
+
+def load_07(ent):
+    with open(RUN07 / "checkpoint.jsonl", "rb") as f:
+        f.seek(ent["offset"])
+        line = f.read(ent["length"])
+    if hashlib.sha256(line).hexdigest() != ent["line_sha256"]:
+        raise SystemExit(f"07's checkpoint line sha256 differs at offset {ent['offset']}")
+    g = json.loads(line)["data"]
+    gp = ent["pickle"]
+    grec = CK.load_pickle(RUN07 / gp[0], gp[1], gp[2], gp[3])
+    return g, grec
+
+
+def load_mine(d, pdir):
+    return CK.load_pickle(Path(pdir) / d["pickle"], d["pickle_offset"], d["pickle_length"], d["pickle_record_sha256"])
+
+
+def uid_07(uid):
+    return uid  # prompt 07b: 07a's census names its units as this harness does (07a: "g-" + the rest)
+
+
+def by_index(lst):
+    out = {}
+    for e in lst or []:
+        if isinstance(e, dict) and "index" in e:
+            out.setdefault(e["index"], []).append(e)
+    return out
+
+
+def normalise(uid, d, prec):
+    """Copies of a unit's record and pickle record with h's unit and tree under 07's names."""
+    d2, prec2 = dict(d), dict(prec)
+    normalised = {}
+    for name, rec in (("data", d2), ("pickle", prec2)):
+        if rec.get("unit") == uid and uid != uid_07(uid):
+            rec["unit"] = uid_07(uid)
+            normalised[f"{name}.unit"] = [uid, uid_07(uid)]
+    if d2.get("tree") != "h":  # prompt 07b: 07a's tree name (07a: "g")
+        normalised["data.tree"] = [d2.get("tree"), "h"]
+        d2["tree"] = "h"
+    return d2, prec2, normalised
+
+
+def unit_evidence(d, variant):
+    mods = d.get("modules") or {}
+    lq_file = str(PARENT_VARIANT_07A.resolve()) if variant else str(H_TREE / "AdaptiveLevin" / "levin_quadrature.py")
+    roots = mods.get("counts_by_root") or {}
+    return {
+        "lq_file_is_expected": (mods.get("files") or {}).get("AdaptiveLevin.levin_quadrature") == lq_file,
+        "all_in_tree": bool(mods.get("all_in_tree")),
+        "no_foreign": not mods.get("foreign"),
+        "variant_root_as_expected": ("variant" in roots) == bool(variant),
+    }
+
+
+def one_whole(args):
+    """Mode whole: one unit (at the parent's module, in h) against 07's, whole (07a's new keys set aside)."""
+    uid, line, pdir, ent = args
+    set_excluded(True)
+    d = json.loads(line)["data"]
+    row = {"unit": uid, "unit_07": uid_07(uid), "status": d["status"]}
+    g, grec = load_07(ent)
+    row["status_07"] = g["status"]
+    if d["status"] != "done" or g["status"] != "done":
+        row.update(ok=False, differing=["status"], excl_present=[], excl_differing=[])
+        return row
+    prec = load_mine(d, pdir)
+    d2, prec2, normalised = normalise(uid, d, prec)
+    out = []
+    matched = call_keys(d2) == call_keys(g) and region_keys(prec2) == region_keys(grec)
+    if not matched:
+        out.append(("match", False, "calls or region records do not match"))
+    CK.diff(d2, g, "data", out)
+    CK.diff(prec2, grec, "pickle", out)
+    ev = unit_evidence(d, variant=True)
+    differing = sorted({p for p, e, _ in out if not e})
+    row.update(
+        ok=matched and not differing and all(ev.values()),
+        normalised=normalised,
+        differing=differing[:200],
+        detail=[list(x) for x in out if not x[1]][:50],
+        excl_differing=sorted({norm(p) for p, e, _ in out if e}),
+        new_keys_in_07_records=count_keys(g, NEW_KEYS_07A) + count_keys(grec, NEW_KEYS_07A),
+        phase_span_keys=count_keys(d, {"phase_span"}) + count_keys(prec, {"phase_span"}) + count_keys(g, {"phase_span"})
+        + count_keys(grec, {"phase_span"}),
+        n_calls=len(call_keys(d2)),
+        n_group_calls=sum(1 for k in call_keys(d2) if k[0] == "group"),
+        n_regions=len(region_keys(prec2)),
+        n_regions_returned=int(sum(len(r.get("start", [])) for r in (prec.get("regions") or []) + (prec.get("other_regions") or []))),
+        evidence=ev,
+    )
+    row["ok"] = row["ok"] and row["new_keys_in_07_records"] == 0 and row["phase_span_keys"] == 0
+    return row
+
+
+def _call_parts(d, prec, kind, i, pos):
+    """Everything that belongs to call i of a unit record d and its pickle record prec (compare_07.py's, unchanged)."""
+    key = "calls" if kind == "group" else "other_calls"
+    parts = {"summary": [c for c in (d.get(key) or []) if c["index"] == i], "data": {}, "pickle": {}}
+    parts["data"]["scan"] = [e for e in (d.get("scan") or []) if isinstance(e, dict) and e.get("index") == i]
+    for k in ("deep_lines", "deep_lines_06e"):
+        parts["data"][k] = {"lines": [e for e in ((d.get(k) or {}).get("lines") or []) if e.get("index") == i]}
+    for k in sorted(prec):
+        v = prec.get(k)
+        if k == "scan":
+            if kind == "group" and isinstance(v, list):
+                parts["pickle"]["scan"] = [v[pos]] if 0 <= pos < len(v) else ["<missing>"]
+            continue
+        if isinstance(v, list):
+            parts["pickle"][k] = by_index(v).get(i, [])
+    return parts
+
+
+def category(path):
+    if ORACLE_RE.search(path):  # prompt 07b
+        return "oracle"
+    if LOC_RE.search(path):
+        return "location"
+    if HARNESS_RE.search(path):
+        return "harness"
+    return "call"
+
+
+def unit_category(path):
+    if ORACLE_RE.search(path):  # prompt 07b
+        return "oracle"
+    if UNIT_LOC_RE.search(path):
+        return "location"
+    if UNIT_HARNESS_RE.search(path):
+        return "harness"
+    return "call"
+
+
+def fx_of(s, k):
+    v = s.get(k) if s else None
+    return v.get("hex") if isinstance(v, dict) else v
+
+
+def one_calls(args):
+    """Mode calls: every call against 07's, classed and diffed by category."""
+    uid, line, pdir, ent = args
+    set_excluded(True)
+    d = json.loads(line)["data"]
+    row = {"unit": uid, "unit_07": uid_07(uid), "status": d["status"], "rtol": d.get("rtol")}
+    g, grec = load_07(ent)
+    row["status_07"] = g["status"]
+    if d["status"] != "done" or g["status"] != "done":
+        row.update(ok=False, problem="status", calls=[])
+        return row
+    prec = load_mine(d, pdir)
+    d2, prec2, normalised = normalise(uid, d, prec)
+    ck_m, ck_g = call_keys(d2), call_keys(g)
+    row["matched"] = ck_m == ck_g and region_keys(prec2) == region_keys(grec)
+    if not row["matched"]:
+        row.update(ok=False, problem=f"calls {len(ck_m)}/{len(ck_g)} or region records do not match", calls=[])
+        return row
+    pos_of = {c["index"]: j for j, c in enumerate(d2.get("calls") or [])}
+    pos_of_g = {c["index"]: j for j, c in enumerate(g.get("calls") or [])}
+    calls = []
+    for kind, i, label, _span in ck_m:
+        key = "calls" if kind == "group" else "other_calls"
+        s = next(c for c in d2[key] if c["index"] == i)
+        s7 = next(c for c in g[key] if c["index"] == i)
+        g7h, g77 = s.get("guard_07") or {}, s7.get("guard_07") or {}
+        r7a = s.get("route_07a")
+        win07 = bool(g77.get("n_windows"))
+        winh = bool(g7h.get("n_windows"))
+        # prompt 07b: the class is U1's (section 9.5): untouched iff the two counts sum to 0
+        ne_, nr_ = s.get("num_edge_rule_refusals"), s.get("num_range_rule_refusals")
+        if ne_ is None or nr_ is None:
+            cls = "no_counts"
+        elif ne_ + nr_ == 0:
+            cls = "untouched"
+        elif ne_ > 0 and nr_ > 0:
+            cls = "acted_both"
+        elif ne_ > 0:
+            cls = "acted_edge"
+        else:
+            cls = "acted_range"
+        # prompt 07c: the class is Y1's (section 9.5): untouched iff num_chase_stops is 0; 07b's class kept as cls_07b
+        cls_07b = cls
+        ns_, nl_, nb_ = s.get("num_chase_stops"), s.get("num_chase_stopped_leaves"), s.get("num_chase_noise_bisections")
+        if ns_ is None or nl_ is None or nb_ is None:
+            cls = "no_counts"
+        elif ns_ == 0:
+            cls = "untouched"
+        else:
+            cls = "acted"
+        c07c = s.get("chase_07c")
+        u1_keys = ("value", "abserr", "converged", "num_regions", "max_depth")
+        u1_h = [fx_of(s, k) for k in u1_keys]
+        u1_7a = [fx_of(s7, k) for k in u1_keys]
+        r7b = s.get("rate_rule_07b")
+        out = []
+        a = _call_parts(d2, prec2, kind, i, pos_of.get(i, -1))
+        b = _call_parts(g, grec, kind, i, pos_of_g.get(i, -1))
+        CK.diff(a, b, f"call[{kind}:{i}]", out)
+        diffs = sorted({p for p, e, _ in out if not e})
+        cats = {}
+        for p in diffs:
+            cats.setdefault(category(p), []).append(p)
+        post = s.get("route_07a_post") or {}
+        calls.append({
+            "kind": kind, "index": i, "label": label, "class": cls, "win07": win07, "winh": winh,
+            # prompt 07c: 07b's class, the three counts and the stop instrument's summary
+            "class_07b": cls_07b, "num_chase_stops": ns_, "num_chase_stopped_leaves": nl_,
+            "num_chase_noise_bisections": nb_,
+            "chase_07c": None if not isinstance(c07c, dict) or "error" in c07c else {
+                k: c07c.get(k) for k in ("frame_found", "fail", "n_stopped", "n_unrecorded", "n_stopped_roots",
+                                        "n_counted", "counts_equal_result", "by_route", "by_depth", "n_not_returned",
+                                        "share_sum", "carrier")},
+            "chase_07c_error": isinstance(c07c, dict) and "error" in c07c,
+            # prompt 07b: the two counts, U1's five outputs and their equality, the instrument's counts
+            "num_edge_rule_refusals": ne_, "num_range_rule_refusals": nr_,
+            "u1_equal": u1_h == u1_7a, "u1_differing": [k for k, a_, b_ in zip(u1_keys, u1_h, u1_7a) if a_ != b_],
+            "rate_rule_07b": None if not isinstance(r7b, dict) or "error" in r7b else {
+                k: r7b.get(k) for k in ("n_evaluated", "n_unrecorded", "by_rule", "refused_by_depth", "n_depth_unknown",
+                                        "counts_equal_result")},
+            "rate_rule_07b_error": isinstance(r7b, dict) and "error" in r7b,
+            "untrusted": [s.get("num_untrusted_regions"), s7.get("num_untrusted_regions")],
+            "n_windows_07": g77.get("n_windows"), "n_windows_h": g7h.get("n_windows"),
+            "n_points_07": g77.get("n_points"), "n_points_h": g7h.get("n_points"),
+            "guarded_07": g77.get("num_guarded_regions"), "guarded_h": g7h.get("num_guarded_regions"),
+            "skipped_07": g77.get("location_skipped_phases"), "skipped_h": g7h.get("location_skipped_phases"),
+            "route": None if not isinstance(r7a, dict) or "error" in r7a else {
+                k: r7a[k] for k in ("n_solves", "n_route_differs", "n_lu_differs", "n_route_differs_own", "n_lu_differs_own",
+                                    "n_route_differs_aux", "n_lu_differs_aux", "differs_by_class",
+                                    "n_partition_nonforced", "n_cc_with_levin_half", "n_forced")},
+            "post_differs": {k: [v.get("n_route_differs"), v.get("n_lu_differs")] for k, v in post.items() if v},
+            "equal": not diffs,
+            "categories": sorted(cats),
+            "n_differing": {c_: len(v) for c_, v in cats.items()},
+            "first_differing": {c_: v[:6] for c_, v in cats.items()},
+            "value": [fx_of(s, "value"), fx_of(s7, "value")],
+            "abserr": [fx_of(s, "abserr"), fx_of(s7, "abserr")],
+            "converged": [s.get("converged"), s7.get("converged")],
+            "num_regions": [s.get("num_regions"), s7.get("num_regions")],
+            "max_depth": [s.get("max_depth"), s7.get("max_depth")],
+        })
+    # the unit, under each reading of W1: (A) every solve counts, (B) the own solves only
+    w1_classes = {"A": {"untouched"}, "B": {"untouched", "aux_only"}}
+    w1_classes = {"A": {"untouched"}}  # prompt 07b: U1's one reading (the counts)
+    t_m, t_g = d2.get("result") or {}, g.get("result") or {}
+    row["total"] = [fx_of(t_m, "total"), fx_of(t_g, "total")]
+    row["total_abserr"] = [fx_of(t_m, "total_abserr"), fx_of(t_g, "total_abserr")]
+    row["total_converged"] = [t_m.get("total_converged"), t_g.get("total_converged")]
+    row["totals_equal"] = row["total"][0] == row["total"][1] and row["total_abserr"][0] == row["total_abserr"][1]
+    row["all_w1"] = {r_: all(c["class"] in cl for c in calls) for r_, cl in w1_classes.items()}
+    if any(row["all_w1"].values()):  # prompt 07b: U1's one reading (07a: A or B)
+        out = []
+        CK.diff(d2, g, "data", out)
+        CK.diff(prec2, grec, "pickle", out)
+        diffs = sorted({p for p, e, _ in out if not e})
+        cats = {}
+        for p in diffs:
+            cats.setdefault(unit_category(p), []).append(p)
+        row["whole"] = {"equal": not diffs, "categories": sorted(cats), "n_differing": {c_: len(v) for c_, v in cats.items()},
+                        "first_differing": {c_: v[:8] for c_, v in cats.items()}}
+    row.update(
+        ok=True,
+        normalised=normalised,
+        new_keys_in_07_records=count_keys(g, NEW_KEYS_07A) + count_keys(grec, NEW_KEYS_07A),
+        phase_span_keys=count_keys(d, {"phase_span"}) + count_keys(prec, {"phase_span"}) + count_keys(g, {"phase_span"})
+        + count_keys(grec, {"phase_span"}),
+        post_unit=d.get("route_07a_post_unit") and {k: v for k, v in d["route_07a_post_unit"].items() if k != "changed"},
+        evidence=unit_evidence(d, variant=False),
+        calls=calls,
+    )
+    return row
+
+
+def worker_evidence(run_dir, variant):
+    out = {}
+    if (run_dir / "units.jsonl").exists():
+        for wdir in sorted(p for p in run_dir.iterdir() if p.is_dir()):
+            hello = bye = None
+            for line in open(wdir / "emit.jsonl"):
+                m = json.loads(line)
+                if m["kind"] == "hello":
+                    hello = m["evidence"]
+                elif m["kind"] == "bye":
+                    bye = m
+            out[wdir.name] = (hello, bye)
+    else:
+        for hp in sorted(run_dir.glob("worker_*_hello.json")):
+            name = hp.name[len("worker_"):-len("_hello.json")]
+            bp = run_dir / f"worker_{name}_bye.json"
+            out[name] = (json.loads(hp.read_text())["evidence"], json.loads(bp.read_text()) if bp.exists() else None)
+    checks = {}
+    for name, (h, b) in out.items():
+        tree = None if h is None else h.get("tree")
+        v = None if h is None else h.get("variant_06k")
+        c = {
+            "tree": tree,
+            "hello": h is not None,
+            "levin_quadrature_sha256": None if h is None else h.get("levin_quadrature_sha256"),
+            "lq_file": None if h is None else h["files"].get("AdaptiveLevin.levin_quadrature"),
+            "tree_file_is_h": h is not None and h.get("levin_quadrature_tree_file_sha256") == LQ_SHA256_07AH,
+            "all_in_tree": h is not None and bool(h.get("all_in_tree")),
+            "tree_head_clean": h is not None and tree in TREES and h.get("head") == TREES[tree][1]
+            and h.get("status_short", "x").strip() == "",
+            "bye": b is not None,
+            "ray_not_initialized": b is not None and b.get("ray_initialized") is False,
+            "no_foreign_modules": b is not None and not b.get("modules_foreign"),
+        }
+        if variant:
+            c["lq_is_parent"] = h is not None and h.get("levin_quadrature_sha256") == LQ_SHA256_07APARENT
+            c["variant_installed_and_bound"] = v is not None and v.get("path") == str(PARENT_VARIANT_07A.resolve()) and all(
+                v.get(k) for k in ("sys_modules_is_variant", "package_attribute_is_variant", "package_function_is_variants",
+                                   "QuadSourceIntegral_binds_variants", "three_bessel_integrals_binds_variants")) and \
+                v.get("sha256") == v.get("sha256_now") == LQ_SHA256_07APARENT
+            c["tree_lq_not_loaded"] = b is not None and not any(
+                f == str(H_TREE / "AdaptiveLevin" / "levin_quadrature.py") for _n, f in (b.get("modules_own_tree") or []))
+        else:
+            c["lq_is_h"] = h is not None and h.get("levin_quadrature_sha256") == LQ_SHA256_07AH
+            c["variant_none"] = h is not None and v is None
+        checks[name] = c
+    return checks
+
+
+def jobs_of(run_dir, idx):
+    src = run_dir / "units.jsonl"
+    k0 = src.exists()
+    if not k0:
+        src = run_dir / "checkpoint.jsonl"
+    jobs, seen, skipped = [], set(), []
+    with open(src, "rb") as f:
+        for line in f:
+            r = json.loads(line)
+            uid = r["unit"]
+            if not re.fullmatch(r"h-1e-[78]-\d+-\d+", uid):
+                skipped.append(uid)  # e.g. an over-budget pass's record, compared apart
+                continue
+            if uid in seen:
+                raise SystemExit(f"{uid} twice in {src}")
+            seen.add(uid)
+            if uid_07(uid) not in idx:
+                raise SystemExit(f"{uid_07(uid)} is not in 07's census")
+            pdir = run_dir / r["worker"] if k0 else run_dir
+            jobs.append((uid, line, str(pdir), idx[uid_07(uid)]))
+    return src, jobs, skipped
+
+
+def run_pair(a, mode):
+    """Modes det and instr: two run_k0.py runs, unit by unit, whole."""
+    set_excluded(False)
+    if mode == "instr":  # prompt 07b: the instruments' keys (07a: NEW_KEYS_07A)
+        CK.EXCLUDED = set(EXCLUDED_07 | INSTR_KEYS_07B)
+    ra, rb = Path(a.run_dir).resolve(), Path(a.run_dir_b).resolve()
+    title = ("Gate Y0 (4), determinism" if mode == "det" else "Gate Y0 (3), the instrument on against off")  # prompt 07c
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), f"levin-phase-floor 07c: {title}, {ra.name} against {rb.name}",  # prompt 07c
+                 [ra / "units.jsonl", rb / "units.jsonl", HERE / "compare_k0.py"])
+    for l in hdr:
+        print(l, flush=True)
+
+    def load(run):
+        recs = {}
+        for line in open(run / "units.jsonl", "rb"):
+            r = json.loads(line)
+            recs[r["unit"]] = (r["data"], load_mine(r["data"], run / r["worker"]))
+        return recs
+
+    A, B = load(ra), load(rb)
+    rows = []
+    for uid in sorted(set(A) | set(B)):
+        if uid not in A or uid not in B:
+            rows.append({"unit": uid, "ok": False, "differing": ["unit in one run only"]})
+            continue
+        (da, pa), (db, pb) = A[uid], B[uid]
+        out = []
+        CK.diff(da, db, "data", out)
+        CK.diff(pa, pb, "pickle", out)
+        differing = sorted({p for p, e, _ in out if not e})
+        cs_a = (da.get("calls") or []) + (da.get("other_calls") or [])
+        cs_b = (db.get("calls") or []) + (db.get("other_calls") or [])
+        g7 = [c.get("guard_07") for c in cs_a]
+        r7 = [c.get("route_07a") for c in cs_a]
+        ok = not differing and da["status"] == "done" and db["status"] == "done"
+        extra = {}
+        if mode == "instr":
+            extra["A_route_07a_on_every_call"] = bool(cs_a) and all(isinstance(c.get("route_07a"), dict) and "error" not in c["route_07a"] for c in cs_a)
+            extra["B_route_07a_on_no_call"] = all(c.get("route_07a") is None for c in cs_b) and db.get("instrument_07a") is False
+            ok = ok and extra["A_route_07a_on_every_call"] and extra["B_route_07a_on_no_call"] and da.get("instrument_07a") is True
+            # prompt 07b: the rate rules' instrument on every call of A (and its refusals equal to the counts), on none of B
+            extra["A_rate_rule_07b_on_every_call"] = bool(cs_a) and all(
+                isinstance(c.get("rate_rule_07b"), dict) and "error" not in c["rate_rule_07b"]
+                and c["rate_rule_07b"].get("counts_equal_result") is True for c in cs_a)
+            extra["B_rate_rule_07b_on_no_call"] = all(c.get("rate_rule_07b") is None for c in cs_b) and db.get("instrument_07b") is False
+            extra["refusals"] = [(c["index"], c.get("num_edge_rule_refusals"), c.get("num_range_rule_refusals")) for c in cs_a]
+            ok = ok and extra["A_rate_rule_07b_on_every_call"] and extra["B_rate_rule_07b_on_no_call"] and da.get("instrument_07b") is True
+            # prompt 07c: the stop's instrument on every call of A (its counts equal to the result's), on none of B
+            extra["A_chase_07c_on_every_call"] = bool(cs_a) and all(
+                isinstance(c.get("chase_07c"), dict) and "error" not in c["chase_07c"]
+                and c["chase_07c"].get("counts_equal_result") is True for c in cs_a)
+            extra["B_chase_07c_on_no_call"] = all(c.get("chase_07c") is None for c in cs_b) and db.get("instrument_07c") is False
+            extra["stops"] = [(c["index"], c.get("num_chase_stops"), c.get("num_chase_stopped_leaves"),
+                               c.get("num_chase_noise_bisections"), (c.get("chase_07c") or {}).get("n_stopped"))
+                              for c in cs_a]
+            ok = ok and extra["A_chase_07c_on_every_call"] and extra["B_chase_07c_on_no_call"] and da.get("instrument_07c") is True
+        rows.append({"unit": uid, "status": [da["status"], db["status"]], "ok": ok,
+                     "differing": differing[:200], "detail": [list(x) for x in out if not x[1]][:50],
+                     "excl_differing": sorted({norm(p) for p, e, _ in out if e}),
+                     "n_calls": len(g7), "n_calls_windowed": sum(1 for x in g7 if isinstance(x, dict) and x.get("n_windows")),
+                     "n_calls_route_changed": sum(1 for x in r7 if isinstance(x, dict) and (x.get("n_route_differs") or x.get("n_lu_differs"))),
+                     "route_changes": [(c["index"], c["route_07a"].get("n_route_differs"), c["route_07a"].get("n_lu_differs"),
+                                        c["route_07a"].get("own_differs"), c["route_07a"].get("aux_differs"))
+                                       for c in cs_a if isinstance(c.get("route_07a"), dict) and "error" not in c["route_07a"]
+                                       and (c["route_07a"].get("n_route_differs") or c["route_07a"].get("n_lu_differs"))],
+                     "windows": [(c["index"], c["guard_07"].get("n_windows"), c["guard_07"].get("n_points_by_kind"))
+                                 for c in cs_a if isinstance(c.get("guard_07"), dict) and c["guard_07"].get("n_windows")],
+                     "wall_s": [da.get("wall_s"), db.get("wall_s")],
+                     "refusals_07b": [(c["index"], c.get("num_edge_rule_refusals"), c.get("num_range_rule_refusals"))
+                                      for c in cs_a if (c.get("num_edge_rule_refusals") or 0) + (c.get("num_range_rule_refusals") or 0)],  # prompt 07b
+                     "stops_07c": [(c["index"], c.get("num_chase_stops"), c.get("num_chase_stopped_leaves"),
+                                    c.get("num_chase_noise_bisections"))
+                                   for c in cs_a if (c.get("num_chase_stops") or 0) or (c.get("num_chase_noise_bisections") or 0)],  # prompt 07c
+                     **extra})
+    wev = {**{f"A:{k}": v for k, v in worker_evidence(ra, False).items()}, **{f"B:{k}": v for k, v in worker_evidence(rb, False).items()}}
+    wev_ok = bool(wev) and all(all(v for k, v in c.items() if k not in ("levin_quadrature_sha256", "lq_file", "tree")) for c in wev.values())
+    n_fail = sum(1 for r in rows if not r["ok"])
+    doc = {"header": hdr, "mode": mode, "excluded": sorted(CK.EXCLUDED) + ["*_s"], "n_units": len(rows),
+           "n_units_identical": len(rows) - n_fail, "worker_evidence_all_hold": wev_ok, "worker_evidence": wev, "units": rows}
+    Path(a.out_json).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    Path(a.out_json).write_text(json.dumps(doc, indent=1, default=str))
+    for r in rows:
+        print(f"{r['unit']}: {'IDENTICAL' if r['ok'] else 'DIFFERS'}; status {r.get('status')}; calls {r.get('n_calls')}; "
+              f"windowed {r.get('n_calls_windowed')} {r.get('windows')}; route-changed {r.get('n_calls_route_changed')} "
+              f"{r.get('route_changes')}; wall {r.get('wall_s')}; differing {r['differing'][:10]}; excluded keys differing "
+              f"{r.get('excl_differing')}" + (f"; on/off {r.get('A_route_07a_on_every_call')}/{r.get('B_route_07a_on_no_call')}" if mode == "instr" else "")
+              + (f"; chase_07c on/off {r.get('A_chase_07c_on_every_call')}/{r.get('B_chase_07c_on_no_call')}" if mode == "instr" else "")
+              + f"; stops (index, stops, stopped leaves, counted) {r.get('stops_07c')}")  # prompt 07c
+    print(f"worker evidence all hold {wev_ok}")
+    print(f"wrote {a.out_json} sha256 {sha256(a.out_json)}")
+    return 0 if n_fail == 0 and wev_ok else 1
+
+
+CLASSES = ("untouched", "aux_only", "own", "window_both", "window_07_only", "window_h_only", "no_diagnostics")
+CLASSES = ("untouched", "acted_edge", "acted_range", "acted_both", "no_counts")  # prompt 07b
+CLASSES = ("untouched", "acted", "no_counts")  # prompt 07c
+
+
+def summarise_calls(rows):
+    s = {"by_class": {}, "by_class_rtol": {}, "equal_by_class": {}, "categories_by_class": {}, "units": {}}
+    for r in rows:
+        for c in r.get("calls") or []:
+            k = c["class"]
+            s["by_class"][k] = s["by_class"].get(k, 0) + 1
+            kr = f"{r['rtol']}|{c['kind']}|{k}"
+            s["by_class_rtol"][kr] = s["by_class_rtol"].get(kr, 0) + 1
+            e = s["equal_by_class"].setdefault(k, {"equal": 0, "differing": 0})
+            e["equal" if c["equal"] else "differing"] += 1
+            u = s.setdefault("u1_equal_by_class", {}).setdefault(kr, {"equal": 0, "differing": 0})  # prompt 07b
+            u["equal" if c["u1_equal"] else "differing"] += 1
+            key = "+".join(c["categories"]) or "none"
+            cc = s["categories_by_class"].setdefault(k, {})
+            cc[key] = cc.get(key, 0) + 1
+    for reading in ("A",):  # prompt 07b: U1's one reading (07a: "A", "B")
+        all_ = [r for r in rows if (r.get("all_w1") or {}).get(reading)]
+        s["units"][reading] = {"n_all_w1": len(all_), "n_totals_equal": sum(1 for r in all_ if r["totals_equal"]),
+                               "n_whole_equal": sum(1 for r in all_ if (r.get("whole") or {}).get("equal")),
+                               "whole_categories": {}}
+        for r in all_:
+            key = "+".join((r.get("whole") or {}).get("categories") or []) or "none"
+            s["units"][reading]["whole_categories"][key] = s["units"][reading]["whole_categories"].get(key, 0) + 1
+    return s
+
+
+def main():
+    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
+    ap.add_argument("mode", choices=("whole", "calls", "det", "instr"))
+    ap.add_argument("run_dir")
+    ap.add_argument("rest", nargs="+")
+    ap.add_argument("--processes", type=int, default=1)
+    a = ap.parse_args()
+    if a.processes > 6:
+        raise SystemExit("at most six processes")
+    if a.mode in ("det", "instr"):
+        if len(a.rest) != 2:
+            raise SystemExit(f"{a.mode} RUN_DIR_A RUN_DIR_B OUT_JSON")
+        a.run_dir_b, a.out_json = a.rest
+        return run_pair(a, a.mode)
+    if len(a.rest) != 1:
+        raise SystemExit(f"{a.mode} RUN_DIR OUT_JSON")
+    a.out_json = a.rest[0]
+    set_excluded(True)
+    run_dir = Path(a.run_dir).resolve()
+    idx_doc = json.loads(INDEX.read_text())
+    if idx_doc["checkpoint_sha256"] != CHECKPOINT07_SHA256:
+        raise SystemExit("the index is not of 07's checkpoint")
+    src, jobs, skipped = jobs_of(run_dir, idx_doc["units"])
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), f"levin-phase-floor 07c: {run_dir.name} against 07b's census ({RUN07.name}), "  # prompt 07c
+                 f"mode {a.mode}", [src, INDEX, HERE / "compare_k0.py"])
+    for l in hdr:
+        print(l, flush=True)
+    print(f"# {len(jobs)} units to compare ({len(skipped)} other records skipped: {skipped[:10]}), {a.processes} "
+          f"process(es); excluded: 07's {sorted(EXCLUDED_07)} and every key ending _s; set aside as new: "
+          f"{sorted(NEW_KEYS_07A)} (prompt 07c: 07c's)", flush=True)
+    fn = one_whole if a.mode == "whole" else one_calls
+    if a.processes > 1:
+        with mp.get_context("spawn").Pool(a.processes) as pool:
+            rows = list(pool.imap(fn, jobs, chunksize=8))
+    else:
+        rows = [fn(j) for j in jobs]
+    variant = a.mode == "whole"
+    wev = worker_evidence(run_dir, variant)
+    wev_ok = bool(wev) and all(all(v for k, v in c.items() if k not in ("levin_quadrature_sha256", "lq_file", "tree")) for c in wev.values())
+    ev_tot = {}
+    for r in rows:
+        for k, v in (r.get("evidence") or {}).items():
+            ev_tot.setdefault(k, [0, 0])[0 if v else 1] += 1
+    doc = {"header": hdr, "mode": a.mode, "run_dir": str(run_dir), "source": str(src), "source_sha256": sha256(src),
+           "run07": str(RUN07), "checkpoint07_sha256": CHECKPOINT07_SHA256,
+           "excluded_07": sorted(EXCLUDED_07) + ["*_s"], "set_aside_new_07a": sorted(NEW_KEYS_07A),
+           "records_skipped": skipped, "n_units": len(rows),
+           "statuses": {s: sum(1 for r in rows if r["status"] == s) for s in sorted({r["status"] for r in rows})},
+           "unit_evidence_true_false": ev_tot, "worker_evidence_all_hold": wev_ok, "worker_evidence": wev,
+           "new_keys_in_07_records": sum(r.get("new_keys_in_07_records", 0) or 0 for r in rows),
+           "phase_span_keys": sum(r.get("phase_span_keys", 0) or 0 for r in rows)}
+    out_p = Path(a.out_json)
+    out_p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    if a.mode == "whole":
+        n_fail = sum(1 for r in rows if not r["ok"])
+        inv = {}
+        for r in rows:
+            for p in r.get("excl_differing", []):
+                inv[p] = inv.get(p, 0) + 1
+        tot = {k: sum(r.get(k, 0) or 0 for r in rows) for k in ("n_calls", "n_group_calls", "n_regions", "n_regions_returned")}
+        doc.update(n_units_equal=len(rows) - n_fail, n_units_differing=n_fail, totals=tot,
+                   excluded_keys_differing_units=dict(sorted(inv.items())),
+                   first_differing_units=[r for r in rows if not r["ok"]][:20],
+                   units=[{k: v for k, v in r.items() if k != "detail"} | ({"detail": r.get("detail")} if not r["ok"] else {})
+                          for r in rows])
+        out_p.write_text(json.dumps(doc, indent=1, default=str))
+        for r in [r for r in rows if not r["ok"]][:20]:
+            print(f"DIFFERS {r['unit']}: {r['differing'][:10]}", flush=True)
+        print(f"units {len(rows)}; equal {len(rows) - n_fail}; differing {n_fail}; statuses {doc['statuses']}")
+        print(f"totals {tot}; new keys in 07's records {doc['new_keys_in_07_records']}; phase_span keys {doc['phase_span_keys']}")
+        print(f"unit evidence (true, false): {ev_tot}")
+        print(f"worker evidence: {len(wev)} workers, all hold {wev_ok}; levin_quadrature sha256 "
+              f"{sorted({str(c['levin_quadrature_sha256']) for c in wev.values()})}")
+        print(f"excluded keys differing (path: units): {doc['excluded_keys_differing_units']}")
+        print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")
+        return 0 if n_fail == 0 and wev_ok and doc["new_keys_in_07_records"] == 0 else 1
+    # calls
+    calls_p = out_p.with_name(out_p.name.replace(".json", "") + ".calls.jsonl")
+    with open(calls_p, "w") as fc:
+        for r in rows:
+            for c in r.get("calls") or []:
+                fc.write(json.dumps({"unit": r["unit"], "rtol": r["rtol"], **c}, default=str) + "\n")
+    summ = summarise_calls(rows)
+    problems = [{k: r.get(k) for k in ("unit", "status", "status_07", "problem")} for r in rows if not r.get("ok")]
+    doc.update(summary=summ, problems=problems, calls_jsonl=str(calls_p), calls_jsonl_sha256=sha256(calls_p),
+               units=[{k: v for k, v in r.items() if k != "calls"} for r in rows])
+    out_p.write_text(json.dumps(doc, indent=1, default=str))
+    print(f"units {len(rows)}; problems {len(problems)} {problems[:5]}; statuses {doc['statuses']}")
+    print(f"calls by class: {summ['by_class']}")
+    print(f"equal by class: {summ['equal_by_class']}")
+    print(f"U1's five outputs equal by rtol|kind|class: {json.dumps(summ.get('u1_equal_by_class'))}")  # prompt 07b
+    print(f"differing categories by class: {json.dumps(summ['categories_by_class'])}")
+    print(f"units all of W1's classes, reading A (every solve) / B (own solves): {json.dumps(summ['units'])}")
+    print(f"new keys in 07's records {doc['new_keys_in_07_records']}; phase_span keys {doc['phase_span_keys']}")
+    print(f"unit evidence (true, false): {ev_tot}")
+    print(f"worker evidence: {len(wev)} workers, all hold {wev_ok}")
+    print(f"wrote {calls_p} sha256 {sha256(calls_p)}")
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")
+    return 0 if not problems and wev_ok and doc["new_keys_in_07_records"] == 0 else 1
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07c section 6 (the census agent): 07b's census run directory, 07b's K0 run directory, 07b's
+census working records and Part A's records (var/levin-phase-floor-07c/measurement/, whose replay records Y8 reads),
+hashed, read only. New in 07c, after 07b's hash_runs_07b.py (a record, not edited), whose form it keeps.
+
+- **07b's census run directory** (prov.RUN07B): every file, against 07b's KEPT_ON_MAC_STUDIO.txt (in the tree h,
+  docs/levin-phase-floor/07b/census/), section "run directory var/runs/levin-phase-floor-07b-census-20261117T010200/":
+  150 files listed, every one present with its SHA-256 and size, none unlisted; the checkpoint's SHA-256 396784bc...;
+  16 region pickles.
+- **07b's K0 run directory** (prov.RUN07B_K0): against the same file's section for it (5 files).
+- **07b's census working records** (prov.REC07B): every file hashed; the pickles and files over 2 MB listed in 07b's
+  KEPT_ON_MAC_STUDIO.txt (its first section, 54 files) present with their SHA-256 and size.
+- **Part A's records** (var/levin-phase-floor-07c/measurement/): every file hashed (no list to check against; before
+  and after are compared).
+
+Writes OUT_TXT (header, one line per file: sha256, size, path) and prints the verdicts. Exits 1 unless every check holds.
+Usage: python -B hash_runs_07c.py LABEL OUT_TXT
+"""
+
+import os
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import CHECKPOINT07B_SHA256, H_TREE, MAIN, REC07B, RUN07B, RUN07B_K0, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+KEPT07B = H_TREE / "docs" / "levin-phase-floor" / "07b" / "census" / "KEPT_ON_MAC_STUDIO.txt"
+PART_A = MAIN / "var" / "levin-phase-floor-07c" / "measurement"
+
+
+def hash_dir(root, rel_to):
+    out = {}
+    for r, _dirs, files in os.walk(root):
+        for name in files:
+            p = Path(r) / name
+            out[str(p.relative_to(rel_to))] = (sha256(p), p.stat().st_size)
+    return dict(sorted(out.items()))
+
+
+def kept07b(section_marker):
+    want = {}
+    sec = False
+    for line in KEPT07B.read_text().splitlines():
+        if line.startswith("## "):
+            sec = section_marker in line
+            continue
+        if sec and line and not line.startswith("#"):
+            sha, size, rel = line.split(None, 2)
+            want[rel] = (sha, int(size))
+    return want
+
+
+def main():
+    label, out_p = sys.argv[1], Path(sys.argv[2])
+    lines = header(Path(__file__).resolve(),
+                   f"levin-phase-floor 07c: 07b's census and K0 run directories and working records, and Part A's records, "
+                   f"{label}", [KEPT07B])
+    ok = True
+    verdicts = []
+    for run, n_want, n_pkl_want in ((RUN07B, 150, 16), (RUN07B_K0, 5, 0)):
+        kept = kept07b(f"run directory var/runs/{run.name}/")
+        got = hash_dir(run, run.parent)
+        lines.append(f"# {run}: {len(got)} files, {sum(s for _, s in got.values())} bytes")
+        for rel, (sha, size) in got.items():
+            lines.append(f"{sha}  {size}  {rel}")
+        if run == RUN07B:
+            ck = got.get(f"{run.name}/checkpoint.jsonl", (None, None))[0]
+            verdicts.append(f"{run.name}: checkpoint sha256 {ck} {'EQUAL' if ck == CHECKPOINT07B_SHA256 else 'DIFFERS FROM'} "
+                            f"{CHECKPOINT07B_SHA256}")
+            ok &= ck == CHECKPOINT07B_SHA256
+        n_pkl = sum(1 for r in got if Path(r).name.startswith("regions_") and r.endswith(".pkl"))
+        verdicts.append(f"{run.name}: {n_pkl} region pickles")
+        ok &= n_pkl == n_pkl_want
+        bad = [r for r, v in kept.items() if got.get(r) != v]
+        extra = sorted(set(got) - set(kept))
+        verdicts.append(f"{run.name}: against 07b's KEPT_ON_MAC_STUDIO.txt: {len(kept)} listed, {len(got)} present, "
+                        f"{len(bad)} differing or missing {bad[:5]}, {len(extra)} not listed {extra[:5]}")
+        ok &= not bad and not extra and len(kept) == n_want
+    kept_r = kept07b("under var/levin-phase-floor-07b/census/")
+    got_r = hash_dir(REC07B, REC07B)
+    lines.append(f"# {REC07B}: {len(got_r)} files, {sum(s for _, s in got_r.values())} bytes")
+    for rel, (sha, size) in got_r.items():
+        lines.append(f"{sha}  {size}  REC07B/{rel}")
+    bad_r = [r for r, v in kept_r.items() if got_r.get(r) != v]
+    verdicts.append(f"{REC07B}: against 07b's KEPT_ON_MAC_STUDIO.txt (pickles and files over 2 MB): {len(kept_r)} listed, "
+                    f"{len(bad_r)} differing or missing {bad_r[:5]}; {len(got_r)} files hashed in all")
+    ok &= not bad_r and len(kept_r) == 54
+    got_a = hash_dir(PART_A, PART_A)
+    lines.append(f"# {PART_A}: {len(got_a)} files, {sum(s for _, s in got_a.values())} bytes")
+    for rel, (sha, size) in got_a.items():
+        lines.append(f"{sha}  {size}  PARTA/{rel}")
+    verdicts.append(f"{PART_A}: {len(got_a)} files hashed (compared before against after)")
+    lines += ["# " + v for v in verdicts] + [f"# verdict: {'ALL HOLD' if ok else 'A CHECK FAILS'}"]
+    out_p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    out_p.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
+    print("\n".join(l for l in lines if l.startswith("#")))
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")
+    return 0 if ok else 1
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())

+

+from prov import CHECKPOINT06M_SHA256, RUN06M  # noqa: E402  prompt 07

+from prov import CHECKPOINT07A_SHA256, RUN07A  # noqa: E402  prompt 07b
+from prov import CHECKPOINT07B_SHA256, RUN07B  # noqa: E402  prompt 07c

+CHECKPOINT06G_SHA256 = "03adcbba8a78d82d9d6b899b8eee8b9ae1659763f52deb066d2840af3d82c0fa"
+
+
+def main():
+    out_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
+    run = RUN07B  # prompt 07c (07b: RUN07A; 07a: RUN07; 07: RUN06M; 06m: RUN06L_B; 06l: RC.census_run_06g())
+    ck = run / "checkpoint.jsonl"
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07c: an index of 07b's census checkpoint", [ck])  # prompt 07c  # prompt 07b  # prompt 07a  # prompt 06l: the title (06k: 06k); prompt 06m: the title
+    for l in hdr:
+        print(l)
+    if RC.sha256(ck) != CHECKPOINT07B_SHA256:  # prompt 07c (07b: CHECKPOINT07A_SHA256; 07a: CHECKPOINT07_SHA256; 07: CHECKPOINT06M_SHA256; 06m: CHECKPOINT06L_B_SHA256)
+        raise SystemExit("07b's checkpoint sha256 differs")  # prompt 07c
+    units = {}
+    off = 0
+    with open(ck, "rb") as f:
+        for line in f:
+            r = json.loads(line)
+            d = r["data"]
+            uid = r["unit"]
+            if uid in units:
+                raise SystemExit(f"{uid} recorded twice")
+            units[uid] = {
+                "offset": off,
+                "length": len(line),
+                "line_sha256": hashlib.sha256(line).hexdigest(),
+                "recorded": r.get("recorded"),
+                "status": d.get("status"),
+                "worker": d.get("worker"),
+                "rtol": d.get("rtol"),
+                "row": d.get("row"),
+                "k_key": d.get("k_key"),
+                "wall_s": d.get("wall_s"),
+                "rebuild_s": d.get("rebuild_s"),
+                "n_group_calls": len(d.get("calls") or []),
+                "n_other_calls": len(d.get("other_calls") or []),
+                "n_group_unconverged": sum(1 for c in (d.get("calls") or []) if not c.get("converged")),
+                "n_cache_built": len(d.get("cache_built") or []),
+                "pickle": [d.get("pickle"), d.get("pickle_offset"), d.get("pickle_length"), d.get("pickle_record_sha256")],

+                              (c.get("guard_07") or {}).get("n_points"), (c.get("guard_07") or {}).get("num_guarded_regions"),
+                              (c.get("guard_07") or {}).get("num_guard_at_depth_max"),
+                              (c.get("guard_07") or {}).get("location_skipped_phases")]
+                             for kind, key in (("group", "calls"), ("analytic", "other_calls")) for c in d.get(key) or []],
+            }
+            off += len(line)
+    doc = {"header": hdr, "checkpoint": str(ck), "checkpoint_sha256": CHECKPOINT07B_SHA256,  # prompt 07c
+           "n_units": len(units), "units": units}
+    out_p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    out_p.write_text(json.dumps(doc))
+    walls = sorted(u["wall_s"] for u in units.values() if u["wall_s"] is not None)
+    st = {}
+    for u in units.values():
+        st[u["status"]] = st.get(u["status"], 0) + 1
+    print(f"units {len(units)}; statuses {st}; wall_s max {walls[-1]:.1f} p99 {walls[int(0.99 * len(walls))]:.1f} "
+          f"median {walls[len(walls) // 2]:.2f} sum {sum(walls):.0f}")
+    rb = sorted((u["rebuild_s"] or 0.0) for u in units.values())
+    print(f"rebuild_s max {rb[-1]:.1f} p99 {rb[int(0.99 * len(rb))]:.2f} median {rb[len(rb) // 2]:.3f} sum {sum(rb):.0f}")
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {RC.sha256(out_p)}")
+    return 0
+

+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07c section 6: the list of files kept on the Mac Studio only (not copied to the laptop):
+every file of the census and k0 run directories, and every pickle and every file over 2 MB under
+var/levin-phase-floor-07c/census/, each with its SHA-256 and size. Copied from 07b's kept_list_07b.py (a record, not edited; it was copied from 07a's kept_list_07a.py), changed only in its run directories, its paths and its title. Read only.
+Usage: python -B kept_list_07c.py OUT_TXT [RUN_ID ...] (the run directories: the k0 run, and the census run once it exists)"""
+import os
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import MAIN, WIDER, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+RUNS = ["levin-phase-floor-07b-census-20261117T010200",  # prompt 07b
+        "levin-phase-floor-07b-k0-20261116T204402"]  # prompt 07b
+RUNS = None  # prompt 07c: the run ids are given on the command line (sys.argv[2:])
+
+
+def main():
+    out_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
+    runs = sys.argv[2:]  # prompt 07c
+    L = ["# levin-phase-floor 07c census agent: files kept on the Mac Studio only (mps026298), not copied to the laptop.",
+         f"# sha256  size_bytes  path. Written by scripts/kept_list_07c.py (sha256 {sha256(Path(__file__))})."]
+    L += ["", "## pickles, files over 2 MB, and the raw worker streams and unit records under state/ (emit.jsonl, units.jsonl) and state/smoke/y8_on_07b_census.json, under var/levin-phase-floor-07c/census/"]
+    n = 0
+    for root, _d, files in sorted(os.walk(WIDER)):
+        for f in sorted(files):
+            p = Path(root) / f
+            if p == out_p.resolve():
+                continue
+            s = p.stat().st_size
+            # prompt 07c: also kept here by judgment, not size: the raw worker streams and unit records of the Y0 and
+            # smoke runs (emit.jsonl, units.jsonl under state/; their verdicts are in out/), and the smoke dry run of the
+            # Y8 scorer against 07b's census (state/smoke/y8_on_07b_census.json)
+            raw = p.relative_to(WIDER).parts[0] == "state" and f in ("emit.jsonl", "units.jsonl")
+            raw |= str(p.relative_to(WIDER)) == "state/smoke/y8_on_07b_census.json"
+            if f.endswith(".pkl") or s > 2 * 1024 * 1024 or raw:
+                L.append(f"{sha256(p)}  {s}  {p.relative_to(WIDER)}")
+                n += 1
+    L.append(f"# {n} files")
+    for run in runs:  # prompt 07c (07b: RUNS)
+        d = MAIN / "var" / "runs" / run
+        L += ["", f"## run directory var/runs/{run}/ (every file)"]
+        tot = cnt = 0
+        for root, _d, files in sorted(os.walk(d)):
+            for f in sorted(files):
+                p = Path(root) / f
+                s = p.stat().st_size
+                L.append(f"{sha256(p)}  {s}  {p.relative_to(d.parent)}")
+                tot += s
+                cnt += 1
+        L.append(f"# {cnt} files, {tot} bytes")
+    out_p.write_text("\n".join(L) + "\n")
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} ({len(L)} lines) sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07c section 6: write Gate Y0's plan and launch 06j's driver run_06j.py (07b's copy, unchanged)
