+
+Plan ``k0`` (Gate Y0, slug ``k0``), stage by stage, each stage after the one before, never more than six computing
+processes:
+1. ``k0_run_parent``: 06k's 100 K0 units (docs/levin-phase-floor/06l/out/k0/units.json, in the tree h) run by run_k0.py in
+   the tree h with the parent's module installed (variant ``parent``: state/variants/levin_quadrature_parent.py, git show
+   cff4237f:..., 8956c7b6..., byte-identical to 07b's census module), the diagnostics on, six workers, into
+   state/k0/parent (stop_on_fail);
+2. ``k0_compare_parent``: compare_07c.py ``whole`` against 07b's census into out/k0/compare_y0b.json (Y0's second clause);
+3. ``k0_run_h``: the same 100 units at h (h's own module, d141a80b..., code 67b5af74's), six workers, into state/k0/h:
+   the census's own unit timings at h for its cost projection, and the unit the third clause chooses;
+4. ``k0_compare_h``: compare_07c.py ``calls`` against 07b's census into out/k0/compare_k0_h_calls.json (descriptive: Y1's
+   classing on those units, before the census);
+5. ``y0_select``: select_y0_units_07c.py on state/k0/h: the units of the third and fourth clauses, by the rule in its
+   docstring (state/y0c3_units.json, state/y0c4_units.json);
+6. ``y0c3_run``: Y0's third clause, the two units (one where the fix acts, h-1e-8-0-33929, fixed by name; one where it
+   does not, chosen from the K0 units at h) run at h with the instruments on and, at the same time in fresh workers, off,
+   into state/y0c3/on and state/y0c3/off (at most four workers);
+7. ``y0c3_compare``: compare_07c.py ``instr`` on the two into out/y0c3/compare_instr.json;
+8. ``y0c4_run``: Y0's fourth clause, the unit where the fix acts run at h twice, in two fresh workers at once, into
+   state/y0c4/run1 and state/y0c4/run2;
+9. ``y0c4_compare``: compare_07c.py ``det`` on the two into out/y0c4/compare_det.json.

+Plan ``k0`` (Gate W0, slug ``k0``), stage by stage, each stage after the one before, never more than six computing

+7. ``w0c3_compare``: compare_07a.py ``instr`` on the two into out/w0c3/compare_instr.json;
+8. ``w0c4_run``: W0's fourth clause, the window unit run at h twice, in two fresh workers at once, into state/w0c4/run1
+   and state/w0c4/run2;
+9. ``w0c4_compare``: compare_07a.py ``det`` on the two into out/w0c4/compare_det.json.
+
+Plan ``references`` (the user's references at the W5 stop, state/ruling_w5.md item 2; slug ``references``), as 07's plan
+``references`` (launch_07.py, the user's ruling at 07's stop 4): one stage, one reading at a time (each reading's tool runs
+six worker processes), FCC r1 and r2 on each of the four calls of out/refs/inputs.json, QAWO r1 and r2 on those whose QAWO
+readings project at 30 minutes or less (out/refs/refs_projection.json, its ``readings``), each call's readings in the
+tool's own order, the calls cheapest first, each reading by 06g's one_reading.py (unchanged) in its own process,
+PYTHONPATH the tree h then this directory, into out/refs/readings/<sid>-<impl>-r<res>.json; run_06j.py's run backstop
+as 07's (report past 30 minutes, stop at a projected finish of 48 h).

+BACKSTOP_S = 172800.0  # prompt 07a: as 07's references run
+
+
+def plan_references():  # prompt 07a: the user's references (state/ruling_w5.md item 2)
+    inputs = json.loads((ROOT / "out" / "refs" / "inputs.json").read_text())["inputs"]
+    proj = json.loads((ROOT / "out" / "refs" / "refs_projection.json").read_text())["calls"]
+    if set(inputs) != set(proj) or len(inputs) != 4:
+        raise SystemExit("the inputs and the projection do not name the same four calls")
+    cost = {s: sum(proj[s]["projection"][f"{i}_r{r}"]["with_margin_s"] for i, r in proj[s]["readings"]) for s in proj}
+    (ROOT / "out" / "refs" / "readings").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    jobs = []
+    for sid in sorted(proj, key=lambda s: (cost[s], s)):
+        e = inputs[sid]
+        for impl, res in proj[sid]["readings"]:
+            jobs.append({"name": f"{sid}-{impl}-r{res}", "pythonpath": f"{TREE}:{SCRIPTS}", "requires": e["path"],
+                         "projected_s": proj[sid]["projection"][f"{impl}_r{res}"]["with_margin_s"],
+                         "argv": py("one_reading.py", e["path"], impl, res, f"out/refs/readings/{sid}-{impl}-r{res}.json",
+                                    "--sid", sid, "--unit", e["unit"], "--index", e["index"], "--rtol", e["rtol"],
+                                    "--workers", 6)})
+    return {"label": "references", "backstop_s": BACKSTOP_S, "check_after_s": 1800.0,
+            "stages": [{"name": "readings", "max_parallel": 1, "jobs": jobs}]}
+

+                   "command's start and end, and every 60 s by a thread of the driver while its process lives.")
+
+
+def main():
+    label = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else None
+    if label not in PURPOSES:
+        raise SystemExit(f"usage: launch_07c.py {'|'.join(PURPOSES)}")  # prompt 07c
+    plan = {"k0": plan_k0_07c}[label]()  # prompt 07a; prompt 07b: k0 only; prompt 07c: 07c's plan
+    plan_p = ROOT / "state" / f"plan_{label}.json"
+    if plan_p.exists():
+        raise SystemExit(f"{plan_p} exists; not overwritten")
+    plan_p.write_text(json.dumps(plan, indent=1))
+    n = sum(len(st["jobs"]) for st in plan["stages"])
+    argv = [PYTHON, "-B", str(SCRIPT), "--run-dir", "<run dir>", "--plan", str(plan_p)]
+    run = runregistrykit.begin(
+        campaign="levin-phase-floor", prompt="07c", slug=label, purpose=PURPOSES[label], argv=argv, cwd=str(ROOT),  # prompt 07b
+        script=str(SCRIPT), unit="commands", expected_units=n, checkpoint=True, results=None, scope=SCOPES[label],
+        heartbeat_means=HEARTBEAT_MEANS)
+    env = dict(os.environ)
+    env.pop("PYTHONPATH", None)
+    env["PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE"] = "1"
+    stdout = open(run.stdout_path, "ab")
+    stderr = open(run.stderr_path, "ab")
+    command = " ".join(shlex.quote(a) for a in [PYTHON, "-B", str(SCRIPT), "--run-dir", run.path, "--plan", str(plan_p)]) + "; exit $?"
+    child = subprocess.Popen(["nohup", "sh", "-c", command], cwd=str(ROOT), env=env, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
+                             stdout=stdout, stderr=stderr, start_new_session=True, close_fds=True)
+    stdout.close()
+    stderr.close()
+    run.heartbeat(pid=child.pid, stage="launched (detached sh started)")
+    print(f"run id      {run.id}")
+    print(f"run dir     {run.path}")
+    print(f"plan        {plan_p} ({n} commands)")
+    print(f"sh pid      {child.pid}")
+    print(f"command     {command}")
+    print(f"launcher    pid {os.getpid()}, exiting without waiting")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()
+    sys.exit(0)

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07c section 6: one pass over 07c's census (and 07b's census beside it, by state/index_07b.json,
+each line's SHA-256 checked) gathering what Y2, Y3, Y5, Y9 and the descriptive items need. Copied from 07b's score_07b.py
+(a record, not edited), changed only where marked "prompt 07c": the before is 07b's census (every "07a" below reads
+"07b", every "07b" reads "07c"); the 720 are the rows whose 1e-7 call shows a sign change in 07b's census's 06a scan;
+per unit the stop's records are aggregated (``r07c``: per call the three counts, by kind; every stopped leaf from the
+pickle's ``chase_07c`` arrays by kind, route, trust before the stop and depth; the share and r of each; every call's
+carrying stopped leaf where its ``converged`` differs from 07b's); a unit is untouched iff every call's num_chase_stops is
+0. The depth-20 descent (07b's r07b_unit) is kept as it is: a call is kept when its ``converged`` differs from the before's
+or it is unconverged here, and its depth-20 leaves listed with their route and whether each lies inside a leaf the rate
+rules refused (Y9 (i)). 07b's text follows.
+
+levin-phase-floor prompt 07b section 6: one pass over 07b's census (and 07a's census beside it, by state/index_07a.json,
+each line's SHA-256 checked) gathering what U2, U3, U5 and the descriptive items need. Copied from 07a's score_07a.py (a
+record, not edited), changed only where marked "prompt 07b": the before is 07a's census; the 720 are the rows whose 1e-7
+call shows a sign change in 07a's census's 06a scan (as 07a took them from 07's); every unit's pickle is read, and per
+unit the rate rules' evaluations are aggregated (``r07b``: refusals and evaluations by kind, rule and depth, q histograms
+by kind and rule, every edge refusal in full); a call is also kept when its ``converged`` differs from 07a's or it is
+unconverged at 07b, and then its depth-20 leaves are listed with their route (Levin; a window piece; a guarded leaf;
+Clenshaw-Curtis otherwise) and whether each lies inside a leaf the rate rules refused (``depth20``). U1 and U8 are
+compare_07b.py's and score_u8_07b.py's, U4/U6 score_v4_07b.py's and score_refs_07b.py's, U7 xcheck_07b.py's; this script
+decides no verdict (summarise_07b.py does). 07a's text follows.
+
+levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6: one pass over the census h (and 07's census beside it, by state/index_07.json,
+each line's SHA-256 checked) gathering what W2, W3, W5 and the descriptive items need. New in 07a, after 07's
+score_07.py (a record, not edited), whose V2 and V3 extraction (scan_07 against the returned leaves and the located
+roots) it keeps. Read only. W1, W7 and the classing are compare_07a.py's (out/stop3/), W4 score_v4_07a.py's, W6
+xcheck_07a.py's; this script decides no verdict (summarise_07a.py does, by state/predictions.md).
+
+Per unit: rtol, row, band, k, in the 720 (07's census scan at 1e-7, as 07 defined the 720), status, unit times, totals,
+row depth and group regions at h and at 07. Per call, kept when it carries anything W2, W3, W5 or a descriptive item
+reads: its route_07a summary (solves changed by class, the partition counts, forced and gated, the Clenshaw-Curtis
+regions with a Levin half, the margins, the largest |theta| at a gated end), every changed solve in full from the
+pickle (``changed_all``: V, 07's estimate, direction, role, class), guard_07 at h and 07 (points, windows with their stop
+records, unlocated minima, guarded counts), converged, value, abserr, depth and regions at both, the scan (V2's listed
+leaves with their window and guard flags, V3's sign changes and h), and an unconverged call's flag measures.
+
+Usage: PYTHONPATH=<tree h>:<scripts> python -B score_07a.py RUN_DIR OUT_JSON [--processes N]
+"""
+
+import argparse
+from collections import Counter  # prompt 07b
+import hashlib
+import json
+import math
+import multiprocessing as mp
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import CHECKPOINT07_SHA256, RUN07, WIDER, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+from prov import CHECKPOINT07A_SHA256, RUN07A  # noqa: E402  prompt 07b
+from prov import CHECKPOINT07B_SHA256, RUN07B  # noqa: E402  prompt 07c
+
+INDEX07 = WIDER / "state" / "index_07.json"
+INDEX07 = WIDER / "state" / "index_07a.json"  # prompt 07b
+RUN07, CHECKPOINT07_SHA256 = RUN07A, CHECKPOINT07A_SHA256  # prompt 07b: the before is 07a's census
+INDEX07 = WIDER / "state" / "index_07b.json"  # prompt 07c
+RUN07, CHECKPOINT07_SHA256 = RUN07B, CHECKPOINT07B_SHA256  # prompt 07c: the before is 07b's census
+ROWS = WIDER / "state" / "rows.json"
+SIX = 6.0 * math.pi
+RUN = None
+
+
+def H(x):
+    return None if x is None else float.fromhex(x["hex"] if isinstance(x, dict) else x)
+
+
+def load_pickle(path, off, length, sha):
+    import ray.cloudpickle as cp
+
+    with open(path, "rb") as f:
+        f.seek(off)
+        blob = f.read(length)
+    if hashlib.sha256(blob).hexdigest() != sha:
+        raise RuntimeError(f"{path}@{off}: sha256 differs")
+    return cp.loads(blob)
+
+
+def unit_time(d):
+    t = d.get("wall_s") or 0.0
+    for k in ("scan_s", "scan_07_s", "cc_floor_s", "declared_06h_s", "checked_06g_s"):
+        t += d.get(k) or 0.0
+    for k in ("w3", "deep_lines", "analytic_06h"):
+        t += (d.get(k) or {}).get("seconds") or 0.0
+    return t
+
+
+def call_flag(c, total_abserr):
+    v, e = H(c["value"]), H(c["abserr"])
+    req = max(c["atol"], c["rtol"] * abs(v))
+    return {"within_request": e <= req, "over_request_ratio": e / req if req > 0 else math.inf,
+            "node_limited": bool(c.get("node_limited")), "untrusted": int((c.get("leaves") or {}).get("untrusted") or 0),
+            "n_at_depth_max": c.get("n_at_depth_max"), "share": e / total_abserr if total_abserr else None}
+
+
+def _qbin(q):  # prompt 07b: log10 q in quarter-decade bins
+    if q != q:
+        return "nan"
+    if math.isinf(q):
+        return "inf"
+    return f"{math.floor(4 * math.log10(max(q, 1.0))) / 4:.2f}"
+
+
+def r07b_unit(calls, rec, d7calls):  # prompt 07b
+    """The rate rules' evaluations of a unit, aggregated; and, for the calls whose converged differs or that are
+    unconverged at 07b, their depth-20 leaves with route and descent."""
+    agg = {"refused": Counter(), "evaluated": Counter(), "q_refused": Counter(), "q_passed": Counter(), "edge_refusals": [],
+           "calls_acted": Counter(), "refusals_counts": Counter(), "n_unrecorded": 0, "n_counts_unequal": 0,
+           "width_refused": Counter()}
+    rr = {e["index"]: e for e in (rec or {}).get("rate_rule_07b") or []}
+    regarr = {e["index"]: e for e in ((rec or {}).get("regions") or []) + ((rec or {}).get("other_regions") or [])}
+    depth20 = []
+    for kind, c in calls:
+        ne, nr = c.get("num_edge_rule_refusals") or 0, c.get("num_range_rule_refusals") or 0
+        agg["refusals_counts"][(kind, "edge")] += ne
+        agg["refusals_counts"][(kind, "range")] += nr
+        agg["calls_acted"][(kind, "any" if ne + nr else "none")] += 1
+        agg["calls_acted"][(kind, "edge>0")] += ne > 0
+        agg["calls_acted"][(kind, "range>0")] += nr > 0
+        s7 = c.get("rate_rule_07b") or {}
+        agg["n_counts_unequal"] += not s7.get("counts_equal_result", False)
+        e = (rr.get(c["index"]) or {}).get("record")
+        if e is None:
+            continue
+        agg["n_unrecorded"] += e["n_unrecorded"]
+        names = {0: "edge", 1: "range"}
+        for lo, hi, dep, rule, q, ref in zip(e["lo"], e["hi"], e["depth"], e["rule"], e["q"], e["refused"]):
+            rn = names.get(int(rule), "other")
+            agg["evaluated"][(kind, rn, int(dep))] += 1
+            if ref:
+                agg["refused"][(kind, rn, int(dep))] += 1
+                agg["q_refused"][(kind, rn, _qbin(float(q)))] += 1
+                w = float(hi) - float(lo)
+                agg["width_refused"][(kind, rn, f"{math.floor(math.log10(w)) if w > 0 else 'zero'}")] += 1
+                if rn == "edge":
+                    agg["edge_refusals"].append({"kind": kind, "index": c["index"], "lo": float(lo).hex(), "hi": float(hi).hex(),
+                                                 "depth": int(dep), "q": float(q)})
+            else:
+                agg["q_passed"][(kind, rn, _qbin(float(q)))] += 1
+        c7 = d7calls.get((kind, c["index"]))
+        if (c7 is not None and c7["converged"] != c["converged"]) or not c["converged"]:
+            ra = regarr.get(c["index"])
+            ref_spans = [(float(lo), float(hi), int(dep)) for lo, hi, dep, ref in zip(e["lo"], e["hi"], e["depth"], e["refused"]) if ref]
+            leaves = []
+            if ra is not None:
+                for j in range(len(ra["start"])):
+                    if int(ra["depth"][j]) < 20:
+                        continue
+                    a, b = float(ra["start"][j]), float(ra["end"][j])
+                    lo_, hi_ = min(a, b), max(a, b)
+                    route = ("window piece" if int(ra["window_07"][j]) == 1 else "guarded leaf" if int(ra["guarded_07"][j]) == 1
+                             else "Levin" if int(ra["type"][j]) == 0 else "Clenshaw-Curtis")
+                    anc = [dp for (l_, h_, dp) in ref_spans if l_ <= lo_ and hi_ <= h_ and dp < int(ra["depth"][j])]
+                    leaves.append((route, bool(anc), min(anc) if anc else None))
+            depth20.append({"kind": kind, "index": c["index"], "label": c["notify_label"],
+                            "converged": [c["converged"], None if c7 is None else c7["converged"]],
+                            "refusals": [ne, nr], "regions": [c["num_regions"], None if c7 is None else c7["num_regions"]],
+                            "max_depth": [c["max_depth"], None if c7 is None else c7["max_depth"]],
+                            "untrusted": c.get("num_untrusted_regions"), "value": c["value"]["hex"], "abserr": c["abserr"]["hex"],
+                            "value_07a": None if c7 is None else c7["value"]["hex"], "abserr_07a": None if c7 is None else c7["abserr"]["hex"],
+                            "n_depth20": len(leaves), "routes": dict(Counter(x[0] for x in leaves)),
+                            "descends_from_refused": sum(1 for x in leaves if x[1]),
+                            "routes_descending": dict(Counter(x[0] for x in leaves if x[1])),
+                            "refused_ancestor_depths": dict(Counter(x[2] for x in leaves if x[1]))})
+    out = {k: ({"|".join(map(str, kk)): vv for kk, vv in v.items()} if isinstance(v, Counter) else v) for k, v in agg.items()}
+    return out, depth20
+
+
+def r07c_unit(calls, rec, d7calls):  # prompt 07c
+    """The stop's records of a unit: the three counts per call by kind; every stopped leaf by kind, route, trust before
+    the stop and depth, with its share and r; and the carrying stopped leaf of each call whose converged differs from the
+    before's."""
+    agg = {"counts": Counter(), "calls_acted": Counter(), "leaves": Counter(), "share_bins": Counter(), "r_bins": Counter(),
+           "n_counts_unequal": 0, "n_instrument_error": 0, "n_not_returned": 0, "carriers": [], "max_share": None}
+    cf = {e["index"]: e for e in (rec or {}).get("chase_07c") or []}
+    for kind, c in calls:
+        ns, nl, nb = c.get("num_chase_stops") or 0, c.get("num_chase_stopped_leaves") or 0, c.get("num_chase_noise_bisections") or 0
+        agg["counts"][(kind, "stops")] += ns
+        agg["counts"][(kind, "stopped_leaves")] += nl
+        agg["counts"][(kind, "noise_bisections")] += nb
+        agg["calls_acted"][(kind, "acted" if ns else "untouched")] += 1
+        agg["calls_acted"][(kind, "counted>0")] += nb > 0
+        s7 = c.get("chase_07c")
+        if not isinstance(s7, dict) or "error" in s7:
+            agg["n_instrument_error"] += 1
+            continue
+        agg["n_counts_unequal"] += not s7.get("counts_equal_result", False)
+        agg["n_not_returned"] += s7.get("n_not_returned") or 0
+        e = (cf.get(c["index"]) or {}).get("record")
+        if e:
+            for typ, tb, dep, sh, r in zip(e["type"], e["trusted_before"], e["depth"], e["share"], e["r"]):
+                route = "Levin" if typ == "Levin" else "Clenshaw-Curtis"
+                trust = "trusted" if tb is True else "untrusted" if tb is False else "unchecked"
+                agg["leaves"][(kind, route, trust, int(dep))] += 1
+                if sh is not None:
+                    agg["share_bins"][(kind, route, f"1e{math.floor(math.log10(sh))}" if sh > 0 else "zero")] += 1
+                    if agg["max_share"] is None or sh > agg["max_share"][0]:
+                        agg["max_share"] = (sh, kind, c["index"], route, trust, int(dep), r)
+                if r is not None:
+                    agg["r_bins"][(kind, route, "r<0.25" if r < 0.25 else "r<1" if r < 1 else "r>=1")] += 1
+        c7 = d7calls.get((kind, c["index"]))
+        if c7 is not None and c7["converged"] != c["converged"]:
+            agg["carriers"].append({"kind": kind, "index": c["index"], "converged": [c["converged"], c7["converged"]],
+                                    "counts": [ns, nl, nb], "carrier": s7.get("carrier")})
+    out = {k: ({"|".join(map(str, kk)): vv for kk, vv in v.items()} if isinstance(v, Counter) else v) for k, v in agg.items()}
+    return out
+
+
+def one(args):
+    uid, off, length, ent, rowinfo, in720, run = args
+    with open(Path(run) / "checkpoint.jsonl", "rb") as f:
+        f.seek(off)
+        line = f.read(length)
+    r = json.loads(line)
+    assert r["unit"] == uid
+    d = r["data"]
+    with open(RUN07 / "checkpoint.jsonl", "rb") as f:

+        line7 = f.read(ent["length"])

+        raise RuntimeError(f"{uid}: the before's line sha256 differs")  # prompt 07b; prompt 07c: 07b's
+    m = json.loads(line7)["data"]
+    out = {"unit": uid, "rtol": d["rtol"], "row": d["row"], "band": rowinfo["band"], "k": rowinfo["k"], "in720": in720,
+           "status": d["status"], "status_07": m["status"], "wall_s": [d.get("wall_s"), m.get("wall_s")],
+           "time_s": [unit_time(d), unit_time(m)], "scan_07_error": d.get("scan_07_error"),
+           "post_unit": d.get("route_07a_post_unit") and {k: v for k, v in d["route_07a_post_unit"].items() if k != "changed"}}
+    if d["status"] != "done":
+        return out
+    calls = [("group", c) for c in d["calls"]] + [("analytic", c) for c in d["other_calls"]]
+    calls7 = {(k, c["index"]): c for k, c in [("group", c) for c in m["calls"]] + [("analytic", c) for c in m["other_calls"]]}
+    res, res7 = d.get("result") or {}, m.get("result") or {}
+    out["total"] = [(res.get("total") or {}).get("hex"), (res7.get("total") or {}).get("hex")]
+    out["total_abserr"] = [(res.get("total_abserr") or {}).get("hex"), (res7.get("total_abserr") or {}).get("hex")]
+    out["total_converged"] = [res.get("total_converged"), res7.get("total_converged")]
+    out["row_depth"] = [max((c["max_depth"] for _, c in calls), default=None),
+                        max((c["max_depth"] for c in calls7.values()), default=None)]
+    out["group_regions"] = [sum(c["num_regions"] for c in d["calls"]), sum(c["num_regions"] for c in m["calls"])]
+    out["all_regions"] = [sum(c["num_regions"] for _, c in calls), sum(c["num_regions"] for c in calls7.values())]
+    out["n_solves"] = sum(((c.get("route_07a") or {}).get("n_solves") or 0) for _, c in calls)
+    tae, tae7 = H(res.get("total_abserr")), H(res7.get("total_abserr"))
+    need = any((c.get("guard_07") or {}).get("locates") or (c.get("guard_07") or {}).get("guards")
+               or ((c.get("route_07a") or {}).get("n_route_differs") or 0) + ((c.get("route_07a") or {}).get("n_lu_differs") or 0)
+               > (c.get("route_07a") or {}).get("n_changed_kept", 0) for _, c in calls)
+    need |= any((s.get("n_regions_listed") or 0) > 0 or (s.get("n_sign_changes") or 0) > 0 for s in d.get("scan_07") or [])
+    rec = load_pickle(Path(run) / d["pickle"], d["pickle_offset"], d["pickle_length"], d["pickle_record_sha256"])  # prompt 07b: always
+    g7full = {e["index"]: e for e in (rec or {}).get("guard_07") or []}
+    s7full = {e["index"]: e for e in (rec or {}).get("scan_07") or []}
+    r7afull = {e["index"]: e for e in (rec or {}).get("route_07a") or []}
+    regarr = {e["index"]: e for e in ((rec or {}).get("regions") or []) + ((rec or {}).get("other_regions") or [])}
+    out["calls"] = []
+    agg = {"partition_forced": 0, "partition_nonforced": 0, "cc_lh": 0, "cc_lh_07est": 0, "guarded": 0,
+           "guard_at_depth_max": 0, "no_build": 0, "route_errors": 0, "theta_max": {}, "m6": None, "m20": None,
+           "n_route": 0, "n_lu": 0}
+    for kind, c in calls:
+        g7 = c.get("guard_07") or {}
+        c7 = calls7.get((kind, c["index"]))
+        g77 = (c7 or {}).get("guard_07") or {}
+        ra = c.get("route_07a")
+        if not isinstance(ra, dict) or "error" in ra:
+            agg["route_errors"] += 1
+            ra = {}
+        part = ra.get("partition") or {}
+        agg["partition_forced"] += part.get("forced", 0)
+        agg["partition_nonforced"] += ra.get("n_partition_nonforced", 0)
+        agg["cc_lh"] += ra.get("n_cc_with_levin_half", 0)
+        agg["cc_lh_07est"] += ra.get("n_cc_with_levin_half_07est", 0)
+        agg["guarded"] += int(g7.get("num_guarded_regions") or 0)
+        agg["guard_at_depth_max"] += int(g7.get("num_guard_at_depth_max") or 0)
+        agg["no_build"] += ra.get("n_no_build", 0)
+        agg["n_route"] += ra.get("n_route_differs", 0)
+        agg["n_lu"] += ra.get("n_lu_differs", 0)
+        th = H(ra.get("theta_end_max_nonforced"))
+        if th is not None and th == th and (agg["theta_max"].get(kind) is None or th > agg["theta_max"][kind][0]):
+            agg["theta_max"][kind] = (th, c["index"], c["notify_label"])
+        for key_, nm in (("min_margin_6pi_same", "m6"), ("min_margin_20pi_same_levin", "m20")):
+            mm = ra.get(key_)
+            if mm and (agg[nm] is None or H(mm["margin"]) < agg[nm][0]):
+                agg[nm] = (H(mm["margin"]), kind, c["index"], {k_: (H(v_) if isinstance(v_, dict) else v_) for k_, v_ in mm.items()})
+        row = {"kind": kind, "index": c["index"], "label": c["notify_label"], "converged": [c["converged"], (c7 or {}).get("converged")],
+               "value": [c["value"]["hex"], (c7 or {}).get("value", {}).get("hex")],
+               "abserr": [c["abserr"]["hex"], (c7 or {}).get("abserr", {}).get("hex")],
+               "max_depth": [c["max_depth"], (c7 or {}).get("max_depth")], "regions": [c["num_regions"], (c7 or {}).get("num_regions")],
+               "n_at_depth_max": c.get("n_at_depth_max"), "locates": g7.get("locates"), "guards": g7.get("guards"),
+               "n_windows": [g7.get("n_windows"), g77.get("n_windows")], "n_points": [g7.get("n_points"), g77.get("n_points")],
+               "skipped": [g7.get("location_skipped_phases") or [], g77.get("location_skipped_phases") or []],
+               "route": {k_: ra.get(k_) for k_ in ("n_solves", "n_route_differs", "n_lu_differs", "differs_by_class", "partition",
+                                                    "n_cc_with_levin_half", "n_cc_with_levin_half_07est", "n_forced")}}
+        keep = bool(g7.get("locates") or g7.get("guards") or g77.get("locates") or row["skipped"][0] or row["skipped"][1]
+                    or not c["converged"] or not (c7 or {}).get("converged") or ra.get("n_route_differs") or ra.get("n_lu_differs")
+                    or ra.get("n_partition_nonforced") or part.get("forced") or ra.get("n_cc_with_levin_half"))
+        if ra.get("n_route_differs") or ra.get("n_lu_differs"):
+            full_r = (r7afull.get(c["index"]) or {}).get("record") or {}
+            row["changed"] = [{k_: x.get(k_) for k_ in ("a", "b", "desc", "role", "class", "made", "returned", "depth",
+                                                           "V", "est_07", "est_over_V", "decision", "is_direct", "lu_used")}
+                              for x in (full_r.get("changed_all") if full_r else ra.get("changed") or [])]
+        if ra.get("n_cc_with_levin_half"):
+            row["cc_with_levin_half"] = ra.get("cc_with_levin_half")
+        if not c["converged"]:
+            row["flag"] = call_flag(c, tae)
+        if c7 is not None and not c7["converged"]:
+            row["flag_07"] = call_flag(c7, tae7)
+        if g7.get("locates") or g7.get("guards") or g77.get("locates"):
+            full = g7full.get(c["index"]) or {}
+            row["node_limited"] = c.get("node_limited")
+            row["points"] = [{"kind": p["kind"], "found_as": p.get("found_as"), "x": p["x"], "lambda": p.get("lambda"),
+                              "window_pieces": p.get("window_pieces")} for p in full.get("stationary_points") or []]
+            row["windows"] = [{k: w.get(k) for k in ("lo", "hi", "iroff1_count", "stopped", "stopped_after_split",
+                                                    "leaves_at_stop", "leaves_falling_at_stop", "max_depth_at_stop")}
+                              | {"n_pieces": max(len(w.get("cuts") or []) - 1, 0)} for w in full.get("stationary_windows") or []]
+            row["windows_07"] = [{k: w.get(k) for k in ("iroff1_count", "stopped", "leaves_falling_at_stop", "n_pieces")}
+                                 for w in g77.get("windows") or []]
+            row["n_windows_stopped"] = [g7.get("n_windows_stopped"), g77.get("n_windows_stopped")]
+            row["num_window_pieces"] = [full.get("num_window_pieces"), g77.get("num_window_pieces")]
+            row["num_guarded_regions"] = full.get("num_guarded_regions")
+            row["returned"] = g7.get("returned")
+        row["unlocated_minima_lambda"] = [m_.get("lambda") for m_ in (g7full.get(c["index"]) or {}).get("unlocated_minima") or []]
+        if not row["unlocated_minima_lambda"] and g7.get("n_unlocated_minima"):
+            row["unlocated_minima_lambda"] = [m_.get("lambda") for m_ in g7.get("unlocated_minima") or []]
+        keep |= bool(row["unlocated_minima_lambda"])
+        s7c = [s for s in d.get("scan_07") or [] if s["index"] == c["index"]]
+        if s7c:
+            sc = s7c[0]
+            row["scanned"] = True
+            if "error" in sc:
+                row["scan_error"] = sc["error"][-300:]
+                keep = True
+            else:
+                full_s = (s7full.get(c["index"]) or {}).get("scan") if (sc["n_regions_listed"] or sc["n_sign_changes"]) else None
+                if sc["n_regions_listed"]:
+                    ra_ = regarr[c["index"]]
+                    row["listed"] = [{"region_index": x["region_index"], "how": x["how"], "branch": x["branch"],
+                                      "depth": x["depth"], "start": x["start"], "end": x["end"],
+                                      "window": int(ra_["window_07"][x["region_index"]]),
+                                      "guarded": int(ra_["guarded_07"][x["region_index"]]),
+                                      "guard_at_cap": int(ra_["guard_at_depth_max_07"][x["region_index"]])}
+                                     for x in full_s["listed"]]
+                    keep = True
+                row["scan"] = {"h": sc["h"]["hex"], "n_sign_changes": sc["n_sign_changes"],
+                               "min_over_max_abs_theta_deriv": sc.get("min_over_max_abs_theta_deriv"),
+                               "n_nonfinite": sc.get("n_nonfinite"),
+                               "sign_change_x": [] if not sc["n_sign_changes"] else list(full_s["sign_change_x"])}
+                keep |= bool(sc["n_sign_changes"])
+        else:
+            row["scanned"] = False
+        if keep:
+            out["calls"].append(row)
+    out["agg"] = agg
+    out["r07b"], out["depth20"] = r07b_unit(calls, rec, calls7)  # prompt 07b
+    out["unit_untouched"] = all((c.get("num_edge_rule_refusals") or 0) + (c.get("num_range_rule_refusals") or 0) == 0 for _, c in calls)  # prompt 07b
+    out["unit_untouched"] = all((c.get("num_chase_stops") or 0) == 0 for _, c in calls)  # prompt 07c: Y1's rule
+    out["r07c"] = r07c_unit(calls, rec, calls7)  # prompt 07c
+    out["n_calls"] = len(calls)
+    out["n_scanned"] = len(d.get("scan_07") or [])
+    out["n_scanned_by_kind"] = {"group": sum(1 for s in d.get("scan_07") or [] if s["kind"] == "group"),
+                                "analytic": sum(1 for s in d.get("scan_07") or [] if s["kind"] == "analytic")}
+    return out

+def main():
+    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
+    ap.add_argument("run_dir")
+    ap.add_argument("out_json")
+    ap.add_argument("--processes", type=int, default=6)
+    a = ap.parse_args()
+    if a.processes > 6:
+        raise SystemExit("at most six processes")
+    run = Path(a.run_dir).resolve()
+    ck = run / "checkpoint.jsonl"
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07c: Y2, Y3, Y5, Y9 and the descriptive items, gathered from 07c's census",  # prompt 07c
+                 [ck, INDEX07, ROWS])
+    idx = json.loads(INDEX07.read_text())
+    if idx["checkpoint_sha256"] != CHECKPOINT07_SHA256:
+        raise SystemExit("the index is not of the before's checkpoint")  # prompt 07b; prompt 07c: 07b's
+    rows = {r["name"]: r for r in json.loads(ROWS.read_text())["rows"]}
+    jobs, off = [], 0
+    with open(ck, "rb") as f:
+        for line in f:
+            uid = line[:120].decode()[len('{"unit": "'):].split('"', 1)[0]
+            jobs.append([uid, off, len(line)])
+            off += len(line)
+    sp_rows = set()  # the 720: rows whose 1e-7 G+q+r call shows a sign change in 07's census's 06a scan; prompt 07b: 07a's census's
+    with open(RUN07 / "checkpoint.jsonl") as f:
+        for line in f:
+            r = json.loads(line)
+            if r["data"]["rtol"] == "1e-7" and any(s and s["n_sign_changes"] > 0 for s in r["data"]["scan"] or []):
+                sp_rows.add(r["data"]["row"])
+    if len(sp_rows) != 720:
+        raise SystemExit(f"the 720: {len(sp_rows)} rows")
+    full = []
+    for uid, o, n in jobs:
+        p = uid.split("-")
+        if len(p) != 5 or p[0] != "h":
+            raise SystemExit(f"unexpected unit {uid}")
+        row = f"{p[3]}-{p[4]}"
+        full.append((uid, o, n, idx["units"][uid], rows[row], row in sp_rows, str(run)))  # prompt 07b: 07a's names
+    with mp.get_context("spawn").Pool(a.processes) as pool:
+        units = list(pool.imap(one, full, chunksize=16))
+    doc = {"header": hdr, "run": str(run), "checkpoint_sha256": sha256(ck), "n_units": len(units), "n_720": len(sp_rows), "units": units}
+    Path(a.out_json).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    Path(a.out_json).write_text(json.dumps(doc, default=str))
+    print(f"units {len(units)}; statuses {sorted({u['status'] for u in units})}; wrote {a.out_json} sha256 {sha256(a.out_json)}")
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07c section 6: Y4 (a), 07b's U4 set from 07b's Part A and 07a's two rows, and Y6 (06b's 24),
+scored from 07c's census on the same references by the same rule. Copied from 07b's census score_refs_07b.py (a record,
+not edited), changed only where marked "prompt 07c": the run given is 07c's census, and "07a" (the other code: the
+"scored" test's min(abserr) and the columns ``*_07a``) reads 07b's census (state/index_07b.json); each Part A record
+also carries the call's three chase counts. The references, the sets and the rule are 07b's, unchanged. 07b's text
+follows.
+
+levin-phase-floor prompt 07b section 6: U4 (Part A's referenced calls and 07a's two rows) and U6 (06b's 24), scored
+from 07b's census against their references, with 07a's census beside. New in 07b. Read only.
+
+**U4, Part A's calls** (state/predictions.md part 4): every id of Part A's state/census_wanted_07b.json whose ``which``
+is ``h`` and whose set is R1, R2, R3 (aux, own, W7, the pairs) or item 3, with ``~o`` (the other rtol's call on the same
+integrand) included. Each id's reference is Part A's: its ``refs()`` and ``ref_key()`` (Part A's
+scripts/score_07b.py, copied below unchanged in their logic, with the two committed inputs read from the tree h, whose
+SHA-256 equal Part A's), from Part A's readings (out/refs/readings/), 07's V4 readings and 07a's census reference of
+1-34599 (item 3). V4's 25 and 07a's other eight V4 calls are score_v4_07b.py's. **07a's two rows:** 07a's census
+references (var/levin-phase-floor-07a/census/out/refs/scores.json): 1-34599 and 3-678, group calls 2 and 3, both
+rtols. **The rule** (07's, as Part A and 07a's W4 applied it): a call is scored if its reference's stated error (the
+readings' spread) is at most min(abserr_07b, abserr_07a)/100; otherwise reported, not scored; honest iff
+|v - ref| <= abserr. A call unconverged at 07b counts under U4 as "unconverged under section 9's rule".
+
+**U6:** 06b's 24 (docs/levin-phase-floor/06b/census/out/w2_reference.json; unit b-1e-7-X-N is this census's
+h-1e-7-X-N, the group call of the same index), |v - S10|/abserr by 06b's rule: scored if |S9 - S10| <= abserr/100;
+honest iff |v - S10| <= abserr; or unconverged.
+
+Every comparison in mpmath at 40 digits (references as their 30-digit strings or hex). Writes OUT_JSON and prints the
+summary. Usage: python -B score_refs_07b.py RUN_DIR OUT_JSON
+"""

+def fx(h):
+    return float.fromhex(h) if isinstance(h, str) else h

+def refs():  # Part A's (scripts/score_07b.py), unchanged in its logic
+    out = {}
+    v4 = json.loads(V4S.read_text())
+    for c in v4["calls"]:
+        rd = [mpf(v) for v in c["readings_30"].values() if v is not None]

+        out[key] = {"ref": mpf(fx(r["S10"])), "spread": abs(fx(r["S9"]) - fx(r["S10"])), "basis": "S10 (06b)"}
+    for r in json.loads(I3R.read_text())["calls"]:
+        if r["sid"] == "r1-34599-1e-7-2":

+    sids = {p.name.split("-fccsplit")[0] for p in READ.glob("ref-*-fccsplit.json")} | {
+        p.name[: -len("-fcc-r2.json")] for p in READ.glob("ref-*-fcc-r2.json")}
+    for sid in sorted(sids):
+        rd = {}
+        fs = READ / f"{sid}-fccsplit.json"
+        if fs.exists():
+            d = json.loads(fs.read_text())
+            for res, r in d["readings"].items():
+                rd[f"fccsplit-r{res}"] = mp.fsum(mpf(x["value_30"]) for x in r["pieces"])
+        for impl in ("fcc", "qawo"):

+                    if d.get("value_30") is not None:
+                        rd[f"{impl}-r{res}"] = mpf(d["value_30"])
+        key = "fccsplit-r2" if "fccsplit-r2" in rd else ("fcc-r2" if "fcc-r2" in rd else None)
+        if key is None:
+            continue
+        vals = list(rd.values())
+        out[sid] = {"ref": rd[key], "spread": float(max(vals) - min(vals)), "basis": "+".join(sorted(rd)), "n": len(vals)}
+    return out
+
+
+def ref_key(sid):  # Part A's (scripts/score_07b.py), unchanged in its logic
+    if sid.startswith(("v", "cal_")):
+        return sid
+    if sid.startswith("W2-"):
+        return sid.split("~")[0]
+    if sid.startswith("i3-"):
+        return "i3"
+    if sid.startswith("p"):
+        return f"ref-R3p-3-{sid[1:].split('_')[0]}"
+    if sid.startswith("R1-"):
+        return "ref-" + sid.rsplit("-", 1)[0]
+    return "ref-" + sid.split("~")[0]
+
+
+def reader(ck, units):
+    """{unit: data} for the named units, read from a checkpoint by its unit-name prefix."""
+    out = {}
+    with open(ck) as f:
+        for line in f:
+            u = line[10:60].split('"', 1)[0]
+            if u in units:
+                out[u] = json.loads(line)["data"]
+    return out
+
+
+def call_of(d, kind, index):
+    key = "calls" if kind == "group" else "other_calls"
+    cs = [c for c in d.get(key) or [] if c["index"] == index]
+    if len(cs) != 1:
+        raise SystemExit(f"{d['unit']} {kind} #{index}: {len(cs)} calls")
+    return cs[0]
+
+
+def score(c, c7, ref, spread):
+    v, e, v7, e7 = fx(c["value"]["hex"]), fx(c["abserr"]["hex"]), fx(c7["value"]["hex"]), fx(c7["abserr"]["hex"])
+    r = {"value": c["value"]["hex"], "abserr": c["abserr"]["hex"], "converged": c["converged"], "regions": c["num_regions"],
+         "max_depth": c["max_depth"], "refusals": [c.get("num_edge_rule_refusals"), c.get("num_range_rule_refusals")],
+         "chase_counts": [c.get("num_chase_stops"), c.get("num_chase_stopped_leaves"), c.get("num_chase_noise_bisections")],  # prompt 07c
+         "value_07a": c7["value"]["hex"], "abserr_07a": c7["abserr"]["hex"], "converged_07a": c7["converged"],
+         "regions_07a": c7["num_regions"], "ref": mp.nstr(ref, 20), "spread": spread,
+         "ratio": float(abs(mpf(v) - ref) / e), "ratio_07a": float(abs(mpf(v7) - ref) / e7),
+         "scored": spread <= min(e, e7) / 100, "spread_over_abserr": spread / e}
+    r["verdict"] = ("reported, not scored" if not r["scored"] else "unconverged" if not c["converged"]
+                    else "honest" if r["ratio"] <= 1 else "converged and NOT honest")
+    r["verdict_07a"] = ("unconverged" if not c7["converged"] else "honest" if r["ratio_07a"] <= 1 else "converged and NOT honest")
+    return r
+
+
+def main():
+    run, out_p = Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2])
+    ck = run / "checkpoint.jsonl"
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07c: Y4 (a) (07b Part A's calls and 07a's two rows) and Y6, scored",
+                 [V4S, W2R, I3R, WANTED, CALLS, ck])
+    for n, s in PART_A_INPUT_SHA.items():
+        p = V4S if n == V4S.name else W2R
+        if sha256(p) != s:
+            raise SystemExit(f"{p} is not Part A's input")
+    idx = json.loads(INDEX07A.read_text())
+    if idx["checkpoint_sha256"] != CHECKPOINT07A_SHA256:
+        raise SystemExit("the index is not of 07a's checkpoint")
+    R = refs()
+    wanted = {k: v for k, v in json.loads(WANTED.read_text())["wanted"].items() if v["which"] == "h"}
+    sets = {k: v["set"] for k, v in json.loads(CALLS.read_text())["calls"].items()}
+    u4 = {k: v for k, v in wanted.items() if sets[k] in U4_SETS}
+    i3r = json.loads(I3R.read_text())["calls"]
+    w2 = json.loads(W2R.read_text())["results"]
+    units = {v["unit"] for v in u4.values()} | {c["unit"] for c in i3r} | {"h-" + r["unit"].split("-", 1)[1] for r in w2}
+    mine = reader(ck, units)
+
+    def theirs(u):
+        e = idx["units"][u]
+        with open(RUN07A / "checkpoint.jsonl", "rb") as f:
+            f.seek(e["offset"])
+            line = f.read(e["length"])
+        if hashlib.sha256(line).hexdigest() != e["line_sha256"]:
+            raise SystemExit(f"{u}: 07a's line sha256 differs")
+        return json.loads(line)["data"]
+
+    doc = {"header": hdr, "rule": __doc__, "u4_partA": {}, "u4_07a_rows": {}, "u6": {}}
+    for sid in sorted(u4):
+        w = u4[sid]
+        rk = ref_key(sid)
+        if rk not in R:
+            doc["u4_partA"][sid] = {"set": sets[sid], "unit": w["unit"], "verdict": "no reference", "ref_key": rk}
+            continue
+        c, c7 = call_of(mine[w["unit"]], w["kind"], w["index"]), call_of(theirs(w["unit"]), w["kind"], w["index"])
+        doc["u4_partA"][sid] = {"set": sets[sid], "unit": w["unit"], "kind": w["kind"], "index": w["index"], "ref_key": rk,
+                                "ref_basis": R[rk]["basis"], **score(c, c7, R[rk]["ref"], R[rk]["spread"])}
+    for r in i3r:
+        c, c7 = call_of(mine[r["unit"]], "group", r["index"]), call_of(theirs(r["unit"]), "group", r["index"])
+        doc["u4_07a_rows"][r["sid"]] = {"unit": r["unit"], "index": r["index"], "class_07a": r.get("class"),
+                                        **score(c, c7, mpf(r["ref_30"]), r["stated_error_spread"])}
+    for r in w2:
+        u = "h-" + r["unit"].split("-", 1)[1]
+        c, c7 = call_of(mine[u], "group", r["index"]), call_of(theirs(u), "group", r["index"])
+        v, e = fx(c["value"]["hex"]), fx(c["abserr"]["hex"])
+        S10, S9 = mpf(fx(r["S10"])), mpf(fx(r["S9"]))
+        sp = float(abs(S9 - S10))
+        x = {"unit": u, "index": r["index"], "band": r["band"], "k_key": r["k_key"], "value": c["value"]["hex"],
+             "abserr": c["abserr"]["hex"], "converged": c["converged"], "regions": c["num_regions"],
+             "refusals": [c.get("num_edge_rule_refusals"), c.get("num_range_rule_refusals")],
+             "ratio": float(abs(mpf(v) - S10) / e), "S9_S10_over_abserr": sp / e, "scored": sp <= e / 100,
+             "ratio_07a": float(abs(mpf(fx(c7["value"]["hex"])) - S10) / fx(c7["abserr"]["hex"])),
+             "converged_07a": c7["converged"], "ratio_06b": r["err_b_over_abserr_b"], "regions_07a": c7["num_regions"]}
+        x["verdict"] = ("reported, not scored" if not x["scored"] else "unconverged" if not c["converged"]
+                        else "honest" if x["ratio"] <= 1 else "converged and NOT honest")
+        doc["u6"][f"{r['unit']}#{r['index']}"] = x
+
+    def tally(rows):
+        t = {}
+        for x in rows:
+            t[x["verdict"]] = t.get(x["verdict"], 0) + 1
+        return t
+
+    pa, ar, u6 = list(doc["u4_partA"].values()), list(doc["u4_07a_rows"].values()), list(doc["u6"].values())
+    doc["summary"] = {
+        "u4_partA_by_set": {s: tally([x for x in pa if x["set"] == s]) for s in sorted({x["set"] for x in pa})},
+        "u4_partA": tally(pa), "u4_07a_rows": tally(ar),
+        "u4_partA_unconverged": sorted(k for k, x in doc["u4_partA"].items() if x["verdict"] == "unconverged"),
+        "u4_partA_dishonest_converged": sorted(k for k, x in doc["u4_partA"].items() if x["verdict"] == "converged and NOT honest"),
+        "u4_partA_not_scored": sorted(k for k, x in doc["u4_partA"].items() if x["verdict"] in ("reported, not scored", "no reference")),
+        "u4_partA_unconverged_ratios": {k: x["ratio"] for k, x in doc["u4_partA"].items() if x.get("verdict") == "unconverged"},
+        "u4_07a_rows_ratios": {k: [x["ratio"], x["ratio_07a"]] for k, x in doc["u4_07a_rows"].items()},
+        "u4_partA_max_ratio_scored_converged": max((x["ratio"] for x in pa if x.get("verdict") == "honest"), default=None),
+        "u4_partA_07a_dishonest_converged": sorted(k for k, x in doc["u4_partA"].items()
+                                                   if x.get("scored") and x.get("verdict_07a") == "converged and NOT honest"),
+        "u4_partA_distinct_census_calls": len({(x["unit"], x.get("kind"), x.get("index")) for x in pa}),
+        "u6": tally(u6), "u6_ratios": {k: [x["ratio"], x["ratio_07a"], x["ratio_06b"], x["converged"]] for k, x in doc["u6"].items()},
+        "u6_07a_dishonest": sorted(k for k, x in doc["u6"].items() if x["ratio_07a"] > 1 and x["converged_07a"]),
+    }
+    out_p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    out_p.write_text(json.dumps(doc, indent=1, default=str))
+    print(json.dumps(doc["summary"], indent=1, default=str))
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07c section 6, Y4 (b): 07c Part A's referenced census calls, R4 (60), R5 (60) and R2-28's census
+call at both rtols, scored from 07c's census against Part A's references (state/predictions.md part 5). New in 07c. Read
+only.
+
+- **The calls:** every entry of Part A's out/tables/scores_07c.json ``scored`` whose ``ref_key`` starts ``ref-R4-`` or
+  ``ref-R5-``, and ``c-1e-7-0-13757-5`` and ``c-1e-8-0-13757-5`` (``ref-R2-28``). Each id's census call (unit, kind,
+  index) is read from Part A's mappings (out/census_refs_07c.json ``calls``; state/census_wanted_07c*.json ``wanted``).
+- **The reference and its stated error** are Part A's (``ref``, a decimal string; ``spread``).
+- **The rule:** scored iff spread <= min(abserr)/100 over this census's abserr and 07b's census's for the call (and, for
+  R5, 07a's census's, read from 07b's census's stop-3 call list, out/stop3/compare_census_calls.calls.jsonl, its second
+  column); otherwise "reported, not scored". A scored call unconverged here is "unconverged"; otherwise honest iff
+  |v - ref| <= abserr, else "converged and NOT honest". Ratios are computed exactly (Fractions of the hex values and of the
+  reference's decimal string) and printed as floats.
+
+Writes OUT_JSON and prints a summary. Usage: python -B score_refs_partA_07c.py RUN_DIR OUT_JSON
+"""
+import json
+import sys
+from fractions import Fraction
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import MAIN, REC07B, RUN07B, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+PART_A = MAIN / "var" / "levin-phase-floor-07c" / "measurement"
+SCORES = PART_A / "out" / "tables" / "scores_07c.json"
+MAPS = [(PART_A / "out" / "census_refs_07c.json", "calls"), (PART_A / "state" / "census_wanted_07c.json", "wanted"),
+        (PART_A / "state" / "census_wanted_07c_chase.json", "wanted"), (PART_A / "state" / "census_wanted_07c_r5.json", "wanted")]
+CALLS07B = REC07B / "out" / "stop3" / "compare_census_calls.calls.jsonl"
+R2_28 = ("c-1e-7-0-13757-5", "c-1e-8-0-13757-5")
+
+
+def FX(h):
+    return Fraction(float.fromhex(h))
+
+
+def reader(ck, units):
+    out = {}
+    with open(ck) as f:
+        for line in f:
+            u = line[10:60].split('"', 1)[0]
+            if u in units:
+                out[u] = json.loads(line)["data"]
+    return out
+
+
+def call_of(d, kind, index):
+    cs = [c for c in d.get("calls" if kind == "group" else "other_calls") or [] if c["index"] == index]
+    if len(cs) != 1:
+        raise SystemExit(f"{d['unit']} {kind} #{index}: {len(cs)} calls")
+    return cs[0]
+
+
+def main():
+    run, out_p = Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2])
+    ck = run / "checkpoint.jsonl"
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07c: Y4 (b), Part A's R4, R5 and R2-28, scored from 07c's census",
+                 [SCORES] + [p for p, _ in MAPS] + [CALLS07B])
+    m = {}
+    for p, key in MAPS:
+        for k, v in json.loads(p.read_text())[key].items():
+            t = (v["unit"], v["kind"], v["index"])
+            if k in m and m[k] != t:
+                raise SystemExit(f"{k} mapped two ways")
+            m[k] = t
+    sc = json.loads(SCORES.read_text())["scored"]
+    want = {k: v for k, v in sc.items()
+            if (v.get("ref_key") or "").startswith(("ref-R4-", "ref-R5-")) or k in R2_28}
+    sets = {k: ("R2-28" if k in R2_28 else "R4" if v["ref_key"].startswith("ref-R4-") else "R5") for k, v in want.items()}
+    keys = {k: m[k] for k in want}
+    units = {t[0] for t in keys.values()}
+    mine = reader(ck, units)
+    theirs = reader(RUN07B / "checkpoint.jsonl", units)
+    a07 = {}
+    for line in open(CALLS07B):
+        c = json.loads(line)
+        t = (c["unit"], c["kind"], c["index"])
+        if t in set(keys.values()):
+            a07[t] = c["abserr"][1]
+    rows = {}
+    for sid in sorted(want):
+        t = keys[sid]
+        c, c7 = call_of(mine[t[0]], t[1], t[2]), call_of(theirs[t[0]], t[1], t[2])
+        ref = Fraction(want[sid]["ref"])
+        spread = Fraction(want[sid]["spread"])
+        v, e, v7, e7 = FX(c["value"]["hex"]), FX(c["abserr"]["hex"]), FX(c7["value"]["hex"]), FX(c7["abserr"]["hex"])
+        es = [e, e7] + ([FX(a07[t])] if sets[sid] == "R5" else [])
+        scored = spread <= min(es) / 100
+        ratio = abs(v - ref) / e
+        verdict = ("reported, not scored" if not scored else "unconverged" if not c["converged"]
+                   else "honest" if ratio <= 1 else "converged and NOT honest")
+        rows[sid] = {"set": sets[sid], "unit": t[0], "kind": t[1], "index": t[2], "ref_key": want[sid]["ref_key"],
+                     "ref": want[sid]["ref"], "spread": want[sid]["spread"], "value": c["value"]["hex"],
+                     "abserr": c["abserr"]["hex"], "converged": c["converged"], "regions": c["num_regions"],
+                     "max_depth": c["max_depth"], "chase_counts": [c.get("num_chase_stops"), c.get("num_chase_stopped_leaves"),
+                                                                    c.get("num_chase_noise_bisections")],
+                     "ratio": float(ratio), "ratio_07b": float(abs(v7 - ref) / e7), "converged_07b": c7["converged"],
+                     "abserr_07a": a07.get(t) if sets[sid] == "R5" else None, "scored": scored, "verdict": verdict,
+                     "partA_S_ratio": (want[sid]["by"].get("S") or {}).get("ratio"),
+                     "partA_S_verdict": (want[sid]["by"].get("S") or {}).get("verdict")}
+    tally = {}
+    for x in rows.values():
+        tally.setdefault(x["set"], {}).setdefault(x["verdict"], 0)
+        tally[x["set"]][x["verdict"]] += 1
+    bad = sorted(k for k, x in rows.items() if x["verdict"] == "converged and NOT honest")
+    doc = {"header": hdr, "rule": __doc__, "n": len(rows), "by_set": tally, "converged_dishonest": bad,
+           "converged_dishonest_ratios": {k: rows[k]["ratio"] for k in bad},
+           "converged_dishonest_other_than_r2_28": [k for k in bad if k not in R2_28],
+           "unconverged": {k: x["ratio"] for k, x in rows.items() if x["verdict"] == "unconverged"},
+           "not_scored": sorted(k for k, x in rows.items() if not x["scored"]),
+           "max_ratio_honest": max((x["ratio"] for x in rows.values() if x["verdict"] == "honest"), default=None),
+           "n_distinct_census_calls": len({(x["unit"], x["kind"], x["index"]) for x in rows.values()}),
+           "verdict_equals_partA_S": sum(1 for x in rows.values() if x["verdict"] == x["partA_S_verdict"]
+                                         or (x["verdict"] == "converged and NOT honest" and x["partA_S_verdict"] == "dishonest")),
+           "calls": rows}
+    out_p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    out_p.write_text(json.dumps(doc, indent=1))
+    print(json.dumps({k: v for k, v in doc.items() if k not in ("header", "rule", "calls")}, indent=1))
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07c section 6, Y4 (a)'s V4 part: V4's 33 calls re-scored from 07c's census against the readings
+07 scored them on, by 07's rule. Copied from 07b's census score_v4_07b.py (a record, not edited), changed only where
+marked "prompt 07c": "the new" (``_g``) is 07c's census (the run given), "the before" (``_06m``) is 07b's census (by
+state/index_07b.json, each line's SHA-256 checked); 07b's own U4 V4 scores are carried beside. The tolerance is
+min(abserr_07b, abserr_07c)/100. 07b's text follows.
+
+levin-phase-floor prompt 07b section 6, U4's V4 part: V4's 33 calls re-scored from 07b's census against the readings
+07 scored them on, by 07's rule. Copied from 07a's census score_v4_07a.py (a record, not edited), changed only where
+marked "prompt 07b": "the new" is 07b's census (the run given), "the before" is 07a's census (its checkpoint read by
+state/index_07a.json, each line's SHA-256 checked); 07a's own W4 scores (var/levin-phase-floor-07a/census/out/scores/
+w4_scores.json) are carried beside. The tolerance is min(abserr_07a, abserr_07b)/100. Keys keep 07's names: ``_g`` is
+07b, ``_06m`` is 07a. Usage: python -B score_v4_07b.py RUN_DIR OUT_JSON OUT_MD
+07a's text follows.
+
+levin-phase-floor prompt 07a section 6, W4: V4's 33 calls re-scored from 07a's census h against the readings 07
+scored them on, by 07's rule. Copied from 07's census score_v4_07.py (a record, not edited), changed only where marked
+"prompt 07a": "the new" is h's census (the run given), "the before" is 07's census (its checkpoint read by
+state/index_07.json, each line's SHA-256 checked); 07's V4 records are read from 07's working records
+(var/levin-phase-floor-07/census/: state/plan_references.json, state/v4_reading_list_20261109.json, state/v4_selection.json,
+out/v4/inputs.json, out/v4/readings/; read only); 06g's scores from the tree h. The tolerance is therefore
+min(abserr_07, abserr_h)/100 (state/predictions.md part 5), and each call also carries 07's own recorded verdict,
+tolerance and ratio (07's v4_scores.json) beside it. Keys keep 07's names: ``_g`` is h, ``_06m`` is 07.
+Usage: python -B score_v4_07a.py RUN_DIR OUT_JSON OUT_MD
+
+07's text follows.
+levin-phase-floor prompt 07 section 6, V4: score the 24 calls against their references, and z00, z12 and Z3's seven
+against 06g's, and give "the stationary-point calls before". New in 07. Read only.
+
+**The rule** (state/predictions.md, V4, as the user ruled at stop 4; the log's deviation 27):
+- the tolerance of a call is min(abserr_06m, abserr_g)/100, both the call's own census abserrs at its rtol;
+- a call with QAWO r1, r2 and FCC r1, r2 is scored as written: its readings agree when their spread (largest minus
+  smallest real part, ``value_30``) is at most the tolerance; the reference is FCC r2;
+- a call without QAWO is scored on the FCC readings it has: with both, the reference is FCC r2 and the two must agree
+  within the same tolerance; with only one, against that reading, with no agreement test;
+- a call whose readings do not agree is reported, not scored; a scored call is honest iff |v_g - ref| <= abserr_g.
+- **A reading is usable** if it is present, made by the tool at g (SHA-256 d37e633b...), on the capture listed in
+  out/v4/inputs.json (SHA-256 checked), Route B, with Ray not initialised, and every non-monotone segment of its own
+  table integrated directly (``direct``; the tool's own condition for a sound reading of a segment with a stationary
+  point). An unusable or missing reading is recorded, and the call is scored on the rest by the rule above.
+- **z00, z12 and Z3's seven** are scored on 06g's readings as scores_stage3/scores.json records them (``rows``: each
+  reading's ``value_30``; the readings 06g marked ``unusable`` left out; the reference its ``ref_30``, FCC r2), with this
+  rule's tolerance and agreement test, against 07's census value and abserr; 06m's from 06m's census.
+- **"The stationary-point calls before":** |v_06m - ref| / abserr_06m for every scored call.
+Every comparison is exact: doubles as Fractions from their hex, readings as Fractions of their 30-digit decimal strings.
+
+Usage: python -B score_v4_07.py OUT_JSON OUT_MD
+"""
+
+import hashlib
+import json
+import sys
+from fractions import Fraction
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+import refs_common as RC  # noqa: E402
+from prov import RUN06M, WIDER, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+from prov import CHECKPOINT07_SHA256, H_TREE, REC07, RUN07  # noqa: E402  prompt 07a
+from prov import CHECKPOINT07A_SHA256, REC07A, RUN07A  # noqa: E402  prompt 07b
+
+V4 = REC07 / "out" / "v4"  # prompt 07a: 07's records
+PLAN = REC07 / "state" / "plan_references.json"  # prompt 07a
+LIST = REC07 / "state" / "v4_reading_list_20261109.json"  # prompt 07a
+INPUTS = V4 / "inputs.json"
+SEL = REC07 / "state" / "v4_selection.json"  # prompt 07a
+SCORES06G = H_TREE / "docs" / "levin-phase-floor" / "06g" / "references" / "out" / "scores_stage3" / "scores.json"
+INDEX06M = WIDER / "state" / "index_07.json"  # prompt 07a: the before is 07's census
+V4_07 = REC07 / "out" / "v4" / "v4_scores.json"  # prompt 07a: 07's own scores, beside
+INDEX06M = WIDER / "state" / "index_07a.json"  # prompt 07b: the before is 07a's census (units h-...)
+V4_07 = REC07A / "out" / "scores" / "w4_scores.json"  # prompt 07b: 07a's own W4 scores, beside
+RUN07, CHECKPOINT07_SHA256 = RUN07A, CHECKPOINT07A_SHA256  # prompt 07b: the before's checkpoint
+from prov import CHECKPOINT07B_SHA256, REC07B, RUN07B  # noqa: E402  prompt 07c
+INDEX06M = WIDER / "state" / "index_07b.json"  # prompt 07c: the before is 07b's census (units h-...)
+V4_07 = REC07B / "out" / "scores" / "u4_v4_scores.json"  # prompt 07c: 07b's own U4 V4 scores, beside
+RUN07, CHECKPOINT07_SHA256 = RUN07B, CHECKPOINT07B_SHA256  # prompt 07c: the before's checkpoint
+TOOL_SHA256 = "d37e633bf54a179114b2462529eaaa3836cccff8fe22e0250e6d36d61992c7be"
+Z = ("z00", "z12", "z07", "z16", "z18", "z21", "z24", "z28", "z29")
+
+
+def FH(h):
+    return Fraction(float.fromhex(h))
+
+
+def FD(s):
+    return Fraction(s)
+
+
+def census_call(ck, off, kind, index):
+    with open(ck, "rb") as f:
+        f.seek(off[0])
+        line = f.read(off[1])
+    d = json.loads(line)["data"]
+    key = "calls" if kind == "group" else "other_calls"
+    c = [c for c in d[key] if c["index"] == index]
+    if len(c) != 1:
+        raise SystemExit(f"{d['unit']} #{index}: not one call")
+    return c[0]
+
+
+def offsets07(units, ck=None):  # prompt 07a: the run's checkpoint (h's), its units h-... keyed as 07's g-...
+    out, off = {}, 0
+    with open(ck, "rb") as f:
+        for line in f:
+            uid = line[10:60].decode().split('"', 1)[0]
+            gu = "g-" + uid.split("-", 1)[1]
+            if gu in units:
+                out[gu] = (off, len(line))
+            off += len(line)
+    return out
+
+
+def score(readings, kinds_ref, vg, eg, vm, em):
+    """readings: {kind: Fraction}; kinds_ref: the reference kind. Returns the verdict dict."""
+    tol = min(eg, em) / 100
+    vals = list(readings.values())
+    spread = max(vals) - min(vals) if len(vals) > 1 else None
+    if len(vals) == 0:
+        return {"verdict": "no usable reading", "tol": float(tol)}
+    if len(vals) > 1 and spread > tol:
+        return {"verdict": "readings disagree (reported, not scored)", "tol": float(tol), "spread": float(spread),
+                "spread_over_tol": float(spread / tol)}
+    ref = readings[kinds_ref] if kinds_ref in readings else vals[0]
+    dg, dm = abs(vg - ref), abs(vm - ref)
+    return {"verdict": "honest" if dg <= eg else "NOT honest", "tol": float(tol),
+            "spread": None if spread is None else float(spread), "spread_over_tol": None if spread is None else float(spread / tol),
+            "ref_kind": kinds_ref if kinds_ref in readings else list(readings)[0], "ref": float(ref),
+            "ratio_g": float(dg / eg), "ratio_06m": float(dm / em), "honest_06m": dm <= em}
+
+
+def main():
+    run_h, out_json, out_md = Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2]), Path(sys.argv[3])  # prompt 07a
+    CKH = run_h / "checkpoint.jsonl"  # prompt 07a
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07c: Y4 (a)'s V4 part (V4's 33 calls at 07c, on 07's readings)",  # prompt 07b
+                 [PLAN, LIST, INPUTS, SEL, SCORES06G, CKH, INDEX06M, V4_07])  # prompt 07a
+    if json.loads(INDEX06M.read_text())["checkpoint_sha256"] != CHECKPOINT07_SHA256 or sha256(RUN07 / "checkpoint.jsonl") != CHECKPOINT07_SHA256:
+        raise SystemExit("07a's census checkpoint has another SHA-256")  # prompt 07b
+    v4_07 = {x["sid"]: x for x in (lambda d: d["calls"] + d["z"])(json.loads(V4_07.read_text()))}  # prompt 07a
+    plan_jobs = {j["name"] for j in json.loads(PLAN.read_text())["stages"][0]["jobs"]}
+    inputs = json.loads(INPUTS.read_text())["inputs"]
+    sel = {c["sid"]: c for c in json.loads(SEL.read_text())["calls"]}
+    rows06g = {r["sid"]: r for r in json.loads(SCORES06G.read_text())["rows"]}
+    idx06 = json.loads(INDEX06M.read_text())["units"]
+    units = {c["unit"] for c in sel.values()} | {rows06g[z]["unit"] for z in Z}
+    off07 = offsets07(units, CKH)  # prompt 07a: h's offsets, by 07's unit names
+    res = {"header": hdr, "rule": __doc__, "calls": [], "z": []}
+    for sid in sorted(sel):
+        c = sel[sid]
+        g = census_call(CKH, off07[c["unit"]], "group", c["index"])  # prompt 07a: h
+        e6 = idx06["h-" + c["unit"].split("-", 1)[1]]  # prompt 07b: 07a's names
+        m = census_call(RUN07 / "checkpoint.jsonl", (e6["offset"], e6["length"]), "group", c["index"])  # prompt 07a: 07
+        vg, eg, vm, em = FH(g["value"]["hex"]), FH(g["abserr"]["hex"]), FH(m["value"]["hex"]), FH(m["abserr"]["hex"])
+        readings, notes = {}, []
+        for impl, r in (("qawo", 1), ("qawo", 2), ("fcc", 1), ("fcc", 2)):
+            name = f"{sid}-{impl}-r{r}"
+            if name not in plan_jobs:
+                continue
+            p = V4 / "readings" / f"{name}.json"
+            if not p.exists():
+                notes.append(f"{name}: missing")
+                continue
+            d = json.loads(p.read_text())
+            why = []
+            if d.get("tool_sha256") != TOOL_SHA256:
+                why.append("tool sha256")
+            if d.get("pickle_sha256") != inputs[sid]["sha256"] or sha256(d["pickle"]) != inputs[sid]["sha256"]:
+                why.append("pickle sha256")
+            if d.get("route") != "B":
+                why.append("route")
+            if any(bool(x) for x in (d.get("ray_initialized") or [])) if isinstance(d.get("ray_initialized"), list) else d.get("ray_initialized"):
+                why.append("ray initialised")
+            if any((not s.get("monotone")) and not s.get("direct") for s in d.get("segments") or []):
+                why.append("a non-monotone segment not integrated directly")
+            if d.get("value_30") is None:
+                why.append("no value")
+            if why:
+                notes.append(f"{name}: unusable ({', '.join(why)})")
+                continue
+            readings[f"{impl}_r{r}"] = FD(d["value_30"])
+        basis = ("four readings" if len(readings) == 4 else "FCC r1 and r2" if set(readings) == {"fcc_r1", "fcc_r2"}
+                 else "one FCC" if len(readings) == 1 and list(readings)[0].startswith("fcc") else f"other: {sorted(readings)}")
+        if basis.startswith("other"):
+            raise SystemExit(f"{sid}: a basis the rule does not decide: {sorted(readings)} ({notes})")
+        s = score(readings, "fcc_r2", vg, eg, vm, em)
+        res["calls"].append({"sid": sid, "unit": c["unit"], "rtol": c["rtol"], "index": c["index"], "band": c["band"],
+                             "k": c["k"], "basis": basis, "notes": notes, "value_g": g["value"]["hex"],
+                             "abserr_g": g["abserr"]["hex"], "value_06m": m["value"]["hex"], "abserr_06m": m["abserr"]["hex"],
+                             "converged_g": g["converged"], "converged_06m": m["converged"],
+                             "readings_30": {k: str(float(v)) for k, v in readings.items()},
+                             "wall_s": {f"{i}_r{r}": json.loads((V4 / "readings" / f"{sid}-{i}-r{r}.json").read_text())["wall_s"]
+                                        for i, r in (("qawo", 1), ("qawo", 2), ("fcc", 1), ("fcc", 2))
+                                        if (V4 / "readings" / f"{sid}-{i}-r{r}.json").exists()}, **s})
+    for z in Z:
+        r = rows06g[z]
+        kind = r["kind"]
+        g = census_call(CKH, off07[r["unit"]], kind, r["index"])  # prompt 07a: h
+        e6 = idx06["h-" + r["unit"].split("-", 1)[1]]  # prompt 07b: 07a's names
+        m = census_call(RUN07 / "checkpoint.jsonl", (e6["offset"], e6["length"]), kind, r["index"])  # prompt 07a: 07
+        if g["notify_label"] != r["label"] or m["notify_label"] != r["label"]:
+            raise SystemExit(f"{z}: label differs")
+        vg, eg, vm, em = FH(g["value"]["hex"]), FH(g["abserr"]["hex"]), FH(m["value"]["hex"]), FH(m["abserr"]["hex"])
+        unusable = set(r.get("unusable") or [])
+        readings = {k: FD(v["value_30"]) for k, v in r["readings"].items() if k not in unusable and v.get("value_30")}
+        if r.get("ref_30") is None or FD(r["ref_30"]) != readings.get("fcc_r2"):
+            raise SystemExit(f"{z}: 06g's ref_30 is not its FCC r2 reading")
+        s = score(readings, "fcc_r2", vg, eg, vm, em)
+        res["z"].append({"sid": z, "unit": r["unit"], "rtol": r["rtol"], "kind": kind, "index": r["index"], "label": r["label"],
+                         "basis": f"06g's readings: {sorted(readings)}", "unusable_06g": sorted(unusable),
+                         "value_g": g["value"]["hex"], "abserr_g": g["abserr"]["hex"], "value_06m": m["value"]["hex"],
+                         "abserr_06m": m["abserr"]["hex"], "converged_g": g["converged"], "converged_06m": m["converged"],
+                         "ratio_06g_record": r.get("r_g"), **s})
+    for x in res["calls"] + res["z"]:  # prompt 07a: 07's own score of the call, beside
+        o = v4_07[x["sid"]]
+        x["score_07"] = {k: o.get(k) for k in ("verdict", "tol", "ratio_g", "spread_over_tol", "value_g", "abserr_g", "converged_g")}
+        x["value_equal_07"] = x["value_g"] == o["value_g"]
+        x["abserr_equal_07"] = x["abserr_g"] == o["abserr_g"]
+    allc = res["calls"] + res["z"]
+    tot = {}
+    for x in allc:
+        tot[x["verdict"]] = tot.get(x["verdict"], 0) + 1
+    res["totals"] = {"24": {v: sum(1 for x in res["calls"] if x["verdict"] == v) for v in sorted({x["verdict"] for x in res["calls"]})},
+                     "z": {v: sum(1 for x in res["z"] if x["verdict"] == v) for v in sorted({x["verdict"] for x in res["z"]})},
+                     "all": tot,
+                     "basis_24": {b: sum(1 for x in res["calls"] if x["basis"] == b) for b in sorted({x["basis"] for x in res["calls"]})}}
+    scored = [x for x in allc if "ratio_g" in x]
+    res["V4"] = {"scored": len(scored), "honest": sum(1 for x in scored if x["verdict"] == "honest"),
+                 "not_honest": [x["sid"] for x in scored if x["verdict"] != "honest"],
+                 "reported_not_scored": [x["sid"] for x in allc if "ratio_g" not in x],
+                 "holds": all(x["verdict"] == "honest" for x in scored) and bool(scored)}
+    res["before"] = {"honest_06m": sum(1 for x in scored if x["honest_06m"]), "of": len(scored),
+                     "ratio_06m": {x["sid"]: x["ratio_06m"] for x in scored}}
+    out_json.write_text(json.dumps(res, indent=1, default=str))
+    L = [l for l in hdr] + ["", "# V4 scored", "",
+                            f"V4: {'HOLDS' if res['V4']['holds'] else 'FAILS'}: {res['V4']['honest']} of {res['V4']['scored']} scored "
+                            f"calls honest; not honest {res['V4']['not_honest']}; reported, not scored {res['V4']['reported_not_scored']}.",
+                            f"Totals: {json.dumps(res['totals'])}", "",
+                            "| call | unit (07's name) | rtol | basis | |v_h - ref|/abserr_h | |v_07 - ref|/abserr_07 | spread/tol | verdict | notes |",
+                            "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|"]
+    for x in allc:
+        L.append(f"| {x['sid']} | {x['unit']} | {x['rtol']} | {x['basis']} | {x.get('ratio_g', '-'):.4g} | {x.get('ratio_06m', '-'):.4g} | "
+                 f"{x.get('spread_over_tol') if x.get('spread_over_tol') is None else format(x['spread_over_tol'], '.3g')} | {x['verdict']} | "
+                 f"{'; '.join(x.get('notes') or [])} |" if "ratio_g" in x else
+                 f"| {x['sid']} | {x['unit']} | {x['rtol']} | {x['basis']} | - | - | {x.get('spread_over_tol')} | {x['verdict']} | "
+                 f"{'; '.join(x.get('notes') or [])} |")
+    L += ["", f"The before (07a's census; prompt 07b): honest on {res['before']['honest_06m']} of {res['before']['of']} scored calls.",  # prompt 07a
+          "", "Beside 07a's own W4 scores (prompt 07b): value and abserr at 07b equal 07a's (hex), 07a's verdict and ratio:", ""]
+    L += [f"- {x['sid']}: value equal {x['value_equal_07']}, abserr equal {x['abserr_equal_07']}; 07: {x['score_07']['verdict']}, "
+          f"{x['score_07']['ratio_g']:.4g}" for x in allc]
+    out_md.write_text("\n".join(L) + "\n")
+    print("\n".join(L[len(hdr):]))
+    print(f"wrote {out_json} sha256 {sha256(out_json)}; {out_md} sha256 {sha256(out_md)}")
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07c section 9.5, Y8 ("the code is (S)"), at planned stop 3. New in 07c, after 07b's
+score_u8_07b.py (a record, not edited), whose form it keeps. Read only.
+
+The calls (state/predictions.md part 4): every record Part A wrote of (S)'s replays,
+var/levin-phase-floor-07c/measurement/out/replays/S/<id>.json (454), less ``r31-3-20363-1`` (R31's record, a rehearsal
+row, not a census call): 453 ids. Each id's census call (unit, kind, index) is read from Part A's mappings:
+out/census_refs_07c.json ``calls``, and state/census_wanted_07c.json, census_wanted_07c_chase.json and
+census_wanted_07c_r5.json ``wanted`` (every id mapped, no id mapped two ways). The census call is read from this census's
+checkpoint (group calls in ``calls``, analytic in ``other_calls``, by ``index``). (S)'s replay record is the file's
+``replay``; it is checked to equal the same id's row of out/replays/S_summary_vplain.json. Each census call is compared
+with it in value and abserr (``float.hex``), ``converged``, ``num_regions`` and ``max_depth``. Y8 holds if all 453 are
+equal in all five. Where two ids name one census call, each is compared.
+
+Writes OUT_JSON (provenance, every call's two records and its verdict) and prints a summary. Exits 1 unless Y8 holds.
+Usage: python -B score_y8_07c.py RUN_DIR OUT_JSON
+"""
+
+import json
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import MAIN, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+PART_A = MAIN / "var" / "levin-phase-floor-07c" / "measurement"
+MAPS = [(PART_A / "out" / "census_refs_07c.json", "calls"), (PART_A / "state" / "census_wanted_07c.json", "wanted"),
+        (PART_A / "state" / "census_wanted_07c_chase.json", "wanted"), (PART_A / "state" / "census_wanted_07c_r5.json", "wanted")]
+REPLAYS = PART_A / "out" / "replays"
+SUMMARY = REPLAYS / "S_summary_vplain.json"
+EXCLUDED = ("r31-3-20363-1",)
+KEYS = ("value", "abserr", "converged", "num_regions", "max_depth")
+
+
+def hx(v):
+    return v.get("hex") if isinstance(v, dict) else v
+
+
+def main():
+    run_dir, out_p = Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2])
+    ck = run_dir / "checkpoint.jsonl"
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07c: Y8 at planned stop 3, the census against (S)'s replays",
+                 [p for p, _ in MAPS] + [SUMMARY, ck])
+    for l in hdr:
+        print(l, flush=True)
+    m = {}
+    for p, key in MAPS:
+        for k, v in json.loads(p.read_text())[key].items():
+            t = (v["unit"], v["kind"], v["index"])
+            if k in m and m[k] != t:
+                raise SystemExit(f"{k} mapped two ways: {m[k]} and {t}")
+            m[k] = t
+    ids = sorted(f.name[:-5] for f in (REPLAYS / "S").glob("*.json"))
+    wanted = [i for i in ids if i not in EXCLUDED]
+    unmapped = [i for i in wanted if i not in m]
+    if unmapped:
+        raise SystemExit(f"ids with no census call: {unmapped}")
+    srows = json.loads(SUMMARY.read_text())["rows"]
+    units = {m[i][0] for i in wanted}
+    recs = {}
+    with open(ck) as f:
+        for line in f:
+            u = line[10:60].split('"', 1)[0]  # {"unit": "<uid>", ...
+            if u in units:
+                r = json.loads(line)
+                if r["unit"] in recs:
+                    raise SystemExit(f"{r['unit']} twice in the checkpoint")
+                recs[r["unit"]] = r["data"]
+    rows = {}
+    n_eq = 0
+    for sid in wanted:
+        unit, kind, index = m[sid]
+        fp = REPLAYS / "S" / f"{sid}.json"
+        rp = json.loads(fp.read_text())
+        rep = rp["replay"]
+        rep_t = [rep.get(k) for k in KEYS]
+        summ_t = [srows.get(sid, {}).get("replay", {}).get(k) for k in KEYS]
+        d = recs.get(unit)
+        row = {"unit": unit, "kind": kind, "index": index, "set": rp.get("set"), "replay": dict(zip(KEYS, rep_t)),
+               "replay_module_sha256": (rp.get("header") or {}).get("module_sha256"),
+               "replay_file_sha256": sha256(fp), "replay_equals_summary": rep_t == summ_t}
+        if d is None or d["status"] != "done":
+            row.update(census=None, equal=False, why="unit missing or not done")
+        else:
+            key = "calls" if kind == "group" else "other_calls"
+            cs = [c for c in d.get(key) or [] if c["index"] == index]
+            if len(cs) != 1:
+                row.update(census=None, equal=False, why=f"{len(cs)} calls with that index")
+            else:
+                c = cs[0]
+                cen_t = [hx(c.get(k)) for k in KEYS]
+                row["census"] = dict(zip(KEYS, cen_t))
+                row["census_counts"] = [c.get("num_chase_stops"), c.get("num_chase_stopped_leaves"),
+                                        c.get("num_chase_noise_bisections")]
+                row["differing"] = [k for k, a, b in zip(KEYS, cen_t, rep_t) if a != b]
+                row["equal"] = not row["differing"] and row["replay_equals_summary"]
+        n_eq += bool(row["equal"])
+        rows[sid] = row
+    holds = n_eq == len(wanted) == 453
+    mods = sorted({str(r["replay_module_sha256"]) for r in rows.values()})
+    doc = {"header": hdr, "run_dir": str(run_dir), "n_ids": len(ids), "excluded": list(EXCLUDED), "n_calls": len(wanted),
+           "n_equal": n_eq, "replay_modules_sha256": mods,
+           "n_replay_equals_summary": sum(1 for r in rows.values() if r["replay_equals_summary"]),
+           "n_distinct_census_calls": len({(r["unit"], r["kind"], r["index"]) for r in rows.values()}),
+           "n_acted": sum(1 for r in rows.values() if (r.get("census_counts") or [0])[0]),
+           "y8_holds": holds, "failures": {k: r for k, r in rows.items() if not r["equal"]}, "calls": rows}
+    out_p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    out_p.write_text(json.dumps(doc, indent=1))
+    print(f"ids {len(ids)}; calls {len(wanted)} (excluded {list(EXCLUDED)}); distinct census calls "
+          f"{doc['n_distinct_census_calls']}; replay modules {mods}; replay record equals its summary row: "
+          f"{doc['n_replay_equals_summary']}; equal in all five: {n_eq}; the stop acted (census) in {doc['n_acted']}")
+    for k, r in list(doc["failures"].items())[:20]:
+        print(f"FAILS {k}: {r.get('why') or r.get('differing')} census {r.get('census')} replay {r['replay']}")
+    print(f"Y8: {'all equal' if holds else 'NOT all equal'}")
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")
+    return 0 if holds else 1
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())
