+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07c section 6, Gate Y0's third and fourth clauses: the units, chosen by a rule fixed before
+any is run (this docstring; new in 07c, after 07b's select_u0_units_07b.py, a record, not edited, whose form it keeps).
+Read only, except the two units files it writes.
+
+- **The unit where the fix acts** (clauses 3 and 4): row 0-33929 at 1e-8, ``g-1e-8-0-33929`` (run at h as
+  ``h-1e-8-0-33929``), fixed by name. Its group calls #2 and #3 are Part A's chase calls ``c-1e-8-0-33929-2`` and
+  ``c-1e-8-0-33929-3``: two of 07b's 198 newly unconverged calls, both made different from 07b's census by (S)'s replays
+  (07c Part A, out/replays/S_summary_vplain.json: ``reproduces_census`` false), so the stop is expected to act in them.
+  Chosen as the cheapest unit at 07b's census (6.3 s) holding two chase calls (S)'s replays touched. That the fix acts in
+  it at h (some call's num_chase_stops > 0) is read from the clause runs' own records (compare_07c.py prints each unit's
+  stops); if it does not act, the clause is not met and the agent stops.
+- **The unit where the fix does not act** (clause 3): from Gate Y0's K0 run at h (K0_H_DIR/units.jsonl), among the units
+  recorded ``done`` with at least one Levin call (group or analytic), those every one of whose Levin calls has
+  num_chase_stops 0; of these the one with the most Levin calls in which a bisection was counted against a root
+  (num_chase_noise_bisections > 0: the stop's count ran, and the instrument's pass-through with it, but no root reached
+  the limit), then the most Levin calls, then the most group calls, then the first in the order of 06k's K0 list
+  (docs/levin-phase-floor/06l/out/k0/units.json). If none, the script exits 1 and writes nothing (the agent stops and
+  asks).
+
+Writes state/y0c3_units.json (the two units) and state/y0c4_units.json (the unit where the fix acts), in run_k0.py's form
+({"units": [{"uid": "g-..."}]}, the K0 list's names), each with this rule's basis; prints the choice.
+
+Usage: python -B select_y0_units_07c.py K0_H_DIR
+"""
+
+import json
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import H_TREE, WIDER, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+K0_UNITS = H_TREE / "docs" / "levin-phase-floor" / "06l" / "out" / "k0" / "units.json"
+ACTS_UNIT = "g-1e-8-0-33929"
+
+
+def main():
+    k0_dir = Path(sys.argv[1])
+    src = k0_dir / "units.jsonl"
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07c: Gate Y0's clause 3 and 4 units", [K0_UNITS, src])
+    for l in hdr:
+        print(l)
+    order = [u["uid"] for u in json.loads(K0_UNITS.read_text())["units"]]
+    recs = {}
+    for line in open(src):
+        r = json.loads(line)
+        recs["g-" + r["unit"].split("-", 1)[1]] = r["data"]
+    cands = []
+    n_acted_units = 0
+    for pos, uid in enumerate(order):
+        if uid == ACTS_UNIT or uid not in recs or recs[uid]["status"] != "done":
+            continue
+        d = recs[uid]
+        cs = (d.get("calls") or []) + (d.get("other_calls") or [])
+        if not cs:
+            continue
+        counts = [(c.get("num_chase_stops"), c.get("num_chase_noise_bisections")) for c in cs]
+        if any(a is None or b is None for a, b in counts):
+            raise SystemExit(f"{uid}: a call has no counts")
+        if all(a == 0 for a, _b in counts):
+            n_counted = sum(1 for _a, b in counts if b > 0)
+            cands.append((-n_counted, -len(cs), -len(d.get("calls") or []), pos, uid))
+        else:
+            n_acted_units += 1
+    cands.sort()
+    print(f"K0 units at h with a Levin call: {len(cands) + n_acted_units}; the fix acts in {n_acted_units}; "
+          f"untouched in every call: {len(cands)}; with a counted bisection: {sum(1 for c in cands if c[0] < 0)}")
+    if not cands:
+        print("no K0 unit at h is untouched in every Levin call: nothing written")
+        return 1
+    quiet_unit = cands[0][4]
+    d = recs[quiet_unit]
+    basis = {"rule": __doc__.split("Writes ")[0].strip(), "header": hdr, "acts_unit": ACTS_UNIT, "quiet_unit": quiet_unit,
+             "quiet_unit_calls": [(c["index"], c["notify_label"], c.get("num_edge_rule_refusals"),
+                                   c.get("num_range_rule_refusals"), c.get("num_chase_stops"),
+                                   c.get("num_chase_stopped_leaves"), c.get("num_chase_noise_bisections"),
+                                   c.get("num_regions"))
+                                  for c in (d.get("calls") or []) + (d.get("other_calls") or [])],
+             "candidates": [c[4] for c in cands],
+             "acted_units": sorted(u for u in recs if any((c.get("num_chase_stops") or 0) > 0 for c in
+                                                         (recs[u].get("calls") or []) + (recs[u].get("other_calls") or [])))}
+    p3 = WIDER / "state" / "y0c3_units.json"
+    p4 = WIDER / "state" / "y0c4_units.json"
+    for p in (p3, p4):
+        if p.exists():
+            raise SystemExit(f"{p} exists; not overwritten")
+    p3.write_text(json.dumps({"basis": basis, "units": [{"uid": ACTS_UNIT}, {"uid": quiet_unit}]}, indent=1))
+    p4.write_text(json.dumps({"basis": basis, "units": [{"uid": ACTS_UNIT}]}, indent=1))
+    print(f"acts unit {ACTS_UNIT}; quiet unit {quiet_unit} ({len(basis['quiet_unit_calls'])} Levin calls, all stops 0, "
+          f"{-cands[0][0]} with a counted bisection)")
+    print(f"K0 units at h where the fix acts: {basis['acted_units']}")
+    print(f"wrote {p3} sha256 {sha256(p3)}")
+    print(f"wrote {p4} sha256 {sha256(p4)}")
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07c section 6, planned stop 3 (descriptive only; no verdict is scored here). New in 07c, after
+07b's stop3_cost_07b.py (a record, not edited). Read only.
+
+1. **Y1's counts** from compare_07c.py mode ``calls`` (CALLS_JSONL): calls untouched (num_chase_stops 0) and touched, by
+   rtol and kind (analytic or group), with how many of each equal 07b's in Y1's five outputs.
+2. **``converged`` against 07b's census**, by rtol and kind, in each direction. Every call newly unconverged (converged at
+   07b, unconverged here) is listed with the stopped leaf carrying the most of its abserr, from the stop's instrument
+   (``chase_07c``'s ``carrier``: its route, the region's type ``direct`` being the Clenshaw-Curtis pair, its trust before
+   the stop, depth, r, declared resolution, e' as returned, and share = e' / the call's abserr), or "no stopped leaf"
+   where the stop did not act (num_chase_stops 0) or stopped no leaf; with the call's abserr ratio against 07b's and
+   whether |v - v_07b| exceeds the sum of the two abserrs (exact Fractions from the hex values).
+3. **Cost:** the census's wall (manifest ``created`` to the last heartbeat) against the projection
+   (out/census_projection.json: central, central x 07b's shortfall, highest margin) and against 07b's census; unit walls
+   summed by rtol; regions by rtol and kind against 07b's.
+
+Writes OUT_JSON and OUT_MD (the list as a table) and prints a summary.
+Usage: python -B stop3_07c.py RUN_DIR CALLS_JSONL OUT_JSON OUT_MD
+"""
+import collections
+import datetime
+import json
+import sys
+from fractions import Fraction
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import RUN07B, WIDER, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+
+def F(h):
+    return Fraction(float.fromhex(h))
+
+
+def rp(v):
+    return v.get("repr") if isinstance(v, dict) else v
+
+
+def main():
+    run_dir, calls_p, out_p, md_p = map(Path, sys.argv[1:5])
+    proj_p = WIDER / "out" / "census_projection.json"
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07c: planned stop 3, descriptive (Y1's counts, converged "
+                 "against 07b, the newly unconverged calls, cost)", [calls_p, proj_p])
+    out = {"header": hdr}
+    y1 = collections.Counter()
+    conv = collections.Counter()
+    reg = collections.Counter()
+    newly, newly_conv = [], []
+    for line in open(calls_p):
+        c = json.loads(line)
+        k = (c["rtol"], c["kind"])
+        y1[k + (c["class"], "equal" if c["u1_equal"] else "differing")] += 1
+        a, b = c["converged"]
+        conv[k + (f"07b {b} -> 07c {a}",)] += 1
+        reg[k + ("07c",)] += c["num_regions"][0] or 0
+        reg[k + ("07b",)] += c["num_regions"][1] or 0
+        if b and not a:
+            v, v7 = F(c["value"][0]), F(c["value"][1])
+            e, e7 = F(c["abserr"][0]), F(c["abserr"][1])
+            ch = c.get("chase_07c") or {}
+            car = ch.get("carrier")
+            newly.append({
+                "unit": c["unit"], "rtol": c["rtol"], "kind": c["kind"], "index": c["index"], "label": c["label"],
+                "class": c["class"], "counts": [c["num_chase_stops"], c["num_chase_stopped_leaves"],
+                                                 c["num_chase_noise_bisections"]],
+                "rate_rule_refusals": [c["num_edge_rule_refusals"], c["num_range_rule_refusals"]],
+                "regions": c["num_regions"], "max_depth": c["max_depth"], "untrusted": c["untrusted"],
+                "abserr_ratio_07b": float(e / e7) if e7 else None,
+                "moved_past_sum": abs(v - v7) > e + e7, "move_over_sum": float(abs(v - v7) / (e + e7)) if e + e7 else None,
+                "n_stopped": ch.get("n_stopped"), "by_route": ch.get("by_route"), "share_sum": rp(ch.get("share_sum")),
+                "carrier": None if car is None else {k_: rp(v_) for k_, v_ in car.items()},
+            })
+        if a and not b:
+            newly_conv.append({"unit": c["unit"], "rtol": c["rtol"], "kind": c["kind"], "index": c["index"],
+                               "class": c["class"], "regions": c["num_regions"], "max_depth": c["max_depth"]})
+    out["y1_counts"] = {"|".join(k): v for k, v in sorted(y1.items())}
+    out["converged_changes"] = {"|".join(k): v for k, v in sorted(conv.items())}
+    out["newly_unconverged"] = newly
+    out["newly_unconverged_counts"] = {"|".join(k): v for k, v in sorted(collections.Counter(
+        (x["rtol"], x["kind"]) for x in newly).items())}
+    out["newly_unconverged_by_carrier_route"] = dict(collections.Counter(
+        ("no stopped leaf" if x["carrier"] is None else
+         f"{'Clenshaw-Curtis' if x['carrier']['type'] != 'Levin' else 'Levin'}"
+         f"{'' if x['carrier']['trusted_before'] is None else (' trusted' if x['carrier']['trusted_before'] else ' untrusted')}")
+        for x in newly))
+    out["newly_converged"] = newly_conv
+    out["newly_converged_counts"] = {"|".join(k): v for k, v in sorted(collections.Counter(
+        (x["rtol"], x["kind"]) for x in newly_conv).items())}
+    out["regions"] = {f"{a}|{b}": {"07c": reg[(a, b, "07c")], "07b": reg[(a, b, "07b")],
+                                   "ratio": reg[(a, b, "07c")] / reg[(a, b, "07b")]}
+                      for a, b in sorted({(k[0], k[1]) for k in reg})}
+    tot = (sum(v for k, v in reg.items() if k[2] == "07c"), sum(v for k, v in reg.items() if k[2] == "07b"))
+    out["regions_total"] = {"07c": tot[0], "07b": tot[1], "ratio": tot[0] / tot[1]}
+    for name, rd in (("07c", run_dir), ("07b", RUN07B)):
+        man = json.loads((rd / "manifest.json").read_text())
+        st = json.loads((rd / "status.json").read_text())
+        t0 = datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(man["created"]).timestamp()
+        t1 = datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(st["heartbeat"]).timestamp()
+        walls = collections.Counter()
+        n_ob = n = 0
+        mx = 0.0
+        for line in open(rd / "checkpoint.jsonl"):
+            d = json.loads(line)["data"]
+            n += 1
+            n_ob += d["status"] == "over budget"
+            walls[d["rtol"]] += d.get("wall_s") or 0.0
+            mx = max(mx, d.get("wall_s") or 0.0)
+        out[name] = {"created": man["created"], "last_heartbeat": st["heartbeat"], "wall_s": t1 - t0, "n_records": n,
+                     "n_over_budget": n_ob, "unit_wall_sum_s": dict(walls), "largest_unit_wall_s": mx}
+    pj = json.loads(proj_p.read_text())
+    w = out["07c"]["wall_s"]
+    out["wall_against_projection"] = {
+        "wall_s": w, "central_s": pj["projection_central_s"], "wall_over_central": w / pj["projection_central_s"],
+        "central_x_shortfall_s": pj["projection_central_x_07b_shortfall_s"],
+        "highest_s": pj["projection_highest_with_overbudget_s"], "wall_over_07b_census": w / out["07b"]["wall_s"]}
+    out_p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    out_p.write_text(json.dumps(out, indent=1))
+    L = ["# levin-phase-floor 07c, planned stop 3: calls newly unconverged against 07b's census", "",
+         *[f"<!-- {h[2:]} -->" for h in hdr], "",
+         f"{len(newly)} calls, by rtol and kind: {out['newly_unconverged_counts']}; by the carrying stopped leaf: "
+         f"{out['newly_unconverged_by_carrier_route']}. Share = the stopped leaf's e' as returned over the call's abserr.",
+         "",
+         "| call (unit, kind, index) | label | stops / stopped leaves / counted | regions 07c / 07b | depth 07c / 07b | abserr / 07b's | past the sum | carrying stopped leaf: route, trust before, depth, r, share | its declared resolution, delta1, residual |",
+         "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|"]
+    for x in sorted(newly, key=lambda x: (x["rtol"], x["kind"], x["unit"], x["index"])):
+        cr = x["carrier"]
+        if cr is None:
+            cs, cd = "no stopped leaf", ""
+        else:
+            route = "Clenshaw-Curtis" if cr["type"] != "Levin" else "Levin"
+            tb = {True: "trusted", False: "untrusted", None: "unchecked"}[cr["trusted_before"]]
+            cs = f"{route}, {tb}, depth {cr['depth']}, r {float(cr['r']):.3g}, share {float(cr['share']):.3g}"
+            cd = f"{float(cr['declared_resolution']):.3g}, {float(cr['delta1']):.3g}, {float(cr['residual']):.3g}" \
+                if cr['declared_resolution'] is not None else f"(none), {cr['delta1']}, {cr['residual']}"
+        L.append(f"| {x['unit']} {x['kind']} #{x['index']} | {(x['label'] or '').split(', group ')[-1][:20]} | "
+                 f"{'/'.join(map(str, x['counts']))} | {x['regions'][0]} / {x['regions'][1]} | "
+                 f"{x['max_depth'][0]} / {x['max_depth'][1]} | {x['abserr_ratio_07b']:.4g} | {x['moved_past_sum']} | {cs} | {cd} |")
+    md_p.write_text("\n".join(L) + "\n")
+    print(json.dumps({k: v for k, v in out.items() if k not in ("header", "newly_unconverged", "newly_converged")},
+                     indent=1))
+    print(f"newly unconverged: {len(newly)}; newly converged: {len(newly_conv)}")
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")
+    print(f"wrote {md_p} sha256 {sha256(md_p)}")
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07c section 6: Y1-Y9 gathered and scored against state/predictions.md (part 5's readings),
+and the descriptive tables. New in 07c, after 07b's summarise_07b.py (a record, not edited), whose U2, U3 and U5 rules it
+keeps for Y2, Y3 and Y5 (Y5 on gated solves only, 07a's ruling at its W5 stop). Read only.
+
+Inputs: state/score_07c_units.json (score_07c.py); out/stop3/compare_census_calls.json and .calls.jsonl (compare_07c.py:
+Y1, the classes, convergence, abserr, regions); out/stop3/y8.json (score_y8_07c.py); out/scores/y4_v4_scores.json
+(score_v4_07c.py), out/scores/y4a_y6_scores.json (score_refs_07c.py), out/scores/y4b_scores.json
+(score_refs_partA_07c.py); out/xcheck/y7_vs_07b_pop07/xcheck.json (xcheck_07c.py); out/stop3/stop3.json (cost);
+state/rows.json (band, k); 07b's census's stop-3 call list (var/levin-phase-floor-07b/census/out/stop3/
+compare_census_calls.calls.jsonl: 07b's class of each call by the rate rules, and 07a's value, abserr and converged, its
+second column) and 07b's census summary (out/scores/summary.json: the 198 newly unconverged calls, Y9 (ii)). Floats
+compared exactly (Fraction of their hex).
+
+Usage: python -B summarise_07c.py OUT_JSON
+"""
+
+import json
+import sys
+from collections import Counter, defaultdict
+from fractions import Fraction
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import REC07B, WIDER, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+UNITS = WIDER / "state" / "score_07c_units.json"
+CMP = WIDER / "out" / "stop3" / "compare_census_calls.json"
+CALLS = WIDER / "out" / "stop3" / "compare_census_calls.calls.jsonl"
+Y8 = WIDER / "out" / "stop3" / "y8.json"
+Y4V4 = WIDER / "out" / "scores" / "y4_v4_scores.json"
+Y4A = WIDER / "out" / "scores" / "y4a_y6_scores.json"
+Y4B = WIDER / "out" / "scores" / "y4b_scores.json"
+Y7 = WIDER / "out" / "xcheck" / "y7_vs_07b_pop07" / "xcheck.json"
+COST = WIDER / "out" / "stop3" / "stop3.json"
+ROWS = WIDER / "state" / "rows.json"
+CALLS07B = REC07B / "out" / "stop3" / "compare_census_calls.calls.jsonl"
+SUMM07B = REC07B / "out" / "scores" / "summary.json"
+R2_28 = {"R2-28", "R2-28~o", "c-1e-7-0-13757-5", "c-1e-8-0-13757-5"}
+FIVE = {"c-1e-8-1-2229-4", "c-1e-8-3-13490-2", "c-1e-8-3-13490-3", "c-1e-8-3-13615-2", "c-1e-8-3-13615-3"}
+
+
+def F(h):
+    return Fraction(float.fromhex(h))
+
+
+def q(xs):
+    xs = sorted(x for x in xs if x is not None)
+    if not xs:
+        return None
+    return {"n": len(xs), "min": xs[0], "p01": xs[len(xs) // 100], "p10": xs[len(xs) // 10], "median": xs[len(xs) // 2],
+            "p90": xs[9 * len(xs) // 10], "p99": xs[min(len(xs) - 1, 99 * len(xs) // 100)], "max": xs[-1]}
+
+
+def sid_of(unit, kind, index):
+    p = unit.split("-")  # h-1e-8-1-2229
+    return f"c-{p[1]}-{p[2]}-{p[3]}-{p[4]}-{index}" if kind == "group" else f"a-{p[1]}-{p[2]}-{p[3]}-{p[4]}-{index}"
+
+
+def main():
+    out_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07c: Y1-Y9 and the descriptive tables",
+                 [UNITS, CMP, CALLS, Y8, Y4V4, Y4A, Y4B, Y7, COST, ROWS, CALLS07B, SUMM07B])
+    U = json.loads(UNITS.read_text())["units"]
+    cmpd = json.loads(CMP.read_text())
+    rows = {r["name"]: r for r in json.loads(ROWS.read_text())["rows"]}
+    calls = [json.loads(l) for l in open(CALLS)]
+    keyset = {(c["unit"], c["kind"], c["index"]) for c in calls}
+    b7 = {}
+    for l in open(CALLS07B):
+        c = json.loads(l)
+        k = (c["unit"], c["kind"], c["index"])
+        if k in keyset:
+            b7[k] = {"class_07b": c["class"], "value_07a": c["value"][1], "abserr_07a": c["abserr"][1],
+                     "converged_07a": c["converged"][1]}
+    uinfo = {u["unit"]: u for u in U}
+    S = {"header": hdr, "statuses": dict(Counter(u["status"] for u in U)), "n_calls": len(calls),
+         "route_errors": sum(u["agg"]["route_errors"] for u in U if "agg" in u),
+         "scan_07_errors": sum(u.get("scan_07_error") or 0 for u in U), "n_calls_matched_07b_list": len(b7)}
+    # ---------------- Y1
+    y1 = {}
+    for rt in ("1e-7", "1e-8"):
+        cs = [c for c in calls if c["rtol"] == rt and c["class"] == "untouched"]
+        us = [u for u in cmpd["units"] if u["rtol"] == rt and (u.get("all_w1") or {}).get("A")]
+        y1[rt] = {"untouched_calls": len(cs), "by_kind": dict(Counter(c["kind"] for c in cs)),
+                  "touched_calls": sum(1 for c in calls if c["rtol"] == rt and c["class"] == "acted"),
+                  "five_equal": sum(1 for c in cs if c["u1_equal"]), "five_differing": [
+                      {k: c[k] for k in ("unit", "kind", "index", "u1_differing")} for c in cs if not c["u1_equal"]][:50],
+                  "whole_record_equal": sum(1 for c in cs if c["equal"]),
+                  "rows_all_untouched": len(us), "rows_totals_equal": sum(1 for u in us if u["totals_equal"]),
+                  "rows_whole_categories": dict(Counter("+".join((u.get("whole") or {}).get("categories") or []) or "none" for u in us))}
+    S["Y1"] = {"by_rtol": y1, "no_counts_calls": sum(1 for c in calls if c["class"] == "no_counts"),
+               "holds": all(y1[rt]["five_equal"] == y1[rt]["untouched_calls"] and y1[rt]["rows_totals_equal"] == y1[rt]["rows_all_untouched"]
+                            for rt in y1) and not any(c["class"] == "no_counts" for c in calls),
+               "fraction_untouched": sum(y1[rt]["untouched_calls"] for rt in y1) / len(calls)}
+    # ---------------- Y2, Y3, Y5 (07b's U2, U3, U5 extraction, unchanged)
+    V2_levin, listed_other = [], Counter()
+    V3_viol, V3_set_aside, v3_scored = [], [], 0
+    w5 = Counter()
+    w5_calls = {"partition_nonforced": [], "cc_lh": [], "guarded": []}
+    unconv = defaultdict(Counter)
+    for u in U:
+        a = u.get("agg")
+        if a is None:
+            continue
+        w5["partition_nonforced"] += a["partition_nonforced"]
+        w5["partition_forced"] += a["partition_forced"]
+        w5["cc_with_levin_half"] += a["cc_lh"]
+        w5["num_guarded_regions"] += a["guarded"]
+        w5["num_guard_at_depth_max"] += a["guard_at_depth_max"]
+        for c in u.get("calls") or []:
+            key = {"unit": u["unit"], "rtol": u["rtol"], "kind": c["kind"], "index": c["index"], "band": u["band"], "k": u["k"]}
+            r = c.get("route") or {}
+            if (r.get("partition") or {}).get("nonforced") and sum((r.get("partition") or {}).get("nonforced", {}).values()):
+                w5_calls["partition_nonforced"].append(dict(key, partition=r.get("partition")))
+            if r.get("n_cc_with_levin_half"):
+                w5_calls["cc_lh"].append(dict(key, n=r["n_cc_with_levin_half"]))
+            if c.get("num_guarded_regions"):
+                w5_calls["guarded"].append(dict(key, n=c["num_guarded_regions"]))
+            for x in c.get("listed") or []:
+                cl = ("Levin" if x["branch"] == "Levin" else "window" if x["window"] == 1 else
+                      "guard_at_cap" if x["guard_at_cap"] == 1 else "CC")
+                if cl == "Levin":
+                    V2_levin.append(dict(key, **x))
+                listed_other[(u["rtol"], c["kind"], cl)] += 1
+            if c.get("scanned") and "scan" in c:
+                sc = c["scan"]
+                if c["skipped"][0]:
+                    V3_set_aside.append(dict(key, skipped=c["skipped"][0], n_sign_changes=sc["n_sign_changes"]))
+                else:
+                    v3_scored += 1
+                    h = float.fromhex(sc["h"])
+                    S_x = sc["sign_change_x"]
+                    roots = [p["x"] for p in c.get("points") or [] if p["kind"] == "root"]
+                    mc = [x for x in S_x if not any(abs(rr - x) <= h for rr in roots)]
+                    mr = [rr for rr in roots if not any(abs(rr - x) <= h for x in S_x)]
+                    if mc or mr:
+                        V3_viol.append(dict(key, changes_without_root=mc[:8], roots_without_change=mr[:8]))
+            for j, code in ((0, "07c"), (1, "07b")):
+                if c["converged"][j] is False:
+                    unconv[code][(u["rtol"], c["kind"], "720" if u["in720"] else "outside")] += 1
+    S["Y2"] = {"n_levin_leaves_listed": len(V2_levin), "levin_leaves": V2_levin[:100],
+               "listed_by_rtol_kind_class": {"|".join(k): v for k, v in sorted(listed_other.items())}, "holds": len(V2_levin) == 0}
+    bad_skip = [x for x in V3_set_aside if any(s.get("reason") != "zero_rate" for s in x["skipped"]) or x["n_sign_changes"]]
+    S["Y3"] = {"n_calls_scored": v3_scored, "n_violating": len(V3_viol), "violations": V3_viol[:100], "n_set_aside": len(V3_set_aside),
+               "set_aside_by": dict(Counter(f"{x['rtol']}|{x['kind']}|{','.join(s.get('reason') or 'NO REASON' for s in x['skipped'])}|"
+                                            f"sign_changes={x['n_sign_changes']}" for x in V3_set_aside)),
+               "set_aside_not_zero_rate_or_with_sign_change": bad_skip[:50], "holds": len(V3_viol) == 0 and not bad_skip}
+    S["Y5"] = {"counts": dict(w5), "n_calls": {k: len(v) for k, v in w5_calls.items()}, "calls": {k: v[:50] for k, v in w5_calls.items()},
+               "holds_gated": w5["cc_with_levin_half"] == 0 and w5["partition_nonforced"] == 0 and w5["num_guarded_regions"] == 0,
+               "forced_partition_descriptive": w5["partition_forced"]}
+    # ---------------- Y4
+    v4 = json.loads(Y4V4.read_text())
+    ra = json.loads(Y4A.read_text())
+    rb = json.loads(Y4B.read_text())
+    y4rows = ([{"sid": x["sid"], "set": "(a) V4", "verdict": ("unconverged" if not x["converged_g"] else x["verdict"]),
+                "ratio": x.get("ratio_g"), "converged": x["converged_g"]} for x in v4["calls"] + v4["z"]]
+              + [{"sid": k, "set": "(a) " + x["set"], "verdict": x["verdict"], "ratio": x.get("ratio"), "converged": x.get("converged")}
+                 for k, x in ra["u4_partA"].items()]
+              + [{"sid": k, "set": "(a) 07a rows", "verdict": x["verdict"], "ratio": x["ratio"], "converged": x["converged"]}
+                 for k, x in ra["u4_07a_rows"].items()]
+              + [{"sid": k, "set": "(b) " + x["set"], "verdict": x["verdict"], "ratio": x["ratio"], "converged": x["converged"]}
+                 for k, x in rb["calls"].items()])
+    bad = [x for x in y4rows if x["verdict"] in ("NOT honest", "converged and NOT honest")]
+    S["Y4"] = {"n_scorings": len(y4rows), "n_a": sum(1 for x in y4rows if x["set"].startswith("(a)")),
+               "n_b": sum(1 for x in y4rows if x["set"].startswith("(b)")),
+               "by_verdict": dict(Counter(x["verdict"] for x in y4rows)),
+               "by_set_verdict": dict(Counter(f"{x['set']}|{x['verdict']}" for x in y4rows)),
+               "converged_dishonest": bad, "converged_dishonest_other_than_r2_28": [x for x in bad if x["sid"] not in R2_28],
+               "unconverged": {x["sid"]: x["ratio"] for x in y4rows if x["verdict"] == "unconverged"},
+               "not_scored": sorted(x["sid"] for x in y4rows if x["verdict"] in ("reported, not scored", "no reference")),
+               "max_ratio_honest": max((x["ratio"] for x in y4rows if x["verdict"] == "honest"), default=None),
+               "r2_28": {x["sid"]: x["ratio"] for x in y4rows if x["sid"] in R2_28},
+               "holds": not [x for x in bad if x["sid"] not in R2_28]}
+    # ---------------- Y6, Y7, Y8
+    S["Y6"] = {"by_verdict": ra["summary"]["u6"], "ratios": {k: x["ratio"] for k, x in ra["u6"].items()},
+               "ratio_min_max": [min(x["ratio"] for x in ra["u6"].values()), max(x["ratio"] for x in ra["u6"].values())],
+               "n_converged": sum(1 for x in ra["u6"].values() if x["converged"]),
+               "holds": all(v["verdict"] in ("honest", "unconverged") for v in ra["u6"].values()) and len(ra["u6"]) == 24}
+    x7 = json.loads(Y7.read_text())
+    pairs = x7["pairs"]
+    gt = [p for p in pairs if not p["g_self"]]
+    S["Y7"] = {"pairs": len(pairs), "unpaired": len(x7["A"]["unpaired"]), "r_g_le_1": x7["A"]["overall"]["r_g<=1"],
+               "r_g_gt_1": [{k: p[k] for k in ("row", "kind", "index", "r_g", "converged")} for p in gt],
+               "r_g_gt_1_without_unconverged_member": [p["row"] for p in gt if p["converged"]["g7"] and p["converged"]["g8"]],
+               "max_r_g": max(p["r_g"] for p in pairs if p["r_g"] is not None), "r_g_07b_le_1": x7["A"]["overall"]["r_m<=1"],
+               "the_two_pairs": {p["row"]: {"r_g": p["r_g"], "r_g_07b": p["r_m"]} for p in pairs
+                                 if p["row"] in ("3-14501", "3-35006") and p["kind"] == "group" and p["index"] == 1},
+               "pairs_with_unconverged_member": sum(1 for p in pairs if not (p["converged"]["g7"] and p["converged"]["g8"])),
+               "checkpoints_unchanged": x7["checkpoints_unchanged"]}
+    S["Y7"]["holds"] = (len(S["Y7"]["r_g_gt_1_without_unconverged_member"]) == 0 and S["Y7"]["unpaired"] == 0
+                        and S["Y7"]["pairs"] == 2484)
+    y8 = json.loads(Y8.read_text())
+    S["Y8"] = {"n": y8["n_calls"], "n_equal": y8["n_equal"], "n_distinct": y8["n_distinct_census_calls"],
+               "n_acted": y8["n_acted"], "holds": y8["y8_holds"]}
+    # ---------------- Y9
+    d20 = {(u["unit"], x["kind"], x["index"]): x for u in U for x in u.get("depth20") or []}
+    cmap = {(c["unit"], c["kind"], c["index"]): c for c in calls}
+    unconv_calls = [c for c in calls if c["converged"][0] is False]
+    chase = []
+    no_d20 = []
+    for c in unconv_calls:
+        x = d20.get((c["unit"], c["kind"], c["index"]))
+        if x is None:
+            raise SystemExit(f"no depth-20 record for unconverged call {c['unit']} {c['kind']} #{c['index']}")
+        if x["n_depth20"] > 0 and x["descends_from_refused"] == x["n_depth20"]:
+            chase.append({k: x[k] for k in ("kind", "index", "label", "n_depth20", "routes", "refused_ancestor_depths")} | {"unit": c["unit"]})
+        if x["n_depth20"] == 0:
+            no_d20.append(c)
+    s7b = json.loads(SUMM07B.read_text())["newly_unconverged"]["list"]
+    still = []
+    for e in s7b:
+        c = cmap[(e["unit"], e["kind"], e["index"])]
+        if c["converged"][0] is False:
+            still.append({"sid": sid_of(e["unit"], e["kind"], e["index"]), "unit": e["unit"], "kind": e["kind"],
+                          "index": e["index"], "class": c["class"], "u1_equal": c["u1_equal"],
+                          "num_chase_stops": c["num_chase_stops"],
+                          "n_depth20": (d20.get((e["unit"], e["kind"], e["index"])) or {}).get("n_depth20")})
+    ii = ({x["sid"] for x in still} == FIVE and len(still) == 5
+          and all(x["num_chase_stops"] == 0 and x["class"] == "untouched" and x["u1_equal"] for x in still))
+    S["Y9"] = {"i_chase_calls": chase, "i_n_chase_calls": len(chase), "i_holds": len(chase) == 0,
+               "unconverged_calls": len(unconv_calls),
+               "unconverged_by_rtol_kind": dict(Counter(f"{c['rtol']}|{c['kind']}" for c in unconv_calls)),
+               "unconverged_with_no_depth20_leaf": len(no_d20),
+               "unconverged_with_depth20_leaf_not_all_below_refused": len(unconv_calls) - len(chase) - len(no_d20),
+               "ii_n_07b_newly_unconverged": len(s7b), "ii_still_unconverged": still, "ii_holds": ii,
+               "holds": len(chase) == 0 and ii}
+    # ---------------- descriptive: where the fix acts
+    def cinfo(c):
+        p = c["unit"].split("-")
+        R = rows[f"{p[3]}-{p[4]}"]
+        return R["band"], R["k"], (b7.get((c["unit"], c["kind"], c["index"])) or {}).get("class_07b", "?"), uinfo[c["unit"]]["in720"]
+    act = Counter()
+    for c in calls:
+        band, k, cb, in720 = cinfo(c)
+        ns, nl, nb = c["num_chase_stops"] or 0, c["num_chase_stopped_leaves"] or 0, c["num_chase_noise_bisections"] or 0
+        for dim, val in (("rtol|kind", f"{c['rtol']}|{c['kind']}"), ("band", band), ("k", k), ("class_07b", cb),
+                         ("window_07c", c["winh"]), ("in720", in720), ("rtol|kind|band", f"{c['rtol']}|{c['kind']}|{band}"),
+                         ("rtol|kind|k", f"{c['rtol']}|{c['kind']}|{k}")):
+            act[(dim, str(val), "calls")] += 1
+            act[(dim, str(val), "acted")] += ns > 0
+            act[(dim, str(val), "counted_calls")] += nb > 0
+            act[(dim, str(val), "stops")] += ns
+            act[(dim, str(val), "stopped_leaves")] += nl
+            act[(dim, str(val), "noise_bisections")] += nb
+    tab = defaultdict(dict)
+    for (dim, val, what), n in act.items():
+        tab[dim].setdefault(val, {})[what] = n
+    S["where_the_fix_acts"] = tab
+    S["fix_totals"] = {"calls_acted": sum(1 for c in calls if (c["num_chase_stops"] or 0) > 0),
+                       "stops": sum(c["num_chase_stops"] or 0 for c in calls),
+                       "stopped_leaves": sum(c["num_chase_stopped_leaves"] or 0 for c in calls),
+                       "noise_bisections": sum(c["num_chase_noise_bisections"] or 0 for c in calls),
+                       "calls_with_a_counted_bisection": sum(1 for c in calls if (c["num_chase_noise_bisections"] or 0) > 0)}
+    lv, sb, rbn = Counter(), Counter(), Counter()
+    n_uneq = n_err = n_nr = 0
+    mx = None
+    for u in U:
+        r7 = u.get("r07c") or {}
+        for nm, C in (("leaves", lv), ("share_bins", sb), ("r_bins", rbn)):
+            for k_, v_ in (r7.get(nm) or {}).items():
+                C[f"{u['rtol']}|{k_}"] += v_
+        n_uneq += r7.get("n_counts_unequal") or 0
+        n_err += r7.get("n_instrument_error") or 0
+        n_nr += r7.get("n_not_returned") or 0
+        if r7.get("max_share") and (mx is None or r7["max_share"][0] > mx[0]):
+            mx = r7["max_share"] + [u["unit"]]
+    by_route = Counter()
+    by_depth = Counter()
+    for k_, v_ in lv.items():
+        rt, kind, route, trust, dep = k_.split("|")
+        by_route[f"{rt}|{kind}|{route}|{trust}"] += v_
+        by_depth[f"{route}|{dep}"] += v_
+    S["stopped_leaves"] = {"by_rtol_kind_route_trust_depth": dict(lv), "by_rtol_kind_route_trust": dict(by_route),
+                           "by_route_depth": dict(sorted(by_depth.items(), key=lambda kv: (kv[0].split("|")[0], int(kv[0].split("|")[1])))),
+                           "share_bins": dict(sb), "r_bins": dict(rbn), "largest_share": mx,
+                           "instrument_counts_unequal": n_uneq, "instrument_errors": n_err, "not_returned": n_nr,
+                           "total": sum(lv.values())}
+    # ---------------- descriptive: convergence against 07b and 07a
+    conv = Counter((c["rtol"], c["kind"], str(c["converged"][1]), str(c["converged"][0])) for c in calls)
+    S["converged_07b_to_07c"] = {"|".join(k): v for k, v in sorted(conv.items())}
+    conv_a = Counter((c["rtol"], c["kind"], str((b7.get((c["unit"], c["kind"], c["index"])) or {}).get("converged_07a")),
+                      str(c["converged"][0])) for c in calls)
+    S["converged_07a_to_07c"] = {"|".join(k): v for k, v in sorted(conv_a.items())}
+    newly = [c for c in calls if c["converged"][1] is True and c["converged"][0] is False]
+    S["newly_unconverged_vs_07b"] = {"n": len(newly), "list": [
+        {k: c[k] for k in ("unit", "kind", "index", "label", "num_chase_stops", "num_regions", "max_depth")}
+        | {"carrier": (c.get("chase_07c") or {}).get("carrier")} for c in newly]}
+    newly_a = [c for c in calls if (b7.get((c["unit"], c["kind"], c["index"])) or {}).get("converged_07a") is True
+               and c["converged"][0] is False]
+    S["newly_unconverged_vs_07a"] = {"n": len(newly_a), "by": dict(Counter(f"{c['rtol']}|{c['kind']}" for c in newly_a)),
+                                     "list": [{k: c[k] for k in ("unit", "kind", "index", "class", "num_chase_stops", "num_regions",
+                                                                 "max_depth")} for c in newly_a]}
+    regained = [c for c in calls if c["converged"][1] is False and c["converged"][0] is True]
+    S["newly_converged_vs_07b"] = {"n": len(regained), "by": dict(Counter(f"{c['rtol']}|{c['kind']}|{c['class']}" for c in regained))}
+    S["unconverged_by_code"] = {code: {"|".join(k): v for k, v in sorted(cc.items())} for code, cc in unconv.items()}
+    # ---------------- abserr, values past the sum, rows
+    ratios = defaultdict(list)
+    past = defaultdict(int)
+    past_list = []
+    past_a = defaultdict(int)
+    for c in calls:
+        a = "untouched" if c["class"] == "untouched" else "acted"
+        k = f"{c['rtol']}|{c['kind']}|{a}"
+        v, v7, e, e7 = F(c["value"][0]), F(c["value"][1]), F(c["abserr"][0]), F(c["abserr"][1])
+        if e7 and e:
+            ratios[k].append(float(e / e7))
+        if abs(v - v7) > e + e7:
+            past[k] += 1
+            past_list.append({k_: c[k_] for k_ in ("unit", "kind", "index", "value", "abserr", "converged", "num_chase_stops")}
+                             | {"move_over_sum": float(abs(v - v7) / (e + e7))})
+        bb = b7.get((c["unit"], c["kind"], c["index"]))
+        if bb and abs(v - F(bb["value_07a"])) > e + F(bb["abserr_07a"]):
+            past_a[k] += 1
+    S["abserr_ratio_07c_over_07b"] = {k: q(v) for k, v in sorted(ratios.items())}
+    S["abserr_ratio_bins"] = {k: dict(Counter("<0.5" if x < 0.5 else "[0.5,1)" if x < 1 else "1" if x == 1 else "(1,2]" if x <= 2 else ">2"
+                                              for x in v)) for k, v in sorted(ratios.items())}
+    S["values_past_the_sum_vs_07b"] = {"n": len(past_list), "by": dict(past),
+                                       "converged_both": sum(1 for x in past_list if x["converged"][0] and x["converged"][1]),
+                                       "move_over_sum": q([x["move_over_sum"] for x in past_list]),
+                                       "list": sorted(past_list, key=lambda x: -x["move_over_sum"])[:300]}
+    S["values_past_the_sum_vs_07a"] = {"n": sum(past_a.values()), "by": dict(past_a)}
+    mv = {"n": 0, "over_tae07b": [], "conv": []}
+    for u in U:
+        t, ta, tc = u.get("total"), u.get("total_abserr"), u.get("total_converged")
+        if not t or t[0] is None:
+            continue
+        if t[0] != t[1] or ta[0] != ta[1]:
+            mv["n"] += 1
+            if F(ta[1]):
+                mv["over_tae07b"].append(float(abs(F(t[0]) - F(t[1])) / F(ta[1])))
+        if tc[0] != tc[1]:
+            mv["conv"].append({"unit": u["unit"], "07c_07b": tc})
+    S["row_totals"] = {"rows_differing": mv["n"], "move_over_total_abserr_07b": q(mv["over_tae07b"]),
+                       "n_gt_1": sum(1 for x in mv["over_tae07b"] if x > 1),
+                       "total_converged_changes": dict(Counter(f"{x['07c_07b'][1]}->{x['07c_07b'][0]}" for x in mv["conv"])),
+                       "total_converged_change_units": mv["conv"]}
+    S["row_depth_20"] = {rt: [sum(1 for u in U if u["rtol"] == rt and u.get("row_depth") and u["row_depth"][j] == 20) for j in (0, 1)]
+                         for rt in ("1e-7", "1e-8")}
+    cost = json.loads(COST.read_text())
+    S["cost"] = {k: cost[k] for k in ("regions", "regions_total", "07c", "07b", "wall_against_projection")} | {
+        "unit_time_s": {rt: [sum(u["time_s"][j] or 0 for u in U if u["rtol"] == rt) for j in (0, 1)] for rt in ("1e-7", "1e-8")},
+        "unit_wall_s_max": [max((u["wall_s"][0] or 0) for u in U), max((u["wall_s"][1] or 0) for u in U)]}
+    out_p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    out_p.write_text(json.dumps(S, indent=1, default=str))
+    brief = {k: S[k] for k in ("statuses", "n_calls", "route_errors", "scan_07_errors", "n_calls_matched_07b_list")}
+    brief["Y1"] = S["Y1"]
+    for k in ("Y2", "Y3"):
+        brief[k] = {kk: vv for kk, vv in S[k].items() if kk not in ("levin_leaves", "violations")}
+    brief["Y4"] = {kk: vv for kk, vv in S["Y4"].items()}
+    brief["Y5"] = {kk: vv for kk, vv in S["Y5"].items() if kk != "calls"}
+    brief["Y6"] = {kk: vv for kk, vv in S["Y6"].items() if kk != "ratios"}
+    brief["Y7"] = S["Y7"]
+    brief["Y8"] = S["Y8"]
+    brief["Y9"] = S["Y9"]
+    brief["fix_totals"] = S["fix_totals"]
+    brief["where_rtol_kind"] = S["where_the_fix_acts"]["rtol|kind"]
+    brief["stopped_leaves_by_route_trust"] = S["stopped_leaves"]["by_rtol_kind_route_trust"]
+    brief["stopped_leaves_by_route_depth"] = S["stopped_leaves"]["by_route_depth"]
+    brief["stopped_leaves_checks"] = {k: S["stopped_leaves"][k] for k in ("instrument_counts_unequal", "instrument_errors",
+                                                                            "not_returned", "total", "largest_share")}

+        brief[k] = S[k]
+    brief["newly_unconverged_vs_07b_n"] = S["newly_unconverged_vs_07b"]["n"]
+    brief["newly_unconverged_vs_07a"] = {k: v for k, v in S["newly_unconverged_vs_07a"].items() if k != "list"}
+    brief["values_past_the_sum_vs_07b"] = {k: v for k, v in S["values_past_the_sum_vs_07b"].items() if k != "list"}
+    brief["values_past_the_sum_vs_07a"] = S["values_past_the_sum_vs_07a"]
+    brief["row_totals"] = {k: v for k, v in S["row_totals"].items() if k != "total_converged_change_units"}
+    print(json.dumps(brief, indent=1, default=str))
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")

+    main()

+# prompt 07c ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+_C07C_FIELDS = ("seq", "start", "end", "depth", "type", "forced_direct", "window_index", "guarded", "trusted_before",
+                "phase_limited_before", "r", "delta1", "delta2", "nu_B", "residual", "declared_resolution", "e_prime",
+                "root", "value_kind_before")  # prompt 07c: one stopped leaf's record (the instrument's)
+
+
+def _c07c_finish(st, result):
+    """prompt 07c: one call's stopped leaves, completed from the call as returned (each stopped region's e' as returned,
+    its share of the call's abserr, whether it is returned), and the checkpoint's summary. ``st`` is the instrument's
+    state for the call: ``recs`` (one dict per stopped leaf, None where one could not be recorded), ``frame`` (the call's
+    _adaptive_levin frame, or None if the pass-through was never called), ``fail`` (why the frame could not be found or
+    the closure not replaced, or None)."""
+    recs = st["recs"]
+    regs = result.get("regions") or []
+    ids = {id(r): j for j, r in enumerate(regs)}
+    abserr = float(result.get("abserr"))
+    full = []
+    n_unrec = 0
+    for e in recs:
+        if e is None:
+            n_unrec += 1
+            continue
+        reg = e.pop("_region")
+        j = ids.get(id(reg))
+        e["returned_index"] = j
+        try:
+            e["e_prime_returned"] = float(reg.budget_err)
+        except Exception:
+            e["e_prime_returned"] = None
+        e["share"] = (e["e_prime_returned"] / abserr) if (e["e_prime_returned"] is not None and abserr > 0) else None
+        full.append(e)
+    frame_locals = {}
+    if st["frame"] is not None:
+        loc = st["frame"].f_locals
+        frame_locals = {"n_stopped_roots": len(loc["chase_stopped"]),
+                        "n_counted": int(sum(loc["chase_counts"].values())),
+                        "n_rooted_leaves": len(loc["chase_root"])}
+    ns, nl, nb = (result.get("num_chase_stops"), result.get("num_chase_stopped_leaves"),
+                  result.get("num_chase_noise_bisections"))
+    out = {"frame_found": st["frame"] is not None, "fail": st["fail"], "n_stopped": len(full), "n_unrecorded": n_unrec,
+           **frame_locals}
+    out["counts_equal_result"] = (
+        ns is not None and nl is not None and nb is not None and st["fail"] is None and n_unrec == 0
+        and len(full) == int(nl)
+        and (frame_locals.get("n_stopped_roots", 0) == int(ns))
+        and (frame_locals.get("n_counted", 0) == int(nb))
+    )
+    by_route, by_depth = {}, {}
+    for e in full:
+        rk = ("direct" if e["type"] != "Levin" else "Levin") + ("" if e["trusted_before"] is None else
+                                                                 (":trusted" if e["trusted_before"] else ":untrusted"))
+        by_route[rk] = by_route.get(rk, 0) + 1
+        by_depth[str(e["depth"])] = by_depth.get(str(e["depth"]), 0) + 1
+    out["by_route"] = by_route
+    out["by_depth"] = by_depth
+    out["n_not_returned"] = sum(1 for e in full if e["returned_index"] is None)
+    out["share_sum"] = fx(sum(e["share"] for e in full if e["share"] is not None)) if full else None
+    car = max((e for e in full if e["share"] is not None), key=lambda e: e["share"], default=None)
+    out["carrier"] = None if car is None else {
+        "seq": car["seq"], "start": fx(car["start"]), "end": fx(car["end"]), "depth": car["depth"], "type": car["type"],
+        "forced_direct": car["forced_direct"], "window_index": car["window_index"], "trusted_before": car["trusted_before"],
+        "r": fx(car["r"]), "delta1": fx(car["delta1"]), "delta2": fx(car["delta2"]), "nu_B": fx(car["nu_B"]),
+        "residual": fx(car["residual"]), "declared_resolution": fx(car["declared_resolution"]),
+        "e_prime_returned": fx(car["e_prime_returned"]), "share": fx(car["share"]), "root": car["root"]}
+    return out, full
+
+
+def _c07c_arrays(full):
+    """prompt 07c: the pickle's record of one call's stopped leaves, one list per field (floats as floats)."""
+    keys = _C07C_FIELDS + ("returned_index", "e_prime_returned", "share")
+    return {k: [e.get(k) for e in full] for k in keys}

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07c section 6: write state/predictions.md, after Gate Y0 and before the census is launched.
+New in 07c, after 07b's write_predictions_07b.py (a record, not edited), whose method it keeps: each block of the prompt
+is copied **by text**, from a fixed opening string to a fixed closing string of the prompt file in the tree h, each of
+which must occur exactly once; the block is the text between them, inclusive, unchanged. After writing, every block is
+checked to be a substring of the prompt and of the written file, and its first and last lines are printed. Part 5 is
+this agent's reading of each rule; part 6 states Gate Y0 and the projection as they stood, read from their output files.
+Refuses to overwrite; the file is made read-only.
+
+Usage: python -B write_predictions_07c.py OUT_MD
+"""
+
+import json
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import H_TREE, WIDER, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+PROMPT = H_TREE / "prompts" / "levin-phase-floor" / "07c-chase-below-a-refused-leaf.md"
+BLOCKS = [  # (name, title, opening string, closing string)
+    ("s6", "Prompt §6, the predictions and the descriptive items",
+     "**Predictions, fixed before the run** (README §5 rule 3)",
+     "(**the sum**, not each separately); regions and cost against 07b's."),
+    ("s95", "Prompt §9.5, the additions to §5's and §6's predictions (Y9 among them)",
+     "### 9.5 Additions to §5's and §6's predictions",
+     "- `c-1e-8-1-2229-4` (untouched), equal to (S)'s record and to 07b's census."),
+    ("s97", "Prompt §9.7, the stops added (for reference; not predictions)",
+     "### 9.7 Stops added (into §8)",
+     "- **In Part C:** Y8 failing, before anything else is scored."),
+    ("s8", "Prompt §8, Part C's stops (for reference; not predictions)",
+     "- In Part C: S0′ failing;",
+     "before anything else is scored; a lock held."),
+]
+
+OWN = r"""
+## 5. How each rule is read (this agent's, fixed here before the census)
+
+Throughout: a census call is named by its unit `h-<rtol>-<shard>-<serial>`, its kind (group, in the unit's `calls`; or
+analytic, in `other_calls`) and its index; 07b's census call is the one of the same name in
+`levin-phase-floor-07b-census-20261117T010200` (checkpoint `396784bc…`). Floats are compared by `float.hex`; ratios are
+computed in exact `Fraction`s from the hex values and the references' decimal strings. "Unconverged" is the call's
+`converged` false. A unit not `done` in either census is listed and not compared.
+
+- **Y1.** A call is untouched iff `num_chase_stops` is 0 (§9.5), as the call's result returns it (the census records the
+  three counts on every call; a missing count is a fault, reported). Each untouched call is compared with 07b's call in
+  value, abserr, `converged`, `num_regions` and `max_depth`. A "row all of whose calls are such" is read as a unit (one
+  row at one rtol) every one of whose Levin calls, group and analytic, is untouched; it is compared with 07b's unit in
+  `result.total` and `result.total_abserr`. **Holds** if every untouched call is equal in all five at both rtols and
+  every such unit is equal in both totals. Beside it, descriptive: each untouched call's whole record diffed against 07b's
+  by category (compare_07c.py mode `calls`). **Stop** (§8): any untouched call differing in the five, before anything
+  else is scored.
+- **Y8.** The calls are every record Part A wrote of (S)'s replays, `var/levin-phase-floor-07c/measurement/out/replays/S/
+  <id>.json` (454), less `r31-3-20363-1` (R31's record, a rehearsal row and not a census call): 453 ids, 448 distinct
+  census calls (five census calls are named by two ids each; each id is compared). Each id's census call is read from
+  Part A's mappings (`out/census_refs_07c.json` `calls`; `state/census_wanted_07c.json`, `census_wanted_07c_chase.json`,
+  `census_wanted_07c_r5.json` `wanted`). (S)'s replay record is the file's `replay`, checked to equal
+  `S_summary_vplain.json`'s row. **Holds** if all 453 equal the census call in value and abserr (hex), `converged`,
+  `num_regions` and `max_depth`. **Stop** (§9.7): any failure, before anything else is scored (scripts/score_y8_07c.py).
+- **Y2, Y3, Y5** as 07b's census scored U2, U3 and U5 (07a's W2, W3 and W5; score_07b.py), Y5 on gated solves only
+  (07a's W5 ruling), the forced solves' count descriptive.
+- **Y4.** Two sets, each scored from this census against its reference by 07's rule: a call is scored if its reference's
+  stated error is at most min(abserr)/100 over this census's and 07b's census's abserr for the call (07c's form of 07b's
+  U4 rule, min(abserr_07b, abserr_07a)/100; for R5, 07a's census abserr as well, as Part A's R5 rule takes it), otherwise
+  reported and not scored; a scored call is honest iff |v − ref| ≤ abserr; a call unconverged here counts as
+  "unconverged under §9's rule".
+  - (a) **07b's U4's 190 scorings**: the same calls, on the same references, by 07b's census scripts (score_v4_07b.py:
+    V4's 25 and z00 and Z3's seven, with z12, on 07's and 06g's readings; score_refs_07b.py: 07b Part A's R1 60, R2 60,

+    `ref_key` is `ref-R4-*` (sets `chase` and `chase~o`, 60) or `ref-R5-*` (sets `r5` and `r5~o`, 60), and R2-28's census

+  - **Holds** if every scored call in (a) and (b) is honest or unconverged, except R2-28: its census call at both rtols in
+    (b), and its two scorings in (a) (07b Part A's `R2-28` and `R2-28~o`, the same integrand), which §9.5 predicts
+    converged and dishonest at 1.143 of the abserr; each is reported with its ratio against
+    `[07c-r2-28-trusted-levin-leaf-hides-a-common-error]` and does not fail Y4. Any other scored call converged and
+    dishonest fails Y4. A call named in both sets is scored once under each. Nothing is attributed (the dispatch).
+- **Y6.** 06b's 24 calls (`docs/levin-phase-floor/06b/census/out/w2_reference.json`; unit `b-1e-7-X-N` is this census's
+  `h-1e-7-X-N`, the group call of the same index) against $S_{10}$ by 06b's rule, as 07b's score_refs_07b.py scored U6:
+  scored if $|S_9 - S_{10}| \le$ abserr/100; honest iff $|v - S_{10}| \le$ abserr; **holds** if every one is honest or
+  unconverged. Nothing is attributed.
+- **Y7.** $r_g$ over 07's 2,484 pairs (07's census marks), by 07b's xcheck_07b.py with this census and 07b's census as
+  the before; **holds** if every pair has $r_g \le 1$ or a member unconverged.
+- **Y9.** Two clauses, both over this census's Levin calls at both rtols.
+  - (i) **A chase call** is a call that is unconverged, returns at least one leaf at depth 20, and every one of whose
+    depth-20 leaves lies inside the span of a leaf a rate rule refused in the same call at a smaller depth (07b's census
+    rule, score_07b.py's `r07b_unit`: the rate-rule instrument's refused evaluations, `lo ≤ start`, `end ≤ hi`, refused
+    depth below the leaf's). A call unconverged with no leaf at depth 20 (a budget miss) is not a chase call: "every
+    depth-20 leaf" is not read as true of a call with none. Clause (i) is true iff there is no chase call.
+  - (ii) **07b's 198 newly unconverged calls** are the list of 07b's census `out/scores/summary.json`
+    `newly_unconverged`. Clause (ii) is true iff, of the 198, exactly the five named in §9.5 are unconverged here, and
+    each of the five has `num_chase_stops` 0 and equals 07b's call in Y1's five outputs.
+  - **Holds** iff (i) and (ii) are both true. A call newly unconverged against 07b is not a stop and changes no verdict
+    (Y4, Y6 and Y7 count an unconverged call as passing; Y9 is about depth-20 leaves).
+- **The projection** (§9.4): within 48 hours at its highest margin; stated also times 07b's shortfall (1.197).
+- **Descriptive** (§6 and §9.5), not scored:
+  - where the fix acts (`num_chase_stops` > 0) by kind, 07b's class of the call (the rate rules' refusals), window,
+    the 720, band, $k$ and rtol; the stops, stopped leaves and counted bisections by the same;
+  - by the stop instrument (`chase_07c`, marked "prompt 07c"): each stopped leaf's route (Levin or the Clenshaw–Curtis
+    pair, `type` `direct`), its trust before the stop, depth, declaration (declared resolution and e'), $r$, and its share
+    of its call's abserr (its e' as returned over the call's abserr); the instrument's counts against the three result
+    counts on every call;
+  - `converged` changes against 07b's and against 07a's (07b's census `out/stop3/compare_census_calls.calls.jsonl`
+    carries 07a's), in each direction; **every call newly unconverged against 07b listed with the stopped leaf carrying
+    the most of its abserr** (route, depth, $r$, share), or "no stopped leaf" if the stop did not act in it;
+  - the calls still unconverged with every depth-20 leaf below a refused leaf (Y9 (i)'s list, expected empty);
+  - rows (units) with a leaf at depth 20, each rtol, against 07b's;
+  - the abserr ratio against 07b's, untouched and acted calls apart;
+  - values moved past **the sum**: $|v_{07c} - v_{07b}| > {\rm abserr}_{07c} + {\rm abserr}_{07b}$, in exact
+    arithmetic; never each separately;
+  - regions and cost against 07b's (census wall, unit walls, unit time with post sections, regions by rtol and kind).
+"""
+
+
+def y0_status():
+    def load(p):
+        p = WIDER / p
+        return json.loads(p.read_text()) if p.exists() else None
+    lines = ["## 6. Gate Y0 and the projection, as they stood when this file was written", ""]
+    a = (WIDER / "out" / "y0" / "y0a_ast.txt")
+    b = (WIDER / "out" / "y0" / "y0a_imports.txt")
+    for p in (a, b):
+        v = [l for l in p.read_text().splitlines() if l.startswith("# verdict")] if p.exists() else ["(missing)"]
+        lines.append(f"- Y0 (1): `{p.relative_to(WIDER)}`: {v[0][2:] if v else '(no verdict line)'}")
+    w = load("out/k0/compare_y0b.json")
+    lines.append(f"- Y0 (2): `out/k0/compare_y0b.json`: {w and w['n_units_equal']} of {w and w['n_units']} K0 units equal "
+                 f"07b's census at the parent; totals {w and w['totals']}; worker evidence all hold "
+                 f"{w and w['worker_evidence_all_hold']}; new keys in 07b's records {w and w['new_keys_in_07_records']}")
+    i = load("out/y0c3/compare_instr.json")
+    lines.append(f"- Y0 (3): `out/y0c3/compare_instr.json`: "
+                 + ("; ".join(f"{u['unit']} {'IDENTICAL' if u['ok'] else 'DIFFERS'} (stops {u.get('stops')})" for u in i["units"])
+                    if i else "(missing)"))
+    d = load("out/y0c4/compare_det.json")
+    lines.append(f"- Y0 (4): `out/y0c4/compare_det.json`: "
+                 + ("; ".join(f"{u['unit']} {'IDENTICAL' if u['ok'] else 'DIFFERS'}" for u in d["units"]) if d else "(missing)"))
+    pj = load("out/census_projection.json")
+    if pj:
+        lines.append(f"- The projection (`out/census_projection.json`): central {pj['projection_central_s']:.0f} s "
+                     f"({pj['projection_central_s'] / 3600:.2f} h); times 07b's shortfall {pj['shortfall_07b']:.3f}: "
+                     f"{pj['projection_central_x_07b_shortfall_s']:.0f} s; highest margin "
+                     f"{pj['projection_highest_with_overbudget_s']:.0f} s, times the shortfall "
+                     f"{pj['projection_highest_x_07b_shortfall_s']:.0f} s; within 48 h: {pj['within_48h']} "
+                     f"(times the shortfall: {pj['within_48h_x_07b_shortfall']})")
+    return "\n".join(lines)
+
+
+def main():
+    out_p = Path(sys.argv[1])
+    if out_p.exists():
+        raise SystemExit(f"{out_p} exists; not overwritten")
+    text = PROMPT.read_text()
+    hdr = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07c section 6: the census's predictions, fixed before the census",
+                 [PROMPT])
+    blocks = []
+    for name, title, a, b in BLOCKS:
+        if text.count(a) != 1 or text.count(b) != 1:
+            raise SystemExit(f"{name}: the opening or closing string does not occur exactly once")
+        i = text.index(a)
+        j = text.index(b) + len(b)
+        if j <= i:
+            raise SystemExit(f"{name}: the closing string comes before the opening one")
+        blk = text[i:j]
+        l0 = text.count("\n", 0, i) + 1
+        l1 = text.count("\n", 0, j - 1) + 1
+        blocks.append((name, title, blk, l0, l1))
+    parts = [
+        "# levin-phase-floor 07c §6 — the census's predictions, fixed before the census",
+        "",
+        "Provenance (the writer's header):",
+        "",
+        "```",
+        *[l[2:] for l in hdr],
+        "```",
+        "",
+        "Written after Gate Y0 (all its clauses) and before the census is launched. Not to be rewritten: a correction is a "
+        "new file and a deviation.",
+        "",
+        f"Source: `{PROMPT}` (file SHA-256 {sha256(PROMPT)}). Parts 1-4 are copied **by text** (each from a fixed opening "
+        "string to a fixed closing string, both unique in the file; the line numbers are given for reference only) and "
+        "checked to be substrings of the prompt. Parts 5 and 6 are this agent's.",
+        "",
+    ]
+    for k, (name, title, blk, l0, l1) in enumerate(blocks, 1):
+        parts += [f"## {k}. {title} (verbatim; prompt lines {l0}-{l1})", "", blk, ""]
+    parts.append(OWN.strip("\n"))
+    parts += ["", y0_status()]
+    doc = "\n".join(parts) + "\n"
+    out_p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    out_p.write_text(doc)
+    out_p.chmod(0o444)
+    back = out_p.read_text()
+    ok = True
+    for name, title, blk, l0, l1 in blocks:
+        in_prompt = blk in text
+        in_file = blk in back
+        ok &= in_prompt and in_file
+        lines = blk.splitlines()
+        print(f"{name}: lines {l0}-{l1}, {len(lines)} lines, {len(blk)} chars; substring of the prompt {in_prompt}, of the "
+              f"file {in_file}")
+        print(f"  first: {lines[0]}")
+        print(f"  last:  {lines[-1]}")
+    for must in ("Y1 — nothing else moves", "Y2 — no Levin leaf", "Y3 — every phase", "Y4 — honest where it is referenced",
+                 "Y5 — the gate is consistent", "Y6 — 06b's 24 are honest", "Y7 — the pairs",
+                 "Y8 — the code is the chosen variant", "**Y1 counts**", "**Y4, on the user's ruling.**",
+                 "**Y6 and Y7 are predicted to hold.**", "**Y8 — the code is (S).**", "**Y9 — the chase ends.**",
+                 "**Descriptive, not scored:**", "**Descriptive, added:**", "In Part C:** Y8 failing"):
+        f = must in back
+        ok &= f
+        print(f"  contains {must!r}: {f}")
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}; all checks {'HOLD' if ok else 'FAIL'}")
+    return 0 if ok else 1
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07c section 6: Y7 (the cross-check's r_g re-computed at 07c's census over 07's 2,484 pairs).
+Copied from 07b's census xcheck_07b.py (a record, not edited), changed only where marked "prompt 07c": "g" is 07c's

+census xcheck_07.py (a record, not edited), changed only where marked "prompt 07a": "g" is now h's census (RUN_H, its units
+h-..., read by offset as 07's g-...); "m" (the before) is 07's census (BEFORE 07, by state/index_07.json) or 06m's

+INF = float("inf")
+
+
+def Fx(x):
+    """A census float ({repr, hex}) as an exact Fraction; None stays None; a non-finite as a float."""
+    if x is None:
+        return None
+    v = float.fromhex(x["hex"] if isinstance(x, dict) else x)
+    return Fraction(v) if math.isfinite(v) else v
+
+
+def ratio(num, den):
+    """num/den as a double, num and den exact (Fraction) or non-finite; den <= 0 or non-finite -> inf or 0."""
+    if num is None or den is None:
+        return None
+    if isinstance(num, float) or isinstance(den, float) or den <= 0:
+        if isinstance(num, Fraction) and num == 0:
+            return 0.0
+        if isinstance(den, float) and math.isinf(den) and isinstance(num, Fraction):
+            return 0.0
+        return INF
+    return float(num / den)
+
+
+def le(num, den):
+    """num <= den exactly (num, den Fractions or non-finite floats)."""
+    if num is None or den is None:
+        return None
+    if isinstance(num, float) or isinstance(den, float):
+        return float(num) <= float(den)
+    return num <= den
+
+
+def absdiff(a, b):
+    if a is None or b is None:
+        return None
+    if isinstance(a, float) or isinstance(b, float):
+        return abs(float(a) - float(b))
+    return abs(a - b)
+
+
+def qtiles(xs):
+    xs = sorted(x for x in xs if x is not None)
+    if not xs:
+        return None
+    def q(p):
+        return xs[min(len(xs) - 1, int(math.floor(p * (len(xs) - 1) + 0.5)))]
+    return {"n": len(xs), "q0": xs[0], "q10": q(0.1), "q50": q(0.5), "q90": q(0.9), "q99": q(0.99), "q100": xs[-1],
+            "bins": {"<=0.01": sum(1 for x in xs if x <= 0.01), "(0.01,0.1]": sum(1 for x in xs if 0.01 < x <= 0.1),
+                     "(0.1,1]": sum(1 for x in xs if 0.1 < x <= 1), "(1,10]": sum(1 for x in xs if 1 < x <= 10),
+                     ">10": sum(1 for x in xs if x > 10)}}
+
+
+def hx(x):
+    return None if x is None else (x["hex"] if isinstance(x, dict) else x)
+
+

+    # prompt 07a: the arguments; CK07 is h's census, CK06 and INDEX06M the before's, CKPOP the population's

+    with open(CKPOP, "rb") as f:
+        for line in f:
+            if b'"locates": true' in line or b'"guards": true' in line:
+                uid = line[10:60].decode().split('"', 1)[0]
+                p = uid.split("-")
+                marked.add(f"{p[3]}-{p[4]}")
+                d_ = json.loads(line)["data"]
+                for k_, key_ in (("group", "calls"), ("analytic", "other_calls")):
+                    for c_ in d_.get(key_) or []:
+                        g_ = c_.get("guard_07") or {}
+                        if g_.get("locates") or g_.get("guards"):
+                            marked_by.setdefault(f"{p[3]}-{p[4]}", set()).add((k_, c_["index"]))
+                            n_marked_calls += 1
+
+    def load07(uid):
+        o, n = off07[uid]
+        with open(CK07, "rb") as f:
+            f.seek(o)
+            r = json.loads(f.read(n))
+        assert r["unit"] == "h-" + uid.split("-", 1)[1]  # prompt 07a
+        return r["data"]
+
+    def load06(uid):
+        e = idx06[uid] if uid in idx06 else idx06["h-" + uid.split("-", 1)[1]]  # prompt 07b: 07a's index names h-...
+        with open(CK06, "rb") as f:
+            f.seek(e["offset"])
+            line = f.read(e["length"])
+        import hashlib
+
+        if hashlib.sha256(line).hexdigest() != e["line_sha256"]:
+            raise SystemExit(f"{uid}: the before's line sha256 differs")
+        return json.loads(line)["data"]
+
+    def calls_of(d):
+        return {("group", c["index"]): c for c in d.get("calls") or []} | {("analytic", c["index"]): c for c in d.get("other_calls") or []}
+
+    pairs, unpaired, pop_calls = [], [], 0
+    units = {}
+    for row in sorted(marked):
+        U = {}
+        for rt in ("1e-7", "1e-8"):
+            uid = f"g-{rt}-{row}"
+            U[rt] = (load07(uid), load06(uid))
+        units[row] = {rt: {"total": [hx((U[rt][0].get("result") or {}).get("total")), hx((U[rt][1].get("result") or {}).get("total"))],
+                           "total_abserr": [hx((U[rt][0].get("result") or {}).get("total_abserr")),
+                                            hx((U[rt][1].get("result") or {}).get("total_abserr"))],
+                           "status": [U[rt][0]["status"], U[rt][1]["status"]]} for rt in U}
+        C = {rt: (calls_of(U[rt][0]), calls_of(U[rt][1])) for rt in U}
+        keys = set(marked_by.get(row, set()))  # prompt 07a: the population's marked calls (either rtol)
+        pop_calls = n_marked_calls
+        for k in sorted(keys):
+            g7c, g8c = C["1e-7"][0].get(k), C["1e-8"][0].get(k)
+            m7c, m8c = C["1e-7"][1].get(k), C["1e-8"][1].get(k)
+            ok = all(x is not None for x in (g7c, g8c, m7c, m8c))
+            if ok:
+                ids = {(x["notify_label"], tuple(e["hex"] for e in x["x_span"])) for x in (g7c, g8c, m7c, m8c)}
+                ok = len(ids) == 1
+            if not ok:
+                unpaired.append({"row": row, "kind": k[0], "index": k[1]})
+                continue
+            g7, g8, m7, m8 = Fx(g7c["value"]), Fx(g8c["value"]), Fx(m7c["value"]), Fx(m8c["value"])
+            eg7, eg8, em7, em8 = Fx(g7c["abserr"]), Fx(g8c["abserr"]), Fx(m7c["abserr"]), Fx(m8c["abserr"])
+            d8 = absdiff(g8, m8)
+            R = rows[row]
+            pr = {"row": row, "kind": k[0], "index": k[1], "label": g7c["notify_label"],
+                  "group": g7c.get("group") if k[0] == "group" else g7c["notify_label"],
+                  "band": R["band"], "k": R["k"], "k_key": R["k_key"],
+                  "r_g": ratio(absdiff(g7, g8), eg7), "r_m": ratio(absdiff(m7, m8), em7),
+                  "d8_g": ratio(d8, eg8), "d8_m": ratio(d8, em8),
+                  "g_self": le(absdiff(g7, g8), eg7), "m_self": le(absdiff(m7, m8), em7),
+                  "agree8": le(d8, max(eg8, em8)),
+                  "locates": [bool((g7c.get("guard_07") or {}).get("locates")), bool((g8c.get("guard_07") or {}).get("locates"))],
+                  "guards": [bool((g7c.get("guard_07") or {}).get("guards")), bool((g8c.get("guard_07") or {}).get("guards"))],
+                  "values": {"g7": hx(g7c["value"]), "g8": hx(g8c["value"]), "m7": hx(m7c["value"]), "m8": hx(m8c["value"])},
+                  "abserrs": {"g7": hx(g7c["abserr"]), "g8": hx(g8c["abserr"]), "m7": hx(m7c["abserr"]), "m8": hx(m8c["abserr"])},
+                  "depth": {"g7": g7c["max_depth"], "g8": g8c["max_depth"], "m7": m7c["max_depth"], "m8": m8c["max_depth"]},
+                  "regions": {"g7": g7c["num_regions"], "g8": g8c["num_regions"], "m7": m7c["num_regions"], "m8": m8c["num_regions"]},
+                  "converged": {"g7": g7c["converged"], "g8": g8c["converged"], "m7": m7c["converged"], "m8": m8c["converged"]}}
+            if pr["g_self"] and not pr["m_self"] and pr["agree8"]:
+                cls = "a"
+            elif pr["m_self"] and not pr["g_self"] and pr["agree8"]:
+                cls = "b"
+            elif pr["g_self"] and pr["m_self"]:
+                cls = "c"
+            elif not pr["g_self"] and not pr["m_self"]:
+                cls = "d"
+            else:
+                cls = "e"
+            pr["class"] = cls
+            pr["listed"] = {}
+            for rt, (g, m, eg, em) in (("1e-7", (g7, m7, eg7, em7)), ("1e-8", (g8, m8, eg8, em8))):
+                dd = absdiff(g, m)
+                pr["listed"][rt] = not le(dd, max(eg, em))
+                pr[f"dev_{rt}"] = {"over_eg": ratio(dd, eg), "over_em": ratio(dd, em)}
+            pr["v4"] = {rt: v4.get((rt, row, k[0], k[1])) for rt in ("1e-7", "1e-8")}
+            pairs.append(pr)
+    # part A tables
+    def tab(sel):
+        return {"pairs": len(sel), "r_g<=1": sum(1 for p in sel if p["g_self"]), "r_m<=1": sum(1 for p in sel if p["m_self"])}
+
+    A = {"population_calls": pop_calls, "pairs": len(pairs), "unpaired": unpaired, "overall": tab(pairs),
+         "by_kind": {k: tab([p for p in pairs if (p["group"] if p["kind"] == "group" else p["label"]) == k])
+                     for k in sorted({p["group"] if p["kind"] == "group" else p["label"] for p in pairs})},
+         "by_band": {b: tab([p for p in pairs if p["band"] == b]) for b in sorted({p["band"] for p in pairs})},
+         "by_k": {k: tab([p for p in pairs if p["k"] == k]) for k in sorted({p["k"] for p in pairs}, key=float)},
+         "dist": {n: qtiles([p[n] for p in pairs]) for n in ("r_g", "r_m", "d8_g", "d8_m")},
+         "r_g_gt_1": [p for p in pairs if not p["g_self"]]}
+    # part B
+    listed = [(rt, p) for p in pairs for rt in ("1e-7", "1e-8") if p["listed"][rt]]
+    B = {"n_listed_calls": len(listed),
+         "by_class": dict(Counter(p["class"] for _, p in listed)),
+         "by_class_rtol": {f"{c}|{rt}": n for (c, rt), n in sorted(Counter((p["class"], rt) for rt, p in listed).items())},
+         "by_class_band_k": {f"{c}|{b}|{k}": n for (c, b, k), n in sorted(Counter((p["class"], p["band"], p["k"]) for _, p in listed).items(), key=lambda kv: (kv[0][0], kv[0][1], float(kv[0][2])))},
+         "by_class_kind": {f"{c}|{g}": n for (c, g), n in sorted(Counter((p["class"], p["group"] if p["kind"] == "group" else p["label"]) for _, p in listed).items())},
+         "in_v4": [{"rtol": rt, "row": p["row"], "index": p["index"], "sid": p["v4"][rt], "class": p["class"]} for rt, p in listed if p["v4"][rt]],
+         "calls": [{"unit": f"g-{rt}-{p['row']}", "rtol": rt, "index": p["index"], "kind": p["kind"], "group": p["group"],
+                    "band": p["band"], "k": p["k"], "class": p["class"],
+                    "value_g": p["values"]["g7" if rt == "1e-7" else "g8"], "value_06m": p["values"]["m7" if rt == "1e-7" else "m8"],
+                    "abserr_g": p["abserrs"]["g7" if rt == "1e-7" else "g8"], "abserr_06m": p["abserrs"]["m7" if rt == "1e-7" else "m8"],
+                    "dev_over_abserr_g": p[f"dev_{rt}"]["over_eg"], "dev_over_abserr_06m": p[f"dev_{rt}"]["over_em"],
+                    "depth_g": p["depth"]["g7" if rt == "1e-7" else "g8"], "depth_06m": p["depth"]["m7" if rt == "1e-7" else "m8"],
+                    "regions_g": p["regions"]["g7" if rt == "1e-7" else "g8"], "regions_06m": p["regions"]["m7" if rt == "1e-7" else "m8"],
+                    "r_g": p["r_g"], "r_m": p["r_m"], "d8_g": p["d8_g"], "d8_m": p["d8_m"], "v4": p["v4"][rt]}
+                   for rt, p in listed]}
+    # the row totals of the rows the listed calls move
+    tot = []
+    for rt, p in listed:
+        u = units[p["row"]][rt]
+        tg, tm = Fx(u["total"][0]), Fx(u["total"][1])
+        eg, em = Fx(u["total_abserr"][0]), Fx(u["total_abserr"][1])
+        dd = absdiff(tg, tm)
+        tot.append({"unit": f"g-{rt}-{p['row']}", "index": p["index"], "class": p["class"], "total_g": u["total"][0],
+                    "total_06m": u["total"][1], "total_abserr_g": u["total_abserr"][0], "total_abserr_06m": u["total_abserr"][1],
+                    "dtotal_over_abserr_g": ratio(dd, eg), "dtotal_over_abserr_06m": ratio(dd, em),
+                    "dtotal_rel": ratio(dd, abs(tm)) if isinstance(tm, Fraction) else None})
+    B["row_totals"] = tot
+    B["row_totals_summary"] = {"rows": len({t["unit"] for t in tot}),
+                               "dtotal_over_abserr_06m": qtiles([t["dtotal_over_abserr_06m"] for t in tot]),
+                               "dtotal_over_abserr_g": qtiles([t["dtotal_over_abserr_g"] for t in tot])}
+    after = {"h": sha256(CK07), bef: sha256(CK06)}  # prompt 07a
+    doc = {"header": hdr, "rule": str(RULE), "rule_sha256": RULE_SHA256, "checkpoints_before": before,
+           "checkpoints_after": after, "checkpoints_unchanged": before == after, "A": A, "B": B, "pairs": pairs}
+    (out_dir / "xcheck.json").write_text(json.dumps(doc, indent=1, default=str))
+    for l in hdr:
+        print(l)
+    print(json.dumps({"A": {k: v for k, v in A.items() if k not in ("r_g_gt_1",)}, "B": {k: v for k, v in B.items() if k not in ("calls", "row_totals")},
+                      "unchanged": before == after}, indent=1, default=str))
+    print(f"wrote {out_dir / 'xcheck.json'} sha256 {sha256(out_dir / 'xcheck.json')}")
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())

+"""levin-phase-floor prompt 07c section 6, Gate Y0's first clause, the files loaded: new in 07c, after 07b's
+u0a_imports_07b.py (a record, not edited), whose test it keeps. The K0 run at the parent (in h, cc3368ce, with cff4237f's
+module installed) executes 07b's code if: no file of h the K0 workers loaded differs between the parent cff4237f and h
+(levin_quadrature.py is replaced, and so is not loaded from h); h's own AdaptiveLevin/levin_quadrature.py is not among the
+files loaded; the variant loaded is the parent's file (8956c7b6..., 07b's census module); and no file the K0 workers
+loaded differs between 07b's census tree 0a103b5a and h, but levin_quadrature.py (replaced) (out/y0/y0a_ast.txt: S0'
+lists CLAUDE.md alone). Read only.
+
+Usage: python -B y0a_imports_07c.py RUN_K0_DIR OUT_TXT
+"""
+import json
+import subprocess
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
+from prov import H07B_SHA, H_SHA, H_TREE, LQ_SHA256_07CPARENT, PARENT_SHA_07C, PARENT_VARIANT_07C, header, sha256  # noqa: E402
+
+
+def git_names(a, b):
+    return subprocess.run(["git", "--no-optional-locks", "-C", str(H_TREE), "diff", "--name-only", a, b, "--", ".",
+                           ":!docs", ":!prompts"], capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout.split()
+
+
+def main():
+    run_dir, out_p = Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2])
+    files = set()
+    variants = set()
+    n_workers = 0
+    for wdir in sorted(p for p in run_dir.iterdir() if p.is_dir()):
+        for line in open(wdir / "emit.jsonl"):
+            m = json.loads(line)
+            if m["kind"] == "bye":
+                n_workers += 1
+                for _name, f in m["modules_own_tree"]:
+                    files.add(str(Path(f).resolve().relative_to(H_TREE)))
+            if m["kind"] == "hello":
+                v = m["evidence"].get("variant_06k") or {}
+                variants.add((v.get("path"), v.get("sha256_now")))
+    diff_ph = git_names(PARENT_SHA_07C, H_SHA)
+    diff_b = git_names(H07B_SHA, H_SHA)
+    loaded = files | {"main.py"}
+    inter_ph = sorted(set(diff_ph) & loaded)
+    inter_b = sorted(set(diff_b) & loaded)
+    lq_loaded = "AdaptiveLevin/levin_quadrature.py" in files
+    variant_ok = variants == {(str(PARENT_VARIANT_07C.resolve()), LQ_SHA256_07CPARENT)}
+    ok = not inter_ph and not inter_b and not lq_loaded and variant_ok and n_workers > 0
+    lines = header(Path(__file__).resolve(), "levin-phase-floor 07c: Gate Y0 (1), the files of h the K0 workers loaded",
+                   [PARENT_VARIANT_07C])
+    lines.append(f"# workers with a bye: {n_workers}; distinct files of h loaded: {len(files)}")
+    lines.append(f"# variants installed (path, sha256 at hello): {sorted(variants)}; the parent's: {variant_ok}")
+    lines.append(f"# h's own AdaptiveLevin/levin_quadrature.py among the files loaded: {lq_loaded}")
+    lines.append(f"# git diff --name-only {PARENT_SHA_07C[:8]} {H_SHA[:8]} -- . ':!docs' ':!prompts': {diff_ph}")
+    lines.append(f"# files both changed (parent to h) and loaded: {inter_ph} (none expected: levin_quadrature.py is replaced)")
+    lines.append(f"# git diff --name-only {H07B_SHA[:8]} {H_SHA[:8]} -- . ':!docs' ':!prompts' (07b's census tree to h): "
+                 f"{diff_b}; loaded among them: {inter_b} (none expected)")
+    lines.append(f"# verdict: {'HOLDS (the code the K0 workers executed is the parent code, and the parent is 07bs)' if ok else 'FAILS'}")
+    lines += sorted(files)
+    out_p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    out_p.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
+    print("\n".join(l for l in lines if l.startswith("#")))
+    print(f"wrote {out_p} sha256 {sha256(out_p)}")
+    return 0 if ok else 1
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(main())
